On Fila-King Conjecture in Dimension Four
Juncheng Wei, Qidi Zhang, Yifu Zhou

Abstract
. We consider the following Cauchy problem for the four-dimensional energy critical heat equation { u t = Δ ​ u + u 3 in ​ ℝ 4 × ( 0 , ∞ ) , u ​ ( x , 0 ) = u 0 ​ ( x ) in ​ ℝ 4 . \begin{cases}u_{t}=\Delta u+u^{3}~&\mbox{ in }~{\mathbb{R}}^{4}\times(0,\infty),\\ u(x,0)=u_{0}(x)~&\mbox{ in }~{\mathbb{R}}^{4}.\end{cases} We construct a positive infinite time blow-up solution u ⁡ ( x , t ) u(x,t) with the blow-up rate ‖ u ⁡ ( ⋅ , t ) ‖ L ∞ ​ ( ℝ 4 ) ∼ ln ⁡ t \|u(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}}^{4})}\sim\ln t as t → ∞ t\to\infty and show the stability of the infinite time blow-up. This gives a rigorous proof of a conjecture by Fila and King [ 15 , Conjecture 1.1] .



1.
Introduction and main results

Since the seminal work of Fujita
[
19
]
, the following nolinear heat equation

(1.1)

\begin{cases}u_{t}=\Delta u+|u|^{p-1}u~&\mbox{ in }~{\mathbb{R}}^{n}\times(0,\infty),\\
u(x,0)=u_{0}(x)~&\mbox{ in }~{\mathbb{R}}^{n},\end{cases}

with
p>1
,
n\geq 3
has been extensively studied. The energy functional for (
1.1
) is

E(u)=\frac{1}{2}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{n}}|\nabla u|^{2}-\frac{1}{p+1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{n}}|u|^{p+1},

and for classical solution
u(x,t)
with sufficient spatial decay, one has

\frac{d}{dt}E(u(\cdot,t))=-\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{n}}|u_{t}|^{2}.

Many literatures have been devoted to studying problem (
1.1
) about the singularity formation, especially the blow-up rates, profiles and sets.
We refer the readers to the book of Quittner and Souplet
[
38
]
for comprehensive survey and also recent developments.

For the finite time blow-up, it is said to be of

•

type I
if

{\lim\sup}_{t\to T}(T-t)^{\frac{1}{p-1}}\|u(\cdot,t)\|_{\infty}<\infty;

•

type II
if

{\lim\sup}_{t\to T}(T-t)^{\frac{1}{p-1}}\|u(\cdot,t)\|_{\infty}=\infty.

Type I blow-up is more “generic”, while type II blow-up is much more difficult to detect. In particular, two different types of blow-up phenomena in problem (
1.1
) depend sensitively on the value of the exponent
p
. In this setting, the critical Sobolev exponent

p_{s}=\begin{cases}\frac{n+2}{n-2}~&\mbox{ for }~n\geq 3\\
\infty~&\mbox{ for }~n=1,2\\
\end{cases}

is special in various ways. Giga, Matsui and Sasayama
[
22
,
23
]
proved that for
p<p_{s}
, only type I blow-up can occur in the case that
\Omega
is
\mathbb{R}^{n}
or a convex domain. For the energy critical case
p=p_{s}
,
in the positive radial and monotonically decreasing class, Filippas, Herrero and Velázquez
[
18
]
excluded the possibility of type II blow-up for
n\geq 3
, and Matano and Merle
[
28
, Theorem 1.7]
removed the monotone assumption and obtained the same result.
Wang and Wei
[
43
]

proved the same result to the non-radial positive class in higher dimensions
n\geq 7
. For
p<p_{s}
, finite time type I blow-up solution was found and its stability was studied in
[
32
]
. For the critical case
p=p_{s}
in
\mathbb{R}^{n}
with
n\geq 7
, classification results were proved near the ground state of the energy critical heat equation in
[
4
]
. On the other hand, sign-changing type II blow-up solutions to the energy critical heat equation in dimensions
n=3,4,5,6
were first conjectured to exist by
[
18
]
and have been rigorously constructed recently in
[
39
,
9
,
14
,
24
,
25
,
12
,
27
]
. In the supercritical case, classification of type I and type II solutions in radially symmetric class have been studied in
[
29
,
30
,
31
]
and the references therein, and the construction of Type II blow-up was first established in the radial case by Herrero and Velázquez
[
26
]
and in the non-radial case (under some restrictions of the exponent
p
) by Collot
[
3
]
.

Concerning infinite time blow-up for
p=p_{s}
, Galaktionov and King
[
20
]
investigated positive, radially symmetric, global unbounded solutions for problem (
1.1
) in the case of unit ball with Dirichlet boundary condition in dimensions
n\geq 3
. See also
[
42
, Theorem 1.4]
for the case that the domain is symmetric and convex. In the non-radial setting, positive infinite time blow-up solution for problem (
1.1
) with Dirichlet boundary condition and
n\geq 5
was constructed by Cortazar, del Pino and Musso in
[
5
]
. The solution constructed in
[
5
]
takes the profile of sharply scaled Aubin-Talenti bubbles

U_{\mu,\xi}(x)=\mu^{-\frac{n-2}{2}}U\left(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu}\right)=(n(n-2))^{\frac{n-2}{4}}\left(\frac{\mu}{\mu^{2}+|x-\xi|^{2}}\right)^{\frac{n-2}{2}},

which solve the Yamabe problem

\Delta U+U^{\frac{n+2}{n-2}}=0~\mbox{ in }~{\mathbb{R}}^{n}.

Moreover, the blow-up location for the solution is determined by the Green’s function of
-\Delta
in
\Omega
, while for elliptic problems, the role of the Green’s function in bubbling phenomena has been known for a long time since the works
[
1
]
and
[
2
]
. In
[
13
]
, non-radial and sign-changing solution which blows up at infinite time has been constructed. Bubble towers at infinite time and backward time infinity have been constructed in
[
11
]
and
[
41
]
, respectively.

In a very interesting paper
[
15
]
, Fila and King studied problem (
1.1
) in the whole space
{{\mathbb{R}}}^{n}
with the critical exponent
p=p_{s}
and gave insight on the infinite time blow-up in the case of a
radially symmetric, positive initial condition with an exact power decay rate. By formal matched
asymptotic analysis, they demonstrated that the blow-up rate is determined by the power decay in a precise
manner. Intriguingly enough, their analysis leads them to conjecture that infinite time blow-up should
only happen in low dimensions 3 and 4, see Conjecture 1.1 in
[
15
]
. Recently, this has been confirmed and rigorously proved by del Pino, Musso and the first author in
[
10
]
for
n=3
, where the leading part of the scaling parameter is achieved by asymptotic analysis. For the case
n=4
, Fila and King conjectured that infinite time blow-up only exists when
\ell>2
for radial solutions, where

\lim_{|x|\to\infty}|x|^{\ell}u_{0}(|x|)=A

for some
A>0
.

In other contexts, for instance, Liouville-type theorems for Fujita equation, in parallel with the seminal work of Gidas and Spruck
[
21
]
in the elliptic setting, and long-time behaviors for the solutions to Fujita equation with supercritical exponent have been studied in
[
35
,
16
,
34
,
17
,
36
,
37
,
33
]
and the references therein.

In this paper, we are concerned with the following Cauchy problem for the Fujita equation with critical exponent in dimension
n=4

(1.2)

\begin{cases}u_{t}=\Delta u+u^{3}~&\mbox{ in }~{\mathbb{R}}^{4}\times(t_{0},\infty),\\
u(x,t_{0})=u_{0}(x)~&\mbox{ in }~{\mathbb{R}}^{4}.\end{cases}

The aim of this paper is to construct infinite time blow-up solution, confirming the conjecture by
[
15
, Conjecture 1.1]
, and further investigate the stability of the infinite time blow-up.
Throughout this paper,
\eta
is a smooth cut-off function which satisfies that
\eta(s)=1
for
s\leq 1
and
\eta(s)=0
for
s\geq\frac{3}{2}
. Our main results are stated as follows.

Theorem 1.1
.

For
t_{0}
sufficiently large, there exists initial value
u_{0}>0
with exponential decay such that the positive solution
u(x,t)
to (
1.2
) blows up at infinite time. More precisely, the solution takes the form of the sharply scaled bubble

u(x,t)=\eta\left(\frac{x-\xi}{\sqrt{t}}\right)\mu^{-1}(t)w\left(\frac{x-\xi(t)}{\mu(t)}\right)+O\big((\ln t)^{-1}\min\{t^{-1},|x|^{-2}\}\big)

where
w(y)=2^{\frac{3}{2}}\frac{1}{1+|y|^{2}}
. The blow-up rate and location are given by

\mu(t)=\frac{1}{\ln t}\Big(1+O\big(\frac{\ln\ln t}{\ln t}\big)\Big),\quad\xi(t)=O(t^{-1}).

More precisely, the positive initial value of the solution constructed above is

\displaystyle u(x,t_{0})=

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}(t_{0})w\big(\frac{x-\xi(t_{0})}{\mu(t_{0})}\big)\eta\big(\frac{x-\xi(t_{0})}{\sqrt{t_{0}}}\big)+2^{\frac{3}{2}}\mu(t_{0})|x-\xi(t_{0})|^{-2}\Big(e^{-\frac{|x-\xi(t_{0})|^{2}}{4t_{0}}}-\eta\big(\frac{x-\xi(t_{0})}{\sqrt{t_{0}}}\big)\Big)

\displaystyle+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}(t_{0})\Phi_{0}\big(\frac{x-\xi(t_{0})}{\bar{\mu}_{0}(t_{0})},t_{0}\big)\eta\big(\frac{4(x-\xi(t_{0}))}{\sqrt{t_{0}}}\big)+\eta\big(\frac{x-\xi(t_{0})}{\mu_{0}(t_{0})R(t_{0})}\big)e_{0}\mu^{-1}(t_{0})Z_{0}\big(\frac{x-\xi(t_{0})}{\mu(t_{0})}\big),

where
\mu_{0}
,
\bar{\mu}_{0}
are the leading order of
\mu
, and
\bar{\mu}_{0}\sim\mu_{0}=(\ln t)^{-1}
;
\Phi_{0}
is a global correction function given in Section
2.3
;
e_{0}
is a constant and
Z_{0}
is the eigenfunction with respect to the first eigenvalue for the linearized operator, which has exponential decay, see (
7.3
).

We further investigate the stability of the blow-up solution constructed in Theorem
1.1
and obtain the stability in the following sense.

Theorem 1.2
.

For any
g_{0}
, not necessarily radially symmetric, satisfying
|g_{0}(x)|\leq C_{g}t_{0}^{-\frac{\min{\{\ell,4}\}}{2}}\langle x\rangle^{-\ell}
,
\ell>3
, and for
t_{0}
sufficiently large, there exists a solution
u[g_{0}](x,t)
to (
1.2
) blowing up at infinite time
with the rate

\mu[g_{0}](t)=\frac{1}{\ln t}\Big(1+O\big(\frac{\ln\ln t}{\ln t}\big)\Big),\quad\xi[g_{0}](t)=O(t^{-1}).

The initial value is given by

\displaystyle u(x,t_{0})=

\displaystyle\big(\mu[g_{0}](t_{0})\big)^{-1}w\big(\frac{x-\xi[g_{0}](t_{0})}{\mu[g_{0}](t_{0})}\big)\eta\big(\frac{x-\xi[g_{0}](t_{0})}{\sqrt{t_{0}}}\big)

\displaystyle+2^{\frac{3}{2}}\mu[g_{0}](t_{0})\big|x-\xi[g_{0}](t_{0})\big|^{-2}\Big(e^{-\frac{|x-\xi[g_{0}](t_{0})|^{2}}{4t_{0}}}-\eta\big(\frac{x-\xi[g_{0}](t_{0})}{\sqrt{t_{0}}}\big)\Big)

\displaystyle+\big(\bar{\mu}_{0}(t_{0})\big)^{-1}\Phi_{0}\big(\frac{x-\xi[g_{0}](t_{0})}{\bar{\mu}_{0}(t_{0})},t_{0}\big)\eta\big(\frac{4(x-\xi[g_{0}](t_{0}))}{\sqrt{t_{0}}}\big)

\displaystyle+\eta\big(\frac{x-\xi[g_{0}](t_{0})}{\mu_{0}(t_{0})R(t_{0})}\big)e_{0}[g_{0}]\big(\mu[g_{0}](t_{0})\big)^{-1}Z_{0}\big(\frac{x-\xi[g_{0}](t_{0})}{\mu[g_{0}](t_{0})}\big)+g_{0}

where
\mu[g_{0}]\rightarrow\mu
,
\xi[g_{0}]\rightarrow\xi
,
e_{0}[g_{0}]\rightarrow e_{0}
in some topology as
C_{g}\rightarrow 0
. In the radial setting, the same conclusion holds for
\ell>2
with
\xi[g_{0}]\equiv 0
and
\bar{\mu}_{0}[g_{0}]\rightarrow\bar{\mu}_{0}
as
C_{g}\rightarrow 0
additionally.

Remark 1.2.1
.

•

Indeed, the initial value of the infinite time blow-up solution in Theorem
1.1
has exponential decay at space infinity. By Theorem
1.2
, we can add suitable perturbation for the initial value to achieve that

\lim\limits_{|x|\rightarrow\infty}|x|^{\ell}u(x,t_{0})=A

for any
|A|
small enough, recovering the assumption on the initial value in the conjecture by Fila and King
[
15
]
.

•

It is very possible to generalize the stability result for all
\ell>2
in the non-radial setting, see Remark
6.0.1
.

•

We do not know if the solution we construct is threshold solution or not.

Our construction is based on the
inner–outer gluing method
developed recently in
[
5
,
8
]
for parabolic problems, and the gluing method has been a powerful tool to investigate the singularity formation for various nonlinear PDEs such as parabolic equations and systems, fluid equations, geometric flows and others. See
[
10
,
7
,
40
]
and the references therein. The parabolic gluing method is much more different from the asymptotic analysis given in
[
15
]
. Some essential new features and difficulties in this paper are listed below.

One key feature and difficulty is the non-local dynamics for the scaling parameter
\mu(t)
. It turns out that the dynamics for
\mu(t)
is governed by an integro-differential operator, which is a natural consequence of the fact that the linear generator of dilations of the Aubin-Talenti bubble is of slow decay in lower dimensions. This non-local phenomenon has also been observed in
[
8
,
10
,
14
,
6
]
for lower dimensional problems. In our case here, neither the usual Laplace transform nor Riemann-Louville type method is applicable since the integro-differential equation is not in the class of Abel-type integral equations. The non-local operator here is the threshold/endpoint case in certain sense, and one needs to carry out much more delicate analysis to investigate its solvability.

Our strategy is to decompose the non-local equation for
\mu(t)
into two parts: the dominating term and the remainder term. The dominating term will be solved by contraction mapping theorem, while the remainder term will leave a much smaller error. To be more precise, the desired blow-up rate is determined at leading order. However, due to the way that we handle the non-local operator, the time decay is not fast enough for the remainder in the gluing procedure, and we will iterate this process finitely many times to make the remainder term have faster time decay than the one provided by the outer problem. This smaller remainder will be handled when solving the next order of
\mu(t)
.

After getting the leading order of
\mu(t)
, we need to solve the corresponding linearized elliptic equation to improve the time decay of the error term, which is essential for finding suitable weighted topologies ensuring the implementation of the gluing procedure. When solving the next order
\mu_{1}(t)
, we still need to decompose the non-local equation into two parts. The main difference is that the involved outer problem in the equation of
\mu_{1}(t)
only has Hölder continuity in
t
variable. The derivative of
\mu_{1}(t)
will inherit Hölder continuity from the outer problem, which will be used to control the remainder term.

On the other hand, the rather slow logarithmic blow-up rate produces following difficulties.
There are several slow decaying linear terms which involves the inner part cannot be controlled as the right hand side of the inner or outer problem. Instead, we regard these slow decaying terms as part of the linearization of the inner problem and develop a new linear theory. See Remark
3.0.1
. The dealing of these terms is in a similar spirit as in
[
6
]
, where the logarithmic blow-up speed also appears.

Thanks to the generality for the gluing method, we are able to study the stability for the solution constructed in Theorem
1.1
with both radial and non-radial perturbations, and non-radial infinite time blow-up solutions are easily found by suitable perturbation for the initial value.

Before carrying out the construction, we list several commonly used notations throughout the paper as follows.

Notations:

•

We write
a\lesssim b
(
a\gtrsim b
) if there exists a constant
C>0
such that
a\leq Cb
(
a\geq Cb
) where
C
is independent of
t
,
t_{0}
. Set
a\sim b
if
b\lesssim a\lesssim b
.

•

In general, the letter
C(a,b,\dots)

stands for a positive constant depending on parameters
a,b\dots
that
might change its actual value at each occurrence.

•

The symbol
f[g_{1},g_{2},\dots]
means that the function
f
depends on some functions
g_{1}
,
g_{2}
, ….

•

f\approx g
means that
|f-g|\rightarrow 0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
.

•

The symbol
O(f(x))
is used to denote a real-valued function that satisfies
|O(f(x))|\lesssim|f(x)|
in a domain of
x
that is either specified explicitly or
follows from the context.

•

For any fixed real number
x
, the symbol
x-
denotes a number which is less than
x
and can be chosen close to
x
arbitrarily.

•

Denote
\langle y\rangle=\sqrt{1+|y|^{2}}
for any
y\in{{\mathbb{R}}}^{n}
.

•

Denote
\mathbf{1}_{\{x\in\Omega\}}
as the characteristic function with
\mathbf{1}_{\{x\in\Omega\}}=1
if
x\in\Omega
and
\mathbf{1}_{\{x\in\Omega\}}=0
if
x\not\in\Omega
.

2.
Approximate solution and improvement

2.1.
First approximate solution

We consider the energy critical heat equation in dimension
4

(2.1)

\begin{cases}u_{t}=\Delta u+u^{3}~&\mbox{ in }~{\mathbb{R}}^{4}\times(0,\infty),\\
u(x,0)=u_{0}(x)~&\mbox{ in }~{\mathbb{R}}^{4}.\end{cases}

Since changing the initial time will not change the structure of the nonlinear heat equation, we assume the initial time is
t=t_{0}
and
t_{0}
is sufficiently large.

We use the steady state solution

w(y)=2^{\frac{3}{2}}\frac{1}{1+|y|^{2}}

as the building block of construction. It is known that all the bounded kernels of the corresponding linearized operator
\Delta+3w^{2}
are given by

Z_{i}(y)={\partial}_{y_{i}}w=-2^{\frac{5}{2}}\frac{y_{i}}{(1+|y|^{2})^{2}},\mbox{ \ for \ }i=1,\dots,4,\quad Z_{5}(y)=w+y\cdot\nabla w=2^{\frac{3}{2}}\frac{1-|y|^{2}}{(1+|y|^{2})^{2}}.

We take the leading profile of the infinite time blow-up solution as

u_{1}(x,t)=\mu^{-1}(t)w\left(\frac{x-\xi(t)}{\mu(t)}\right)\eta\left(\frac{x-\xi(t)}{\sqrt{t}}\right)

where
\mu(t)
,
\xi(t)\in C^{1}(t_{0},\infty)
. Throughout this paper, we make the following ansatz

(2.2)

\frac{1}{C_{\mu}\ln t}\leq|\mu|+t\ln t|\mu_{t}|\leq\frac{C_{\mu}}{\ln t},

\xi(t)\rightarrow 0\mbox{ \ as \ }t\rightarrow\infty

where
C_{\mu}\geq 1
is a large constant. Later we shall rigorously justify the above ansatz about the asymptotics for the scaling and translation parameters.

Denote the error function as

S[g]:=-{\partial}_{t}g+\Delta g+g^{3}.

Then the error produced by the first approximate solution
u_{1}
is given by

\displaystyle S[u_{1}]=

\displaystyle\mu^{-2}\mu_{t}Z_{5}\left(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu}\right)\eta\left(\frac{x-\xi}{\sqrt{t}}\right)+E[\mu]+\mu^{-2}\xi_{t}\cdot\nabla w\left(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu}\right)\eta\left(\frac{x-\xi}{\sqrt{t}}\right)

\displaystyle+\mu^{-1}t^{-\frac{1}{2}}\xi_{t}\cdot\nabla\eta\left(\frac{x-\xi}{\sqrt{t}}\right)w\left(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu}\right)

where

\displaystyle E[\mu]:=

\displaystyle 2^{-1}\mu^{-1}t^{-1}w\left(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu}\right)\nabla\eta\left(\frac{x-\xi}{\sqrt{t}}\right)\cdot\frac{x-\xi}{\sqrt{t}}+2\mu^{-2}t^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla w\left(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu}\right)\cdot\nabla\eta\left(\frac{x-\xi}{\sqrt{t}}\right)

\displaystyle+\mu^{-1}t^{-1}w\left(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu}\right)\Delta\eta\left(\frac{x-\xi}{\sqrt{t}}\right)+\mu^{-3}w^{3}\left(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu}\right)\left[\eta^{3}\left(\frac{x-\xi}{\sqrt{t}}\right)-\eta\left(\frac{x-\xi}{\sqrt{t}}\right)\right].

In next section, we shall add two global corrections to improve the slow decaying error.

2.2.
Transferring slow decaying terms by heat equations

For some admissible function
f(x,t)
, denote

(2.3)

\mathcal{T}^{out}_{n}[f](x,t):=\int_{t_{0}}^{t}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{n}}(4\pi(t-s))^{-\frac{n}{2}}e^{-\frac{|x-z|^{2}}{4(t-s)}}f(z,s)dzds.

In the rest of the paper, we will use Lemma
A.1
and Lemma
A.2
in the appendix to estimate

\mathcal{T}^{out}_{n}
frequently and sometimes will not state repeatedly.

Set
y=\frac{x-\xi}{\mu}
. A term is said to be of slow decay if its spatial decay is equal to or slower than
\langle y\rangle^{-2}
. Otherwise, it is of fast decay. Fast decay is necessary for the gluing procedure.
For this reason we will transfer the slow decaying terms in
S[u_{1}]
by heat equations. We now introduce the correction function
\varphi
to improve the error. For

S[u_{1}+\varphi]=-{\partial}_{t}\varphi+\Delta\varphi+S[u_{1}]+(u_{1}+\varphi)^{3}-u_{1}^{3},

we set
\bar{x}=x-\xi
and choose
\varphi(\bar{x},t)=\varphi_{1}(\bar{x},t)+\varphi_{2}(\bar{x},t)
such that

{\partial}_{t}\varphi_{1}=\Delta_{\bar{x}}\varphi_{1}+E[\mu],\quad{\partial}_{t}\varphi_{2}=\Delta_{\bar{x}}\varphi_{2}+\mu^{-2}\mu_{t}Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu})\eta(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}}).

The properties of
\varphi_{1}
and
\varphi_{2}
are given in the following two lemmas.

Lemma 2.1
.

Assume that
\mu
satisfies (
2.2
) and
\mu_{1}
satisfies
|\mu_{1}|\leq\frac{\mu}{2}
.
Consider

(2.4)

{\partial}_{t}\varphi_{1}=\Delta_{\bar{x}}\varphi_{1}+E[\mu].

There exists a solution
\varphi_{1}=\varphi_{1}[\mu]
satisfying the following pointwise estimates

|\varphi_{1}[\mu]|\lesssim(t\ln t)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+t^{2}(\ln t)^{-1}|\bar{x}|^{-6}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}},

|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\varphi_{1}|\lesssim t^{-\frac{3}{2}}(\ln t)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+t^{\frac{3}{2}}(\ln t)^{-1}|\bar{x}|^{-6}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}.

More precisely,

\displaystyle\varphi_{1}[\mu]=\

\displaystyle\left[-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mu t^{-1}+O(\mu t^{-2}|\bar{x}|^{2})+O\Big(t^{-2}\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t}(s^{-1}\mu^{3}(s)+s|\mu_{t}(s)|)ds\Big)\right]\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}

\displaystyle+O\left(\mu|\bar{x}|^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}+|\bar{x}|^{-6}\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t}s^{2}|\mu_{t}(s)|ds+t^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t/2}(s^{-1}\mu^{3}(s)+s|\mu_{t}(s)|)ds\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}},

\displaystyle\varphi_{1}[\mu+\mu_{1}]-\varphi_{1}[\mu]=\bigg[-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mu_{1}t^{-1}+O(|\mu_{1}|t^{-2}|\bar{x}|^{2})

\displaystyle+O\Big(t^{-2}\mu^{2}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|+t^{-2}\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t/2}\big(s^{-1}|\mu_{1}(s)|\mu^{2}(s)+s|\mu_{1t}(s)|\big)ds\Big)\bigg]\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}

\displaystyle+O\bigg(\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1}(t_{1})||\bar{x}|^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}+|\bar{x}|^{-6}\Big(t^{3}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|+\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t/2}s^{2}|\mu_{1t}(s)|ds\Big)

\displaystyle+t^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t/2}\Big(s^{-1}|\mu_{1}(s)|\mu^{2}(s)+s|\mu_{1t}(s)|\Big)ds\bigg)\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}.

Proof.

The support of
E
is in
\{t^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}
. In this region, by (
2.2
),
\mu^{-1}|\bar{x}|\gg 1
, which implies

w(\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\mu})=2^{\frac{3}{2}}\mu^{2}|\bar{x}|^{-2}+O(\mu^{4}|\bar{x}|^{-4}),\quad\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\mu}w^{\prime}(\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\mu})=-2^{\frac{5}{2}}\mu^{2}|\bar{x}|^{-2}+O(\mu^{4}|\bar{x}|^{-4}).

Then the leading term of
E
denoted by
\tilde{E}
is given by

\tilde{E}=2^{\frac{3}{2}}\mu t^{-2}\left(2^{-1}\zeta^{-1}\eta^{\prime}(\zeta)-\zeta^{-3}\eta^{\prime}(\zeta)+\zeta^{-2}\eta^{\prime\prime}(\zeta)\right),\quad\zeta=\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\sqrt{t}}.

Take
\tilde{\varphi}_{1}
as the approximate solution to (
2.4
). Set
\tilde{\varphi}_{1}=\mu\hat{\varphi}_{1}
,
\tilde{E}=\mu\hat{E}
and
\hat{\varphi}_{1}
satisfies

{\partial}_{t}\hat{\varphi}_{1}=\Delta_{\bar{x}}\hat{\varphi}_{1}+\hat{E}.

We take

\hat{\varphi}_{1}=t^{-1}A\left(\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\sqrt{t}}\right)
in the self-similar form. Then

(2.5)

A^{\prime\prime}+\left(\frac{3}{\zeta}+\frac{\zeta}{2}\right)A^{\prime}+A+h(\zeta)=0,

where

h(\zeta)=2^{\frac{3}{2}}\zeta^{-2}\left(\eta^{\prime\prime}(\zeta)-\frac{1}{\zeta}\eta^{\prime}(\zeta)+\frac{\zeta}{2}\eta^{\prime}(\zeta)\right).

Observe that
\zeta^{-2}
,
\zeta^{-2}(1-e^{-\frac{\zeta^{2}}{4}})
are linearly independent kernels to the homogeneous part of (
2.5
). And (
2.5
) has a particular solution

A_{p}(\zeta)=-\zeta^{-2}\int_{0}^{\zeta}ae^{-\frac{a^{2}}{4}}\int_{0}^{a}h(b)be^{\frac{b^{2}}{4}}dbda=-\zeta^{-2}\int_{0}^{\zeta}2^{\frac{3}{2}}\eta^{\prime}(a)da=2^{\frac{3}{2}}\zeta^{-2}(1-\eta(\zeta)),

where we have used
h(b)be^{\frac{b^{2}}{4}}=2^{\frac{3}{2}}(b^{-1}e^{\frac{b^{2}}{4}}\eta^{\prime}(b))^{\prime}
.

In order to find a solution with fast spatial decay, we take

A(\zeta)=A_{p}(\zeta)-2^{\frac{3}{2}}\zeta^{-2}(1-e^{-\frac{\zeta^{2}}{4}})=2^{\frac{3}{2}}\zeta^{-2}(e^{-\frac{\zeta^{2}}{4}}-\eta(\zeta))

which implies that

(2.6)

\hat{\varphi}_{1}(\bar{x},t)=2^{\frac{3}{2}}|\bar{x}|^{-2}\Big(e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{4t}}-\eta(\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\sqrt{t}})\Big),\quad\tilde{\varphi}_{1}[\mu]=2^{\frac{3}{2}}\mu|\bar{x}|^{-2}\Big(e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{4t}}-\eta(\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\sqrt{t}})\Big).

It is straightforward to see
\tilde{\varphi}_{1}[\mu](0,t)=-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mu t^{-1}
,

\tilde{\varphi}_{1}[\mu]=\left(-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mu t^{-1}+O(\mu t^{-2}|\bar{x}|^{2})\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+O\left(\mu|\bar{x}|^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{4t}}\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}},

\tilde{\varphi}_{1}[\mu+\mu_{1}]-\tilde{\varphi}_{1}[\mu]=\left(-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mu_{1}t^{-1}+O(|\mu_{1}|t^{-2}|\bar{x}|^{2})\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+O\left(|\mu_{1}||\bar{x}|^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{4t}}\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}},

and

\displaystyle{\partial}_{|\bar{x}|}\hat{\varphi}_{1}=

\displaystyle-2^{\frac{5}{2}}|\bar{x}|^{-3}\Big(e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{4t}}-\eta(\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\sqrt{t}})\Big)-2^{\frac{3}{2}}|\bar{x}|^{-2}\Big(e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{4t}}\frac{|\bar{x}|}{2t}+\eta^{\prime}(\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\sqrt{t}})t^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\Big(|\bar{x}|t^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+|\bar{x}|^{-1}t^{-1}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{4t}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}\Big),

(2.7)

|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\tilde{\varphi}_{1}[\mu]|=|\mu\nabla_{\bar{x}}\hat{\varphi}_{1}|\lesssim|\bar{x}||\mu|t^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+|\bar{x}|^{-1}|\mu|t^{-1}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{4t}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}.

Take
\varphi_{1}=\tilde{\varphi}_{1}+\tilde{\varphi}_{1b}
. Then
\tilde{\varphi}_{1b}
satisfies

{\partial}_{t}\tilde{\varphi}_{1b}=\Delta_{\bar{x}}\tilde{\varphi}_{1b}-\mu_{t}\hat{\varphi}_{1}+E-\tilde{E},

where
\tilde{\varphi}_{1b}
is given by

(2.8)

\tilde{\varphi}_{1b}[\mu](\bar{x},t)={\mathcal{T}}_{4}^{out}[-\mu_{t}\hat{\varphi}_{1}+E-\tilde{E}](\bar{x},t)

with

(2.9)

\displaystyle E-\tilde{E}=\frac{1}{2}\mu^{-1}t^{-1}\Big(w(\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\mu})-2^{\frac{3}{2}}\mu^{2}|\bar{x}|^{-2}\Big)\eta^{\prime}(\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\sqrt{t}})\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\sqrt{t}}+2\mu^{-1}t^{-1}\Big(\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\mu}w^{\prime}(\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\mu})+2^{\frac{5}{2}}\mu^{2}|\bar{x}|^{-2}\Big)\frac{1}{|\bar{x}|t^{-\frac{1}{2}}}\eta^{\prime}(\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\sqrt{t}})

\displaystyle+\mu^{-1}t^{-1}\Big(w(\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\mu})-2^{\frac{3}{2}}\mu^{2}|\bar{x}|^{-2}\Big)\Delta\eta(\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\sqrt{t}})+\mu^{-3}w^{3}(\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\mu})\Big(\eta^{3}(\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\sqrt{t}})-\eta(\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\sqrt{t}})\Big)=O(\mu^{3}t^{-3}\mathbf{1}_{\{\sqrt{t}\leq|\bar{x}|\leq 2\sqrt{t}\}}).

Similarly, we evaluate

(E-\tilde{E})[\mu+\mu_{1}]-(E-\tilde{E})[\mu]=O\big(|\mu_{1}|\mu^{2}t^{-3}\mathbf{1}_{\{\sqrt{t}\leq|\bar{x}|\leq 2\sqrt{t}\}}\big).

By Lemma
A.1
, one has

{\mathcal{T}}_{4}^{out}\left[\mu^{3}t^{-3}\mathbf{1}_{\{\sqrt{t}\leq|\bar{x}|\leq 2\sqrt{t}\}}\right]\lesssim t^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t/2}\mu^{3}(s)s^{-1}ds+\begin{cases}\mu^{3}t^{-2}&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\mu^{3}t^{-1}|\bar{x}|^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\end{cases},

\displaystyle{\mathcal{T}}_{4}^{out}\left[|\mu_{1}|\mu^{2}t^{-3}\mathbf{1}_{\{\sqrt{t}\leq|\bar{x}|\leq 2\sqrt{t}\}}\right]\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t/2}|\mu_{1}(s)|\mu^{2}(s)s^{-1}ds

\displaystyle+\begin{cases}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|\mu^{2}t^{-2}&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|\mu^{2}t^{-1}|\bar{x}|^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\end{cases}.

Notice that

(2.10)

|\mu_{t}\hat{\varphi}_{1}|\lesssim|\mu_{t}|t^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+|\mu_{t}||\bar{x}|^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{4t}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}.

Therefore, by Lemma
A.1
, we obtain

{\mathcal{T}}_{4}^{out}\left[|\mu_{t}|t^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}\right]\lesssim t^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t/2}s|\mu_{t}(s)|ds+\begin{cases}|\mu_{t}|&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
|\mu_{t}|t|x|^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\end{cases}.

By Lemma
A.2
, we have

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\mathcal{T}}_{4}^{out}\left[|\mu_{t}||\bar{x}|^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{4t}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}\right]\lesssim{\mathcal{T}}_{4}^{out}\left[|\mu_{t}|t^{2}|\bar{x}|^{-6}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t/2}s|\mu_{t}(s)|ds+\begin{cases}|\mu_{t}|&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
|\bar{x}|^{-6}(t^{3}|\mu_{t}|+\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t/2}|\mu_{t}(s)|s^{2}ds)&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\end{cases},

and thus

|{\mathcal{T}}_{4}^{out}[\mu_{t}\hat{\varphi}_{1}]|\lesssim t^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t/2}|\mu_{t}(s)|sds+\begin{cases}|\mu_{t}|&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
|\bar{x}|^{-6}\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t}|\mu_{t}(s)|s^{2}ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\end{cases}.

It then follows that

\displaystyle|\tilde{\varphi}_{1b}[\mu]|\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t/2}(s^{-1}\mu^{3}(s)+s|\mu_{t}(s)|)ds

\displaystyle+\begin{cases}\mu^{3}t^{-2}+|\mu_{t}|&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\mu^{3}t^{-1}|\bar{x}|^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}+|\bar{x}|^{-6}\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t}s^{2}|\mu_{t}(s)|ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\end{cases},

(2.11)

\displaystyle\big|\tilde{\varphi}_{1b}[\mu+\mu_{1}]-\tilde{\varphi}_{1b}[\mu]\big|\lesssim t^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t/2}\big(s^{-1}|\mu_{1}(s)|\mu^{2}(s)+s|\mu_{1t}(s)|\big)ds

\displaystyle+\begin{cases}t^{-2}\mu^{2}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|\mu^{2}t^{-1}|\bar{x}|^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}+|\bar{x}|^{-6}\Big(t^{3}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|+\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t/2}s^{2}|\mu_{1t}(s)|ds\Big)&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\end{cases}.

In particular, for
|\mu|\lesssim(\ln t)^{-1}
,
|\mu_{t}|\lesssim t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}
, one has

(2.12)

|\tilde{\varphi}_{1b}[\mu]|\lesssim t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+t^{2}(\ln t)^{-2}|\bar{x}|^{-6}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}},

|E-\tilde{E}|\lesssim(t\ln t)^{-3}\mathbf{1}_{\{\sqrt{t}\leq|\bar{x}|\leq 2\sqrt{t}\}},\quad|\mu_{t}\hat{\varphi}_{1}|\lesssim(t\ln t)^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}|\bar{x}|^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{4t}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}.

Then by scaling argument, we have

|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\tilde{\varphi}_{1b}[\mu]|\lesssim t^{-\frac{3}{2}}(\ln t)^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+t^{\frac{3}{2}}(\ln t)^{-2}|\bar{x}|^{-6}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}.

Combining above estimates with (
2.7
), we have

|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\varphi_{1}|\lesssim t^{-\frac{3}{2}}(\ln t)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+t^{\frac{3}{2}}(\ln t)^{-1}|\bar{x}|^{-6}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}.

∎

Lemma 2.2
.

Assume that
\mu
satisfies (
2.2
) and
\mu_{1}
satisfies
|\mu_{1}|\leq\frac{\mu}{2}
,
|\mu_{1t}|\leq\frac{|\mu_{t}|}{2}
. Consider

{\partial}_{t}\varphi_{2}=\Delta_{\bar{x}}\varphi_{2}+\mu^{-2}\mu_{t}Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu})\eta(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}}),

where
\varphi_{2}
is given by
\varphi_{2}=\varphi_{2}[\mu]={\mathcal{T}}^{out}_{4}\left[\mu^{-2}\mu_{t}Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu})\eta(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\right]
.
Then the following estimates hold

(2.13)

\displaystyle|\varphi_{2}[\mu]|\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t/2}s|\mu_{t}(s)|ds+\begin{cases}|\mu_{t}|(\ln(\mu^{-1}t^{\frac{1}{2}})+1)&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|\leq\mu\\
|\mu_{t}|(\ln(|\bar{x}|^{-1}t^{\frac{1}{2}})+1)&\mbox{ \ if \ }\mu<|\bar{x}|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
|\mu_{t}|t|\bar{x}|^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\end{cases}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\begin{cases}(t\ln t)^{-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|\leq\mu\\
t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}(\ln(|\bar{x}|^{-1}t^{\frac{1}{2}})+1)&\mbox{ \ if \ }\mu<|\bar{x}|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\end{cases},

(2.14)

|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\varphi_{2}[\mu]|\lesssim\begin{cases}t^{-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|\leq\mu\\
t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}(\ln(|\bar{x}|^{-1}t^{\frac{1}{2}})+1)|\bar{x}|^{-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\mu<|\bar{x}|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
t^{-\frac{3}{2}}(\ln t)^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{30t}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\end{cases},

(2.15)

\displaystyle\big|\varphi_{2}[\mu+\mu_{1}]-\varphi_{2}[\mu]\big|\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t/2}s|\mu_{t}(s)|\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(s)|}{\mu(s)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(s)|}{|\mu_{t}(s)|}\Big)ds

\displaystyle+|\mu_{t}|\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|}{\mu(t)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|}{|\mu_{t}(t)|}\Big)\begin{cases}\ln(\mu^{-1}t^{\frac{1}{2}})+1&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|\leq\mu\\
\ln(|\bar{x}|^{-1}t^{\frac{1}{2}})+1&\mbox{ \ if \ }\mu<|\bar{x}|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
t|\bar{x}|^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\end{cases}.

More precisely,

\displaystyle\varphi_{2}[\mu]=

\displaystyle\bigg[-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{t}(s)}{t-s}ds+O\Big(t^{-2}\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t}s|\mu_{t}(s)|ds+\min\{\mu^{-1}|\mu_{t}||\bar{x}|,\ln t|\mu_{t}|\}\Big)\bigg]\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}

\displaystyle+O\Big(t^{-2}\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t}s|\mu_{t}(s)|ds\Big)e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}},

\displaystyle\varphi_{2}[\mu+\mu_{1}]-\varphi_{2}[\mu]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Bigg[-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{1t}(s)}{t-s}ds+O\bigg(|\mu_{t}|\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|}{\mu(t)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|}{|\mu_{t}(t)|}\Big)+t^{-2}\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t/2}s|\mu_{t}(s)|\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(s)|}{\mu(s)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(s)|}{|\mu_{t}(s)|}\Big)ds

\displaystyle+|\mu_{t}|\ln t\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|}{\mu(t)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|}{|\mu_{t}(t)|}\Big)^{2}+\mu^{-1}|\mu_{t}|\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|}{\mu(t)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|}{|\mu_{t}(t)|}\Big)|\bar{x}|\bigg)\Bigg]\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}

\displaystyle+O\bigg(|\mu_{t}|\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|}{\mu(t)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|}{|\mu_{t}(t)|}\Big)+t^{-2}\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t/2}s|\mu_{t}(s)|\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(s)|}{\mu(s)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(s)|}{|\mu_{t}(s)|}\Big)ds\bigg)e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}.

Proof.

Since

(2.16)

|\mu^{-2}\mu_{t}Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu})\eta(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})|\lesssim\mu^{-2}\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq\mu\}}+\mu_{t}|\bar{x}|^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{\mu<|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}},

by Lemma
A.1
and (
2.2
), we conclude the validity of (
2.13
).
By scaling argument, (
2.14
) follows.

For
\mu_{1}
satisfying
|\mu_{1}|\leq\frac{\mu}{2}
and
|\mu_{1t}|\leq\frac{|\mu_{t}|}{2}
, we have

(2.17)

\displaystyle(\mu+\mu_{1})^{-2}(\mu_{t}+\mu_{1t})Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu+\mu_{1}})-\mu^{-2}\mu_{t}Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu^{-2}\mu_{1t}Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu})-\mu^{-3}\mu_{1}\mu_{t}\Big(2Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu})+\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu}\cdot\nabla Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu})\Big)+\big(\mu^{-3}\mu_{1}\mu_{t}+\mu^{-2}\mu_{1t}\big)\langle\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu}\rangle^{-2}O\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}|}{\mu}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}|}{|\mu_{t}|}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\bigg(\mu^{-2}|\mu_{t}|\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}|}{\mu}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}|}{|\mu_{t}|}\Big)\langle\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu}\rangle^{-2}\bigg).

Then by Lemma
A.1
, one gets (
2.15
).

In order to extract the dominating part of
\varphi_{2}
for the preparation of solving the orthogonal equation, we split
\varphi_{2}
into several parts to estimate. Set
\mu_{0}(t)=(\ln t)^{-1}
and consider

\displaystyle\varphi_{2}=

\displaystyle\left(\int_{t_{0}}^{t/2}+\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}(t)}+\int_{t-\mu_{0}^{2}(t)}^{t}\right)\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}(4\pi(t-s))^{-2}e^{-\frac{|x-z|^{2}}{4(t-s)}}\mu^{-2}(s)\mu_{t}(s)Z_{5}(\frac{|z|}{\mu(s)})\eta(\frac{|z|}{\sqrt{s}})dzds

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3}.

For
I_{1}
, by rearrangement inequality, we have

\displaystyle|I_{1}|\lesssim\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t/2}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}(t-s)^{-2}e^{-\frac{|x-z|^{2}}{4(t-s)}}\mu^{-2}(s)|\mu_{t}(s)|\langle\frac{|z|}{\mu(s)}\rangle^{-2}\eta(\frac{|z|}{\sqrt{s}})dzds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-2}\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t/2}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}e^{-\frac{|z|^{2}}{4t}}\mu^{-2}(s)|\mu_{t}(s)|\langle\frac{|z|}{\mu(s)}\rangle^{-2}\eta(\frac{|z|}{\sqrt{s}})dzds\lesssim t^{-2}\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t/2}\mu^{-2}(s)|\mu_{t}(s)|\int_{0}^{2\sqrt{s}}\langle\frac{r}{\mu(s)}\rangle^{-2}r^{3}drds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-2}\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t/2}s|\mu_{t}(s)|ds

since
\int_{0}^{\mu(s)}\langle\frac{r}{\mu(s)}\rangle^{-2}r^{3}dr\sim\mu^{4}(s),\quad\int_{\mu(s)}^{2\sqrt{s}}\langle\frac{r}{\mu(s)}\rangle^{-2}r^{3}dr\lesssim s\mu^{2}(s)
.

Using (
2.17
) and similar calculations above, one has

\big|I_{1}[\mu+\mu_{1}]-I_{1}[\mu]\big|\lesssim t^{-2}\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t/2}s|\mu_{t}(s)|\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(s)|}{\mu(s)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(s)|}{|\mu_{t}(s)|}\Big)ds.

For
I_{3}
, we have

\displaystyle|I_{3}|\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{t-\mu_{0}^{2}(t)}^{t}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}(t-s)^{-2}e^{-\frac{|z|^{2}}{4(t-s)}}\mu^{-2}(s)|\mu_{t}(s)|\langle\frac{|z|}{\mu(s)}\rangle^{-2}\eta(\frac{|z|}{\sqrt{s}})dzds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-2}|\mu_{t}|\int_{t-\mu_{0}^{2}(t)}^{t}(t-s)^{-2}\int_{0}^{2\sqrt{t}}e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{4(t-s)}}\langle\frac{r}{\mu(t)}\rangle^{-2}r^{3}drds\lesssim|\mu_{t}|

since for
s\in(t-\mu_{0}^{2}(t),t)
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\mu(t)}e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{4(t-s)}}\langle\frac{r}{\mu(t)}\rangle^{-2}r^{3}dr\sim\int_{0}^{\mu(t)}e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{4(t-s)}}r^{3}dr\sim(t-s)^{2}\int_{0}^{\frac{\mu^{2}(t)}{4(t-s)}}e^{-z}zdz\sim(t-s)^{2},

\displaystyle\int_{\mu(t)}^{2\sqrt{t}}e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{4(t-s)}}\langle\frac{r}{\mu(t)}\rangle^{-2}r^{3}dr\sim(t-s)\mu^{2}(t)\int_{\frac{\mu^{2}(t)}{4(t-s)}}^{\frac{t}{t-s}}e^{-z}dz\lesssim(t-s)\mu^{2}(t)e^{-\frac{\mu^{2}(t)}{4(t-s)}}\lesssim(t-s)^{2}.

Similarly, using (
2.17
), one has

\big|I_{3}[\mu+\mu_{1}]-I_{3}[\mu]\big|\lesssim|\mu_{t}|\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|}{\mu(t)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|}{|\mu_{t}(t)|}\Big).

For
I_{2}
, more delicate calculations are needed to single out the leading term. Set

I_{2}=I_{02}+(I_{2}-I_{02}),

where

I_{02}=\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}(t)}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}(4\pi(t-s))^{-2}e^{-\frac{|z|^{2}}{4(t-s)}}\mu^{-2}(s)\mu_{t}(s)Z_{5}(\frac{|z|}{\mu(s)})\eta(\frac{|z|}{\sqrt{s}})dzds:=I_{*}+I_{021}+I_{022}

and

\displaystyle I_{*}=

\displaystyle-2^{\frac{3}{2}}\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}(t)}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}(4\pi(t-s))^{-2}e^{-\frac{|z|^{2}}{4(t-s)}}\mu^{-2}(s)\mu_{t}(s)\frac{\mu^{2}(s)}{|z|^{2}}dzds,

\displaystyle I_{021}=

\displaystyle 2^{\frac{3}{2}}\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}(t)}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}(4\pi(t-s))^{-2}e^{-\frac{|z|^{2}}{4(t-s)}}\mu^{-2}(s)\mu_{t}(s)\frac{\mu^{2}(s)}{|z|^{2}}\Big(1-\eta(\frac{|z|}{\sqrt{s}})\Big)dzds,

\displaystyle I_{022}=

\displaystyle\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}(t)}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}(4\pi(t-s))^{-2}e^{-\frac{|z|^{2}}{4(t-s)}}\mu^{-2}(s)\mu_{t}(s)\Big(Z_{5}(\frac{|z|}{\mu(s)})+2^{\frac{3}{2}}\frac{\mu^{2}(s)}{|z|^{2}}\Big)\eta(\frac{|z|}{\sqrt{s}})dzds.

For
I_{*}
, we evaluate

\displaystyle I_{*}=-2^{\frac{3}{2}}|S^{3}|\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}(t)}\mu_{t}(s)\int_{0}^{\infty}(4\pi(t-s))^{-2}e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{4(t-s)}}rdrds=-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}(t)}\frac{\mu_{t}(s)}{t-s}ds.

In the same way, one has

I_{*}[\mu+\mu_{1}]-I_{*}[\mu]=-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}(t)}\frac{\mu_{1t}(s)}{t-s}ds.

For
I_{021}
, we get

|I_{021}|\lesssim|\mu_{t}|\int_{\frac{t}{2}}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}(t)}\int_{\frac{\sqrt{t}}{2}}^{\infty}(t-s)^{-2}e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{4(t-s)}}rdrds\lesssim|\mu_{t}|,\quad\big|I_{021}[\mu+\mu_{1}]-I_{021}[\mu]\big|\lesssim\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|.

For
I_{022}
, we have

\displaystyle|I_{022}|\lesssim\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}(t-s)^{-2}e^{-\frac{|z|^{2}}{4(t-s)}}\mu^{-2}(s)|\mu_{t}(s)|(\frac{|z|}{\mu(s)})^{-2}\langle\frac{|z|}{\mu(s)}\rangle^{-2}\eta(\frac{|z|}{\sqrt{s}})dzds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle|\mu_{t}|\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}(t-s)^{-2}e^{-\frac{|z|^{2}}{4(t-s)}}\left|z\right|^{-2}\langle\frac{|z|}{\mu(t)}\rangle^{-2}\eta(\frac{|z|}{2\sqrt{t}})dzds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle|\mu_{t}|\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}(t-s)^{-2}\int_{0}^{4\sqrt{t}}e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{4(t-s)}}\langle\frac{r}{\mu(t)}\rangle^{-2}rdrds\lesssim|\mu_{t}|\mu^{2}\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}(t-s)^{-2}\Big(1-\ln(\frac{\mu^{2}(t)}{4(t-s)})\Big)ds\lesssim|\mu_{t}|

since for
\frac{t}{2}\leq s\leq t-\mu^{2}(t)
,

\int_{0}^{\mu(t)}e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{4(t-s)}}\langle\frac{r}{\mu(t)}\rangle^{-2}rdr\lesssim\mu^{2}(t),

\displaystyle\int_{\mu(t)}^{4\sqrt{t}}e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{4(t-s)}}\langle\frac{r}{\mu(t)}\rangle^{-2}rdr\sim\mu^{2}(t)\int_{\mu(t)}^{4\sqrt{t}}e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{4(t-s)}}r^{-1}dr\sim\mu^{2}(t)\int_{\frac{\mu^{2}(t)}{4(t-s)}}^{\frac{4t}{t-s}}e^{-z}z^{-1}dz\lesssim\mu^{2}(t)\Big(1-\ln(\frac{\mu^{2}(t)}{4(t-s)})\Big).

Next, we estimate
I_{022}[\mu+\mu_{1}]-I_{022}[\mu]
. By (
2.17
), we have

\displaystyle(\mu+\mu_{1})^{-2}(\mu_{t}+\mu_{1t})\left(Z_{5}(\frac{|z|}{\mu+\mu_{1}})+2^{\frac{3}{2}}\frac{(\mu+\mu_{1})^{2}}{|z|^{2}}\right)-\mu^{-2}\mu_{t}\left(Z_{5}(\frac{|z|}{\mu})+2^{\frac{3}{2}}\frac{\mu^{2}}{|z|^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu^{-2}\mu_{1t}Z_{5}(\frac{z}{\mu})+2^{\frac{3}{2}}\frac{\mu_{1t}}{|z|^{2}}-\mu^{-3}\mu_{1}\mu_{t}\Big(2Z_{5}(\frac{z}{\mu})+\frac{z}{\mu}\cdot\nabla Z_{5}(\frac{z}{\mu})\Big)+\big(\mu^{-3}\mu_{1}\mu_{t}+\mu^{-2}\mu_{1t}\big)\langle\frac{z}{\mu}\rangle^{-2}O\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}|}{\mu}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}|}{|\mu_{t}|}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\Big(\mu^{-2}|\mu_{1t}|\frac{|z|^{-2}}{\mu^{-2}}\langle\frac{z}{\mu}\rangle^{-2}\Big)-\mu^{-3}\mu_{1}\mu_{t}\Big(2Z_{5}(\frac{z}{\mu})+\frac{z}{\mu}\cdot\nabla Z_{5}(\frac{z}{\mu})\Big)+\big(\mu^{-3}\mu_{1}\mu_{t}+\mu^{-2}\mu_{1t}\big)\langle\frac{z}{\mu}\rangle^{-2}O\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}|}{\mu}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}|}{|\mu_{t}|}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\bigg(\mu^{-2}|\mu_{t}|\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}|}{\mu}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}|}{|\mu_{t}|}\Big)\frac{|z|^{-2}}{\mu^{-2}}\langle\frac{z}{\mu}\rangle^{-2}\bigg)+O\bigg(\mu^{-2}|\mu_{t}|\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}|}{\mu}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}|}{|\mu_{t}|}\Big)^{2}\langle\frac{z}{\mu}\rangle^{-2}\bigg).

Similar to the estimates of
I_{022}
, we then have

\displaystyle\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}(4\pi(t-s))^{-2}e^{-\frac{|z|^{2}}{4(t-s)}}\mu^{-2}(s)|\mu_{t}(s)|\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(s)|}{\mu(s)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(s)|}{|\mu_{t}(s)|}\Big)\frac{|z|^{-2}}{\mu^{-2}(s)}\langle\frac{z}{\mu(s)}\rangle^{-2}\eta(\frac{|z|}{\sqrt{s}})dzds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|\mu_{t}|\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|}{\mu(t)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|}{|\mu_{t}(t)|}\Big),

\displaystyle\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}(4\pi(t-s))^{-2}e^{-\frac{|z|^{2}}{4(t-s)}}\mu^{-2}(s)|\mu_{t}(s)|\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(s)|}{\mu(s)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(s)|}{|\mu_{t}(s)|}\Big)^{2}\langle\frac{z}{\mu(s)}\rangle^{-2}\eta(\frac{|z|}{\sqrt{s}})dzds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-2}|\mu_{t}|\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}|}{\mu}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}|}{|\mu_{t}|}\Big)^{2}\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}(t-s)^{-2}\int_{0}^{2t^{\frac{1}{2}}}e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{4(t-s)}}\langle\frac{r}{\mu(t)}\rangle^{-2}r^{3}drds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|\mu_{t}|\ln t\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|}{\mu(t)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|}{|\mu_{t}(t)|}\Big)^{2}

since

(2.18)

\int_{0}^{\mu(t)}e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{4(t-s)}}\langle\frac{r}{\mu(t)}\rangle^{-2}r^{3}dr\lesssim\mu^{4},\quad\int_{\mu(t)}^{2t^{\frac{1}{2}}}e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{4(t-s)}}\langle\frac{r}{\mu(t)}\rangle^{-2}r^{3}dr\sim\mu^{2}\int_{\mu(t)}^{2t^{\frac{1}{2}}}e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{4(t-s)}}rdr\lesssim\mu^{2}(t-s).

Therefore, one has

\big|I_{022}[\mu+\mu_{1}]-I_{022}[\mu]\big|\lesssim|\mu_{t}|\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|}{\mu(t)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|}{|\mu_{t}(t)|}\Big)+|\mu_{t}|\ln t\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|}{\mu(t)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|}{|\mu_{t}(t)|}\Big)^{2}.

Let us now estimate
I_{2}-I_{02}

\displaystyle|I_{2}-I_{02}|=\bigg|\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}(t)}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}\int_{0}^{1}(4\pi(t-s))^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\theta x-z|^{2}}{4(t-s)}}\frac{\theta x-z}{2(t-s)}\cdot x\mu^{-2}(s)\mu_{t}(s)Z_{5}(\frac{|z|}{\mu(s)})\eta(\frac{|z|}{\sqrt{s}})d\theta dzds\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-2}|\mu_{t}||x|\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}(t)}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}\int_{0}^{1}(t-s)^{-\frac{5}{2}}e^{-\frac{|\theta x-z|^{2}}{8(t-s)}}\langle\frac{|z|}{\mu(s)}\rangle^{-2}\eta(\frac{|z|}{\sqrt{s}})d\theta dzds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-2}|\mu_{t}||x|\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}(t)}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}(t-s)^{-\frac{5}{2}}e^{-\frac{|z|^{2}}{8(t-s)}}\langle\frac{|z|}{\mu(s)}\rangle^{-2}\eta(\frac{|z|}{\sqrt{s}})dzds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-2}|\mu_{t}||x|\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}(t)}(t-s)^{-\frac{5}{2}}\int_{0}^{2\sqrt{t}}e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{8(t-s)}}\langle\frac{r}{\mu(t)}\rangle^{-2}r^{3}drds\lesssim\mu^{-1}|\mu_{t}||\bar{x}|

since
\int_{0}^{2\sqrt{t}}e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{8(t-s)}}\langle\frac{r}{\mu(t)}\rangle^{-2}r^{3}dr\lesssim\mu^{2}(t-s)
by similar estimate in (
2.18
).

Using rearrangement inequality, one has another upper bound for
|I_{2}-I_{02}|
,

\displaystyle|I_{2}-I_{02}|\lesssim\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}(t)}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}(t-s)^{-2}e^{-\frac{|z|^{2}}{4(t-s)}}\mu^{-2}(s)|\mu_{t}(s)|\langle\frac{|z|}{\mu(s)}\rangle^{-2}\eta(\frac{|z|}{\sqrt{s}})dzds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-2}|\mu_{t}|\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}(t)}(t-s)^{-2}\int_{0}^{2t^{\frac{1}{2}}}e^{-\frac{r^{2}}{4(t-s)}}\langle\frac{r}{\mu(t)}\rangle^{-2}r^{3}drds\lesssim|\mu_{t}(t)|\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}(t)}(t-s)^{-1}ds\lesssim\ln t|\mu_{t}|.

Thus

|I_{2}-I_{02}|\lesssim\min\big\{\mu^{-1}|\mu_{t}||\bar{x}|,\ln t|\mu_{t}|\big\}.

Using (
2.17
) and similar calculations, one has

\big|(I_{2}-I_{02})[\mu+\mu_{1}]-(I_{2}-I_{02})[\mu]\big|\lesssim\mu^{-1}|\mu_{t}|\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|}{\mu(t)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|}{|\mu_{t}(t)|}\Big)|\bar{x}|.

Combining all the estimates above, we conclude the validity of Lemma
2.2
.

∎

Recalling
\varphi[\mu]=\varphi_{1}[\mu]+\varphi_{2}[\mu]
and combining Lemma
2.1
and Lemma
2.2
, one has

Corollary 2.3
.

Assume that
\mu
satisfies (
2.2
) and
\mu_{1}
satisfies
|\mu_{1}|\leq\frac{\mu}{2}
,
|\mu_{1t}|\leq\frac{|\mu_{t}|}{2}
. We have

\displaystyle|\varphi[\mu]|\lesssim

\displaystyle(\mu t^{-1}+g[\mu])\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+\begin{cases}|\mu_{t}|(\ln(\mu^{-1}t^{\frac{1}{2}})+1)&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|\leq\mu\\
|\mu_{t}|(\ln(|\bar{x}|^{-1}t^{\frac{1}{2}})+1)&\mbox{ \ if \ }\mu<|\bar{x}|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
t|\mu_{t}||\bar{x}|^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\end{cases}

\displaystyle+O\Big(\mu|\bar{x}|^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}+|\bar{x}|^{-6}\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t}s^{2}|\mu_{t}(s)|ds+g[\mu]e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}\Big)\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(t\ln t)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+O\big(t^{2}(\ln t)^{-1}|\bar{x}|^{-6}\big)\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}

where

g[\mu]=O\Big(t^{-2}\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t}(s^{-1}\mu^{3}(s)+s|\mu_{t}(s)|)ds\Big).

|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\varphi[\mu]|\lesssim\begin{cases}t^{-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|\leq\mu\\
t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}(\ln(|\bar{x}|^{-1}t^{\frac{1}{2}})+1)|\bar{x}|^{-1}+t^{-\frac{3}{2}}(\ln t)^{-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\mu<|\bar{x}|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
t^{\frac{3}{2}}(\ln t)^{-1}|\bar{x}|^{-6}&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\end{cases}.

\displaystyle\big|\varphi[\mu+\mu_{1}]-\varphi[\mu]\big|\lesssim\left(O(|\mu_{1}|t^{-1})+\tilde{g}[\mu,\mu_{1}]\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}

\displaystyle+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|}{\mu(t)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|}{|\mu_{t}(t)|}\Big)\begin{cases}|\mu_{t}|(\ln(\mu^{-1}t^{\frac{1}{2}})+1)&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|\leq\mu\\
|\mu_{t}|(\ln(|\bar{x}|^{-1}t^{\frac{1}{2}})+1)&\mbox{ \ if \ }\mu<|\bar{x}|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
t|\mu_{t}||\bar{x}|^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\end{cases}

\displaystyle+O\bigg(\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1}(t_{1})||\bar{x}|^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}+|\bar{x}|^{-6}\Big(t^{3}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|+\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t}s^{2}|\mu_{1t}(s)|ds\Big)+\tilde{g}[\mu,\mu_{1}]e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}\bigg)\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}

where

\displaystyle\tilde{g}[\mu,\mu_{1}]=O\Big(|\mu_{t}|\ln t\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|}{\mu(t)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|}{|\mu_{t}(t)|}\Big)^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle+O\bigg(|\mu_{t}|\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|}{\mu(t)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|}{|\mu_{t}(t)|}\Big)+t^{-2}\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t}\Big(s^{-1}|\mu_{1}(s)|\mu^{2}(s)+s|\mu_{t}(s)|\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(s)|}{\mu(s)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(s)|}{|\mu_{t}(s)|}\Big)\Big)ds\bigg).

More precisely,

\displaystyle\varphi[\mu]=

\displaystyle\bigg[-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\mu t^{-1}+\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{t}(s)}{t-s}ds\Big)+O\big(\mu t^{-2}|\bar{x}|^{2}+|\mu_{t}|\min\{\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\mu},\ln t\}\big)+g[\mu]\bigg]\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}

\displaystyle+O\Big(\mu|\bar{x}|^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}+|\bar{x}|^{-6}\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t}s^{2}|\mu_{t}(s)|ds+g[\mu]e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}\Big)\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}},

\displaystyle\varphi[\mu+\mu_{1}]-\varphi[\mu]=\bigg[-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\mu_{1}t^{-1}+\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{1t}(s)}{t-s}ds\Big)

\displaystyle+O\Big(|\mu_{1}|t^{-2}|\bar{x}|^{2}+|\mu_{t}|\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|}{\mu(t)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|}{|\mu_{t}(t)|}\Big)\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\mu}\Big)+\tilde{g}[\mu,\mu_{1}]\bigg]\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}

\displaystyle+O\Big(\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1}(t_{1})||\bar{x}|^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}+|\bar{x}|^{-6}\Big(t^{3}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|+\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t/2}s^{2}|\mu_{1t}(s)|ds\Big)+e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}\tilde{g}[\mu,\mu_{1}]\Big)\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}.

In order to extract the leading term, we will use the precise version of
\varphi[\mu]
and
\varphi[\mu+\mu_{1}]-\varphi[\mu]
when calculating the orthogonal equation. In other cases, we are inclined to adopt the rougher upper bound.

With introduction of the correction term
\varphi
, the new error is given by

\displaystyle S[u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]]=

\displaystyle 3u_{1}^{2}\varphi[\mu]+3u_{1}\varphi^{2}[\mu]+\varphi^{3}[\mu]+\xi_{t}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}\varphi[\mu](x-\xi,t)

\displaystyle+\mu^{-2}\xi_{t}\cdot\nabla w(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu})\eta(\frac{x-\xi}{\sqrt{t}})+\mu^{-1}t^{-\frac{1}{2}}\xi_{t}\cdot\nabla\eta(\frac{x-\xi}{\sqrt{t}})w(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu}).

2.3.
Further improvement by solving an elliptic equation

In order to find suitable parameters to design the topology for solving inner-outer gluing system and the orthogonal equation, we will use the corresponding linearized elliptic equation to cut off the error term so that the time decay rate will be improved.

Set the correction term as

\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)

where
\bar{\mu}_{0}
is the leading order of
\mu
to be determined later. Formally speaking,
\Phi_{0}
will be chosen to satisfy the following equation

(2.19)

\displaystyle\Delta_{y}\Phi_{0}+3w^{2}(y)\Phi_{0}\approx

\displaystyle-\mu^{3}\Big(3u_{1}^{2}\varphi[\mu](\bar{x},t)+3u_{1}\varphi^{2}[\mu](\bar{x},t)\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-3\mu\Big(w^{2}(y)\eta^{2}(\frac{\mu y}{\sqrt{t}})\varphi[\mu](\mu y,t)+\mu w(y)\eta(\frac{\mu y}{\sqrt{t}})\varphi^{2}[\mu](\mu y,t)\Big).

Set

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}[\mu]:=

\displaystyle\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}\Big(w^{2}(y)\eta^{2}(\frac{\mu y}{\sqrt{t}})\varphi[\mu](\mu y,t)+\mu w(y)\eta(\frac{\mu y}{\sqrt{t}})\varphi^{2}[\mu](\mu y,t)\Big)Z_{5}(y)dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu^{-4}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}\Big(w^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu})Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu})\eta^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\varphi[\mu](\bar{x},t)+\mu w(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu})Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu})\eta(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\varphi^{2}[\mu](\bar{x},t)\Big)d\bar{x}.

In order to find
\Phi_{0}
with fast spatial decay, we aim to find
\bar{\mu}_{0}
as the leading order of
\mu
such that
\mathcal{M}[\mu]\approx 0
. In other words, above orthogonality condition is satisfied at leading order for careful choice of
\bar{\mu}_{0}
, which will be adjusted and corrected several times in order to further improve the time decay, and we shall see that

\bar{\mu}_{0}\sim(\ln t)^{-1}.

The iteration of finding proper
\bar{\mu}_{0}
consists of three steps:

•

the first step is to single out the leading part in above orthogonal equation, and this results in the blow-up rate predicted in
[
15
]
,

•

the second step is to add next-order correction of the scaling parameter,

•

the last step is to iterate the second step finitely many times such that the new error has sufficiently fast time decay.

We now start the iteration.

Step 1. Finding the leading part
\mu_{0}
.

Using the precise expression of
\varphi[\mu]
in Corollary
2.3
, one has

\displaystyle\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}w^{2}(y)Z_{5}(y)\eta^{2}(\frac{\mu y}{\sqrt{t}})\varphi(\mu y,t)dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\mu t^{-1}+\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{t}(s)}{t-s}ds\Big)\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}w^{2}(y)Z_{5}(y)\eta^{2}(\frac{\mu y}{\sqrt{t}})dy+O\big(\mu^{3}t^{-2}\ln(\mu^{-1}t^{\frac{1}{2}})\big)+O(|\mu_{t}|)+g[\mu],

and

\displaystyle\mu\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}w(y)\eta(\frac{\mu y}{\sqrt{t}})\varphi^{2}(\mu y,t)Z_{5}(y)dy=

\displaystyle\mu\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}w(y)\eta(\frac{\mu y}{\sqrt{t}})Z_{5}(y)O\big(\mu^{2}t^{-2}+g^{2}[\mu]+|\mu_{t}|^{2}(\ln(\mu^{-1}t^{\frac{1}{2}}))^{2}\big)dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu\ln(\mu^{-1}t^{\frac{1}{2}})O\big(\mu^{2}t^{-2}+g^{2}[\mu]+|\mu_{t}|^{2}(\ln(\mu^{-1}t^{\frac{1}{2}}))^{2}\big).

Therefore, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}[\mu]=

\displaystyle-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}w^{2}(y)Z_{5}(y)\eta^{2}(\frac{\mu y}{\sqrt{t}})dy\bigg(\mu t^{-1}+\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{t}(s)}{t-s}ds

\displaystyle+O(|\mu_{t}|)+g[\mu]+\mu\ln(\mu^{-1}t^{\frac{1}{2}})O\big(\mu^{2}t^{-2}+g^{2}[\mu]+|\mu_{t}|^{2}(\ln(\mu^{-1}t^{\frac{1}{2}}))^{2}\big)\bigg)

where
\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}w^{2}(y)Z_{5}(y)\eta^{2}(\frac{\mu y}{\sqrt{t}})dy<0
when
t
is large.
Balancing the following two leading terms

\mu t^{-1}+\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{t}(s)}{t-s}ds\sim\mu t^{-1}+\mu_{t}(t)\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\frac{1}{t-s}ds\sim\mu t^{-1}+\mu_{t}\ln t=0,

one gets
\mu_{0}=(\ln t)^{-1}
as the leading order of
\mu
.
Notice that

\displaystyle\mu_{0}t^{-1}+\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{0t}(s)}{t-s}ds=(t\ln t)^{-1}-t^{-1}\int_{1/2}^{1-t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}}\frac{(\ln t+\ln z)^{-2}}{z(1-z)}dz

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(t\ln t)^{-1}-t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}(1+O((\ln t)^{-1}))\int_{1/2}^{1-t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}}\frac{1}{z(1-z)}dz

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(t\ln t)^{-1}-t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}(1+O((\ln t)^{-1}))\ln(t(\ln t)^{2}-1)=O(t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}\ln\ln t),

O(|\mu_{0t}|)+g[\mu_{0}]+\mu_{0}\ln(\mu_{0}^{-1}t^{\frac{1}{2}})O\big(\mu_{0}^{2}t^{-2}+|\mu_{0t}|^{2}(\ln(\mu_{0}^{-1}t^{\frac{1}{2}}))^{2}+g^{2}[\mu_{0}]\big)=O(t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}),

and thus

\mathcal{M}[\mu_{0}]=O(t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}\ln\ln t).

Step 2. Finding the corrected term
\mu_{01}
.

In order to improve the time decay of the error, we introduce the next order term
\mu_{01}
and make the ansatz
|\mu_{01}|\ll\mu_{0}
,
|\mu_{01t}|\ll|\mu_{0t}|
.

Then
by Corollary
2.3
, we estimate

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}[\mu_{0}+\mu_{01}]

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}\bigg((\mu_{0}+\mu_{01})^{-4}w^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}+\mu_{01}})Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}+\mu_{01}})\eta^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\varphi[\mu_{0}+\mu_{01}](\bar{x},t)

\displaystyle+(\mu_{0}+\mu_{01})^{-3}w(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}+\mu_{01}})Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}+\mu_{01}})\eta(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\varphi^{2}[\mu_{0}+\mu_{01}](\bar{x},t)\bigg)d\bar{x}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}\Bigg\{\Big[(\mu_{0}+\mu_{01})^{-4}w^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}+\mu_{01}})Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}+\mu_{01}})-\mu_{0}^{-4}w^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}})Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}})\Big]\eta^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\varphi[\mu_{0}+\mu_{01}](\bar{x},t)

\displaystyle+\mu_{0}^{-4}w^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}})Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}})\eta^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})(\varphi[\mu_{0}+\mu_{01}]-\varphi[\mu_{0}])(\bar{x},t)+\mu_{0}^{-4}w^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}})Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}})\eta^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\varphi[\mu_{0}](\bar{x},t)

\displaystyle+\Big[(\mu_{0}+\mu_{01})^{-3}w(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}+\mu_{01}})Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}+\mu_{01}})-\mu_{0}^{-3}w(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}})Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}})\Big]\eta(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\varphi^{2}[\mu_{0}+\mu_{01}](\bar{x},t)

\displaystyle+\mu_{0}^{-3}w(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}})Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}})\eta(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})(\varphi^{2}[\mu_{0}+\mu_{01}]-\varphi^{2}[\mu_{0}])(\bar{x},t)+\mu_{0}^{-3}w(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}})Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}})\eta(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\varphi^{2}[\mu_{0}](\bar{x},t)\Bigg\}d\bar{x}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}\Bigg\{O\Big(\frac{|\mu_{01}|}{\mu_{0}}\mu_{0}^{-4}\langle\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}}\rangle^{-6}\Big)\eta^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\bigg[-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big((\mu_{0}+\mu_{01})t^{-1}+\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{0t}(s)+\mu_{01t}(s)}{t-s}ds\Big)

\displaystyle+O\Big((\mu_{0}+\mu_{01})t^{-2}|\bar{x}|^{2}+|\mu_{0t}+\mu_{01t}|\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\mu_{0}+\mu_{01}}\Big)+g[\mu_{0}+\mu_{01}]\bigg]

\displaystyle+\mu_{0}^{-4}w^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}})Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}})\eta^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\bigg[-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\mu_{01}t^{-1}+\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{01t}(s)}{t-s}ds\Big)

\displaystyle+O\Big(|\mu_{01}|t^{-2}|\bar{x}|^{2}+|\mu_{0t}|\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{01}(t_{1})|}{\mu_{0}}+\frac{|\mu_{01t}(t_{1})|}{|\mu_{0t}|}\Big)\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\mu_{0}}\Big)+\tilde{g}[\mu_{0},\mu_{01}]\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{|\mu_{01}|}{\mu_{0}}\mu_{0}^{-3}\langle\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}}\rangle^{-4}\eta(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})O((t\ln t)^{-2})+\mu_{0}^{-3}w(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}})Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}})\eta(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})O((t\ln t)^{-2})\Bigg\}d\bar{x}+\mathcal{M}[\mu_{0}]

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}\Bigg\{O\Big(\frac{|\mu_{01}|}{\mu_{0}}\langle\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}}\rangle^{-6}\Big)\eta^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\bigg[-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\mu_{01}t^{-1}+\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{01t}(s)}{t-s}ds\Big)+O(t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}\ln\ln t)

\displaystyle+O\Big(\mu_{0}^{3}t^{-2}\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{\mu_{0}^{2}}+|\mu_{0t}|\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\mu_{0}}\Big)\bigg]+w^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}})Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}})\eta^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\bigg[-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\mu_{01}t^{-1}+\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{01t}(s)}{t-s}ds\Big)

\displaystyle+O\Big(\mu_{0}^{2}|\mu_{01}|t^{-2}\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{\mu_{0}^{2}}+|\mu_{0t}|\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{01}(t_{1})|}{\mu_{0}}+\frac{|\mu_{01t}(t_{1})|}{|\mu_{0t}|}\Big)\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\mu_{0}}\Big)+\tilde{g}[\mu_{0},\mu_{01}]\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{|\mu_{01}|}{\mu_{0}^{2}}\langle\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}}\rangle^{-4}\eta(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})O((t\ln t)^{-2})+\mu_{0}\langle\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}}\rangle^{-4}\eta(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})O((t\ln t)^{-2})\Bigg\}d(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu_{0}})+\mathcal{M}[\mu_{0}]

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle O(\frac{|\mu_{01}|}{\mu_{0}})\Big(\mu_{01}t^{-1}+\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{01t}(s)}{t-s}ds\Big)-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}w^{2}(y)Z_{5}(y)\eta^{2}(\frac{\mu_{0}y}{\sqrt{t}})dy\Big(\mu_{01}t^{-1}+\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{01t}(s)}{t-s}ds\Big)

\displaystyle+O(t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}\ln\ln t)\frac{|\mu_{01}|}{\mu_{0}}+O\Big(\mu_{0}^{2}|\mu_{01}|t^{-2}\ln t+|\mu_{0t}|\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{01}(t_{1})|}{\mu_{0}}+\frac{|\mu_{01t}(t_{1})|}{|\mu_{0t}|}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle+\tilde{g}[\mu_{0},\mu_{01}]+\frac{|\mu_{01}|}{\mu_{0}^{2}}\ln tO((t\ln t)^{-2})+\mu_{0}\ln tO((t\ln t)^{-2})+\mathcal{M}[\mu_{0}]

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\Big(O(\frac{|\mu_{01}|}{\mu_{0}})-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}w^{2}(y)Z_{5}(y)\eta^{2}(\frac{\mu_{0}y}{\sqrt{t}})dy\Big)\Big(\mu_{01}t^{-1}+\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{01t}(s)}{t-s}ds\Big)

\displaystyle+O\big((t\ln t)^{-1}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{01}(t_{1})|+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{01t}(t_{1})|\big)

\displaystyle+O(t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-1}\ln\ln t)|\mu_{01}|+\tilde{g}[\mu_{0},\mu_{01}]+O((t\ln t)^{-2})+\mathcal{M}[\mu_{0}]

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\Big(O(\frac{|\mu_{01}|}{\mu_{0}})-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}w^{2}(y)Z_{5}(y)\eta^{2}(\frac{\mu_{0}y}{\sqrt{t}})dy\Big)\bigg[\mu_{01}t^{-1}(1+O((\ln t)^{-\frac{1}{2}}))+\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu_{1}}}\frac{\mu_{01t}(s)}{t-s}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{t-t^{1-\nu_{1}}}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}(t)}\frac{\mu_{01t}(t)}{t-s}ds+\mathcal{E}_{\nu_{1}}[\mu_{01}]+O\big((t\ln t)^{-1}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{01}(t_{1})|+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{01t}(t_{1})|\big)

\displaystyle+\tilde{g}[\mu_{01},\mu_{0}]+O((t\ln t)^{-2})+\mathcal{M}[\mu_{0}]\bigg]

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\Big(O(\frac{|\mu_{01}|}{\mu_{0}})-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}w^{2}(y)Z_{5}(y)\eta^{2}(\frac{\mu_{0}y}{\sqrt{t}})dy\Big)\bigg[\mu_{01}t^{-1}\big(1+O((\ln t)^{-\frac{1}{2}})\big)+\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu_{1}}}\frac{\mu_{01t}(s)}{t-s}ds

\displaystyle+\mu_{01t}((1-\nu_{1})\ln t+2\ln\ln t)+O\big((t\ln t)^{-1}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{01}(t_{1})|+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{01t}(t_{1})|\big)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{\nu_{1}}[\mu_{01}]+\tilde{g}[\mu_{01},\mu_{0}]+O((t\ln t)^{-2})+\mathcal{M}[\mu_{0}]\bigg],

where

\mathcal{E}_{\nu_{1}}[\mu_{01}]=\int_{t-t^{1-\nu_{1}}}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}(t)}\frac{\mu_{01t}(s)-\mu_{01t}(t)}{t-s}ds.

Since it is too difficult to solve the nonlocal equation about
\mu_{01}
thoroughly, we put
\mathcal{E}_{\nu_{1}}[\mu_{01}]
aside as the new error term and consider the following equation

(2.20)

\mu_{01t}+\beta_{\nu_{1}}(t)\mu_{01}=f_{\nu_{1}}[\mu_{01}],

where

\beta_{\nu_{1}}(t)=t^{-1}(1+O((\ln t)^{-\frac{1}{2}}))[(1-\nu_{1})\ln t+2\ln\ln t]^{-1},

\displaystyle f_{\nu_{1}}[\mu_{01}]=

\displaystyle\chi(t)\big[(1-\nu_{1})\ln t+2\ln\ln t\big]^{-1}\bigg(-\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu_{1}}}\frac{\mu_{01t}(s)}{t-s}ds-\tilde{g}[\mu_{01},\mu_{0}]

\displaystyle+O\big((t\ln t)^{-1}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{01}(t_{1})|+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{01t}(t_{1})|\big)+O((t\ln t)^{-2})-\mathcal{M}[\mu_{0}]\bigg),

\chi(t)
is a smooth cut-off function such that
\chi(t)=0
for
t<\frac{3}{4}t_{0}
and
\chi(t)=1
for
t\geq t_{0}
. Since
\mu_{01}(t)
will be defined in
(\frac{t_{0}}{4},\infty)
, the introduction of
\chi(t)
is used to avoid the occurrence of
\mu_{01}(t)
for
t
beyond
(\frac{t_{0}}{4},\infty)
in the terms like

\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu_{1}}}\frac{\mu_{01t}(s)}{t-s}ds
. After all, the original orthogonal equation is only required to hold in
(t_{0},\infty)
. For technical reasons, we extend the domain of
\mu_{01}
to
(\frac{t_{0}}{4},\infty)
.

It then suffices to consider the following fixed point problem:

(2.21)

\displaystyle A_{\nu_{1}}[\mu_{01}](t)=

\displaystyle-\int_{t}^{\infty}{\partial}_{t}A_{\nu_{1}}[\mu_{01}](s)ds=-e^{-\int^{t}\beta_{\nu_{1}}(u)du}\int_{t}^{\infty}e^{\int^{s}\beta_{\nu_{1}}(u)du}f_{\nu_{1}}[\mu_{01}](s)ds,

\displaystyle{\partial}_{t}A_{\nu_{1}}[\mu_{01}](t)=

\displaystyle\beta_{\nu_{1}}(t)e^{-\int^{t}\beta_{\nu_{1}}(u)du}\int_{t}^{\infty}e^{\int^{s}\beta_{\nu_{1}}(u)du}f_{\nu_{1}}[\mu_{01}](s)ds+f_{\nu_{1}}[\mu_{01}](t),

where
\nu_{1}\in(0,\frac{1}{2})
will be determined later.

Since

O((t\ln t)^{-2})+|\mathcal{M}[\mu_{0}]|\leq C_{0}t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}\ln\ln t

where
C_{0}\geq 1
is a large constant independent of
t_{0}
, we have

\big|[(1-\nu_{1})\ln t+2\ln\ln t]^{-1}(O((t\ln t)^{-2})+|\mathcal{M}[\mu_{0}]|)\big|\leq(1-\nu_{1})^{-1}C_{0}t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-3}\ln\ln t.

By L’Hôpital’s rule,

\displaystyle\lim\limits_{t\rightarrow\infty}\frac{-\int_{t}^{\infty}e^{\int^{s}\beta_{\nu_{1}}(u)du}s^{-1}(\ln s)^{-3}\ln\ln sds}{e^{\int^{t}\beta_{\nu_{1}}(u)du}(\ln t)^{-2}\ln\ln t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lim\limits_{t\rightarrow\infty}\frac{e^{\int^{t}\beta_{\nu_{1}}(u)du}t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-3}\ln\ln t}{\beta_{\nu_{1}}(t)e^{\int^{t}\beta_{\nu_{1}}(u)du}(\ln t)^{-2}\ln\ln t+e^{\int^{t}\beta_{\nu_{1}}(u)du}(-2)t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-3}\ln\ln t+e^{\int^{t}\beta_{\nu_{1}}(u)du}t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-3}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle[(1-\nu_{1})^{-1}-2]^{-1}.

Notice
\nu_{1}<\frac{1}{2}
implies
e^{\int^{t}\beta_{\nu_{1}}(u)du}\ll(\ln t)^{2}
so that
\int_{t}^{\infty}e^{\int^{s}\beta_{\nu_{1}}(u)du}s^{-1}(\ln s)^{-3}\ln\ln sds
is well defined.
Thus we have

\frac{-\int_{t}^{\infty}e^{\int^{s}\beta_{\nu_{1}}(u)du}s^{-1}(\ln s)^{-3}\ln\ln sds}{e^{\int^{t}\beta_{\nu_{1}}(u)du}(\ln t)^{-2}\ln\ln t}=[(1-\nu_{1})^{-1}-2]^{-1}+o(1)

where
o(1)\rightarrow 0
as
t_{0}\rightarrow\infty
. Then

\displaystyle\bigg|e^{-\int^{t}\beta_{\nu_{1}}(u)du}\int_{\infty}^{t}e^{\int^{s}\beta_{\nu_{1}}(u)du}[(1-\nu_{1})\ln s+2\ln\ln s]^{-1}s^{-1}(\ln s)^{-2}\ln\ln sds\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\nu_{1})^{-1}\bigg|e^{-\int^{t}\beta_{\nu_{1}}(u)du}\int_{\infty}^{t}e^{\int^{s}\beta_{\nu_{1}}(u)du}s^{-1}(\ln s)^{-3}\ln\ln sds\bigg|=|(2\nu_{1}-1)^{-1}+o(1)|(\ln t)^{-2}\ln\ln t,

\displaystyle\bigg|\beta_{\nu_{1}}(t)e^{-\int^{t}\beta_{\nu_{1}}(u)du}\int_{\infty}^{t}e^{\int^{s}\beta_{\nu_{1}}(u)du}[(1-\nu_{1})\ln s+2\ln\ln s]^{-1}s^{-1}(\ln s)^{-2}\ln\ln sds\bigg|

\displaystyle+\Big|[(1-\nu_{1})\ln t+2\ln\ln t]^{-1}t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}\ln\ln t\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle t^{-1}(1-\nu_{1})^{-1}(\ln t)^{-1}|(2\nu_{1}-1)^{-1}+o(1)|(\ln t)^{-2}\ln\ln t+(1-\nu_{1})^{-1}t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-3}\ln\ln t

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\nu_{1})^{-1}(1+|(2\nu_{1}-1)^{-1}+o(1)|)t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-3}\ln\ln t.

From the estimates above, for
\mu_{01}\in C^{1}(t_{0}/4,\infty)
and
\mu_{01}(t)\rightarrow 0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
, we set the norm as

\|\mu_{01}\|_{01}=\sup\limits_{t\geq t_{0}/4}t(\ln t)^{3}(\ln\ln t)^{-1}|\mu_{01t}(t)|

and will solve the fixed point problem (
2.21
) in the space

B_{01}=\left\{g(t)\in C^{1}(t_{0}/4,\infty),\quad g(t)\rightarrow 0\mbox{ \ as \ }t\rightarrow\infty\ :\ \|g\|_{01}\leq 2C_{0}C(\nu_{1})\right\}

where
C(\nu_{1})=(1-\nu_{1})^{-1}(1+|(2\nu_{1}-1)^{-1}+o(1)|)
. We take
\nu_{1}<\frac{1}{2}
and
t_{0}
large enough to guarantee
C(\nu_{1})<\infty
. Let us estimate other terms for
{\partial}_{t}A_{\mu_{1}}[\mu_{01}]
in (
2.21
).

For any
\bar{\mu}_{01}\in B_{01}
,

(2.22)

\displaystyle\chi(t)\Big|\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu_{1}}}\frac{\bar{\mu}_{01t}(s)}{t-s}ds\Big|\leq\|\bar{\mu}_{01}\|_{01}\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu_{1}}}\frac{s^{-1}(\ln s)^{-3}\ln\ln s}{t-s}ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|\bar{\mu}_{01}\|_{01}t^{-1}\int_{1/2}^{1-t^{-\nu_{1}}}\frac{(\ln t+\ln z)^{-3}\ln(\ln t+\ln z)}{z(1-z)}dz

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\bar{\mu}_{01}\|_{01}(1+O((\ln t)^{-\frac{1}{2}}))t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-3}\ln\ln t\int_{1/2}^{1-t^{-\nu_{1}}}\frac{1}{z(1-z)}dz

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|\bar{\mu}_{01}\|_{01}(1+O((\ln t)^{-\frac{1}{2}}))t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-3}\ln\ln t\ln(t^{\nu_{1}}-1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\bar{\mu}_{01}\|_{01}\nu_{1}(1+O((\ln t)^{-\frac{1}{2}}))t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}\ln\ln t

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2C_{0}C(\nu_{1})\nu_{1}(1+O((\ln t)^{-\frac{1}{2}}))t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}\ln\ln t

which implies

\bigg|\big[(1-\nu_{1})\ln t+2\ln\ln t+O(1)\big]^{-1}\chi(t)\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu_{1}}}\frac{\bar{\mu}_{01t}(s)}{t-s}ds\bigg|\leq 2C_{0}C(\nu_{1})\nu_{1}(1-\nu_{1})^{-1}(1+O((\ln t)^{-\frac{1}{2}}))t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-3}\ln\ln t.

We take
\nu_{1}\in(0,\frac{1}{4})
to make

\nu_{1}(1-\nu_{1})^{-1}(1+|(2\nu_{1}-1)^{-1}|)<1
.

Since
\bar{\mu}_{01}\in B_{01}
, one has

|\bar{\mu}_{01}(t)|=|\int_{t}^{\infty}\bar{\mu}_{01t}(s)ds|\lesssim\|\bar{\mu}_{01}\|_{01}(\ln t)^{-2}\ln\ln t
.
Then

\displaystyle\chi(t)\tilde{g}[\bar{\mu}_{01},\mu_{0}]=

\displaystyle O\Big(t^{-2}\int_{t_{0}/2}^{t}(s^{-1}\|\bar{\mu}_{01}\|_{01}(\ln s)^{-4}\ln\ln s+(\ln s)^{-2}\|\bar{\mu}_{01}\|_{01}(\ln s)^{-1}\ln\ln s)ds

\displaystyle+(t\ln t)^{-1}\|\bar{\mu}_{01}\|_{01}^{2}((\ln t)^{-1}\ln\ln t)^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\ln t_{0})^{-\frac{1}{2}}O\big((\|\bar{\mu}_{01}\|_{01}+\|\bar{\mu}_{01}\|_{01}^{2})t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}\ln\ln t\big),

\chi(t)O\big((t\ln t)^{-1}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{01}(t_{1})|+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{01t}(t_{1})|\big)\lesssim t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-3}\ln\ln t\lesssim(\ln t_{0})^{-1}t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}\ln\ln t.

Then for any fixed
\nu_{1}\in(0,\frac{1}{4})
and
t_{0}
large enough, one sees that
A_{\nu_{1}}[\bar{\mu}_{01}]\in B_{01}
.

The contraction property can be derived similarly. Indeed, for any
\mu_{01a},\mu_{01b}\in B_{01}
, similar to (
2.22
), we have

\displaystyle\chi(t)\bigg|\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu_{1}}}\frac{{\partial}_{t}\bar{\mu}_{01a}(s)}{t-s}ds-\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu_{1}}}\frac{{\partial}_{t}\bar{\mu}_{01b}(s)}{t-s}ds\bigg|\leq\|\bar{\mu}_{01a}-\bar{\mu}_{01b}\|_{01}\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu_{1}}}\frac{s^{-1}(\ln s)^{-3}\ln\ln s}{t-s}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\bar{\mu}_{01a}-\bar{\mu}_{01b}\|_{01}\nu_{1}(1+O((\ln t)^{-\frac{1}{2}}))t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}\ln\ln t,

\chi(t)|\tilde{g}[\mu_{01a},\mu_{0}]-\tilde{g}[\mu_{01b},\mu_{0}]|=(\ln t_{0})^{-\frac{1}{2}}O(C_{0}C(\nu_{1})t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}\ln\ln t)\|\bar{\mu}_{01a}-\bar{\mu}_{01b}\|_{01},

(2.23)

\displaystyle\chi(t)\Big|O((t\ln t)^{-1}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{01a}(t_{1})|+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|{\partial}_{t}\mu_{01a}(t_{1})|)

\displaystyle-O((t\ln t)^{-1}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{01b}(t_{1})|+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|{\partial}_{t}\mu_{01b}(t_{1})|)\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\chi(t)\big|O((t\ln t)^{-1}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{01a}(t_{1})-\mu_{01b}(t_{1})|+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|{\partial}_{t}\mu_{01a}(t_{1})-{\partial}_{t}\mu_{01b}(t_{1})|)\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\bar{\mu}_{01a}-\bar{\mu}_{01b}\|_{01}t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-3}\ln\ln t\lesssim(\ln t_{0})^{-1}\|\bar{\mu}_{01a}-\bar{\mu}_{01b}\|_{01}t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}\ln\ln t

by the estimate of
\varphi[\mu_{0}+\mu_{01a}]-\varphi[\mu_{0}+\mu_{01b}]
in Corollary
2.3
.

Due to the choice of
\nu_{1}
and
t_{0}
above, the contraction property is achieved. By contraction mapping theorem, there exists a unique solution
\mu_{01}\in B_{01}
for (
2.21
).

From now on,
\nu_{1}
will be regarded as a general constant unless otherwise stated. For notational simplicity,
{\partial}_{t}
is denoted by “
~{}^{\prime}~
”.
Once we have solved
\mu_{01}
, the regularity of
\mu_{01}
can be improved by the equation of
\mu_{01}
and
\mu_{01}^{\prime\prime}
decays to
0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
. For the purpose of finding a better decay estimate of
\mu_{01}^{\prime\prime}
, we take derivative on both sides of (
2.20
). Then

\mu_{01}^{\prime\prime}+(\beta_{\nu_{1}}(t))^{\prime}\mu_{01}+\beta_{\nu_{1}}(t)\mu_{01}^{\prime}=(f_{\nu_{1}}[\mu_{01}])^{\prime},

where we can evaluate

|(\beta_{\nu_{1}}(t))^{\prime}\mu_{01}+\beta_{\nu_{1}}(t)\mu_{01}^{\prime}|\lesssim t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-3}\ln\ln t,

\displaystyle\big(\chi(t)[(1-\nu_{1})\ln t+2\ln\ln t]^{-1}\big)^{\prime}\bigg(-\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu_{1}}}\frac{\mu_{01t}(s)}{t-s}ds-\tilde{g}[\mu_{01},\mu_{0}]

\displaystyle+O((t\ln t)^{-1}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{01}(t_{1})|+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{01t}(t_{1})|)+O((t\ln t)^{-2})-\mathcal{M}[\mu_{0}]\bigg)\lesssim t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-3}\ln\ln t,

\chi(t)\big[(1-\nu_{1})\ln t+2\ln\ln t\big]^{-1}\big(-\tilde{g}[\mu_{01},\mu_{0}]+O((t\ln t)^{-2})-\mathcal{M}[\mu_{0}]\big)^{\prime}\lesssim t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-3}\ln\ln t,

where we used similar calculation in (
C.2
) for
(\tilde{g}[\mu_{01},\mu_{0}])^{\prime}
.

\displaystyle\chi(t)\big[(1-\nu_{1})\ln t+2\ln\ln t\big]^{-1}\Big(-\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu_{1}}}\frac{\mu_{01t}(s)}{t-s}ds\Big)^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\chi(t)\big[(1-\nu_{1})\ln t+2\ln\ln t\big]^{-1}\Big[-\frac{\mu_{01}^{\prime}(t-t^{1-\nu_{1}})}{t^{1-\nu_{1}}}(1-(1-\nu_{1})t^{-\nu_{1}})+\frac{\mu_{01}^{\prime}(\frac{t}{2})}{t}+\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu_{1}}}\frac{\mu_{01}^{\prime}(s)}{(t-s)^{2}}ds)\Big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\chi(t)\big[(1-\nu_{1})\ln t+2\ln\ln t\big]^{-1}\Big[(1-\nu_{1})\frac{\mu_{01}^{\prime}(t-t^{1-\nu_{1}})}{t}-\frac{\mu_{01}^{\prime}(\frac{t}{2})}{t}-\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu_{1}}}\frac{\mu_{01}^{\prime\prime}(s)}{t-s}ds\Big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\chi(t)\big[(1-\nu_{1})\ln t+2\ln\ln t\big]^{-1}\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu_{1}}}\frac{\mu_{01}^{\prime\prime}(s)}{t-s}ds+O(t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-4}\ln\ln t).

Revisiting the process of proving Corollary
2.3
, we have

(2.24)

\displaystyle\chi(t)\big[(1-\nu_{1})\ln t+2\ln\ln t\big]^{-1}\big(O((t\ln t)^{-1}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{01}(t_{1})|+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{01t}(t_{1})|)\big)^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\chi(t)\big[(1-\nu_{1})\ln t+2\ln\ln t\big]^{-1}O\big(\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{01}^{\prime\prime}(t_{1})|\big)+O(t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-4}\ln\ln t).

Using the estimates above, we have

\mu_{01}^{\prime\prime}=\chi(t)\big[(1-\nu_{1})\ln t+2\ln\ln t\big]^{-1}\Big(-\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu_{1}}}\frac{\mu_{01}^{\prime\prime}(s)}{t-s}ds+O(\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{01}^{\prime\prime}(t_{1})|)\Big)+\tilde{f}(t)

where
|\tilde{f}(t)|\leq C_{1}t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-3}\ln\ln t
.
For this reason, we solve
\mu_{01}^{\prime\prime}
in the following space

B_{2,2}=\{g\in C(t_{0}/4,\infty),g(t)\rightarrow 0\mbox{ \ as \ }t\rightarrow\infty\ :\ \|g\|_{2,2}\leq 2C_{1}\},

where for
g\in C(t_{0}/4,\infty)
, we define

\|g\|_{a,b}:=\sup\limits_{t\geq t_{0}/4}t^{a}(\ln t)^{b}|g(t)|.

For any
g_{1},g_{2}\in B_{2,2}
, similar to (
2.22
), we have

\displaystyle\chi(t)\big[(1-\nu_{1})\ln t+2\ln\ln t\big]^{-1}\bigg|\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu_{1}}}\frac{g_{1}(s)}{t-s}ds-\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu_{1}}}\frac{g_{2}(s)}{t-s}ds\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\nu_{1})^{-1}(\ln t)^{-1}\|g_{1}-g_{2}\|_{2,2}\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu_{1}}}\frac{s^{-2}(\ln s)^{-2}}{t-s}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\nu_{1}(1-\nu_{1})^{-1}\|g_{1}-g_{2}\|_{2,2}t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-2}(1+(\ln t)^{-1})(1+(\nu_{1}\ln t)^{-1}).

Similar to (
2.24
), one has

\displaystyle\chi(t)\big[(1-\nu_{1})\ln t+2\ln\ln t\big]^{-1}\Big|O(\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|g_{1}(t_{1})|)-O(\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|g_{2}(t_{1})|)\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\chi(t)\big[(1-\nu_{1})\ln t+2\ln\ln t\big]^{-1}O\big(\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|g_{1}(t_{1})-g_{2}(t_{1})|\big)\lesssim t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-3}\|g_{1}-g_{2}\|_{2,2}.

For any
g\in B_{2,2}
, we have

\displaystyle\Big|\chi(t)[(1-\nu_{1})\ln t+2\ln\ln t]^{-1}\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu_{1}}}\frac{g(s)}{t-s}ds\Big|\leq(1-\nu_{1})^{-1}(\ln t)^{-1}\|g\|_{2,2}\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu_{1}}}\frac{s^{-2}(\ln s)^{-2}}{t-s}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\nu_{1}(1-\nu_{1})^{-1}\|g\|_{2,2}t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-2}(1+(\ln t)^{-1})(1+(\nu_{1}\ln t)^{-1}).

Since
\nu_{1}\in(0,\frac{1}{4})
, when
t_{0}
is large enough, the contraction property follows and then
\mu_{01}^{\prime\prime}\in B_{2,2}
. Thus the improved error is given by

\mathcal{M}[\mu_{0}+\mu_{01}]=\mathcal{E}_{\nu_{1}}[\mu_{01}]=\int_{t-t^{1-\nu_{1}}}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}(t)}\frac{\mu_{01}^{\prime}(s)-\mu_{01}^{\prime}(t)}{t-s}ds=O(t^{-1-\nu_{1}}(\ln t)^{-2}).

Step 3. Further improvement by iteration.

Repeating Step 2 finitely many times, we can find
\mu_{0i}
,
i=1,\dots,k_{0}
such that

(2.25)

M\Big[\mu_{0}+\sum\limits_{i=1}^{k_{0}}\mu_{0i}\Big]=O(t^{-2}).

Denote

\bar{\mu}_{0}=\mu_{0}+\sum\limits_{i=1}^{k_{0}}\mu_{0i}.

From the construction above, we see that
\bar{\mu}_{0}\sim\mu_{0}=(\ln t)^{-1}
,
\bar{\mu}_{0t}\sim\mu_{0t}
.

Since
\bar{\mu}_{0}
is determined, we are now able to describe
\Phi_{0}
rigorously. Set
\bar{y}=\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}}
and consider

\Delta_{\bar{y}}\Phi_{0}+3w^{2}(\bar{y})\Phi_{0}=\tilde{H}(|\bar{y}|,t),

where

\tilde{H}(|\bar{y}|,t)=-3\bar{\mu}_{0}\left(w^{2}(\bar{y})\eta^{2}(\frac{\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y}}{\sqrt{t}})\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}](\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y},t)+\bar{\mu}_{0}w(\bar{y})\eta(\frac{\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y}}{\sqrt{t}})\varphi^{2}[\bar{\mu}_{0}](\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y},t)\right)+3\bar{\mu}_{0}\mathcal{M}[\bar{\mu}_{0}]\frac{\eta(\bar{y})Z_{5}(\bar{y})}{\int_{B_{2}}\eta(z)Z_{5}^{2}(z)dz}.

Then
\Phi_{0}(\bar{y},t)
is given by

\Phi_{0}(\bar{y},t)=\tilde{Z}_{5}(\bar{y})\int_{0}^{|\bar{y}|}\tilde{H}(s,t)Z_{5}(s)s^{3}ds-Z_{5}(\bar{y})\int_{0}^{|\bar{y}|}\tilde{H}(s,t)\tilde{Z}_{5}(s)s^{3}ds,

where
\tilde{Z}_{5}(r)
is the other linearly independent kernel of the homogeneous equation, which satisfies that the Wronskian
W[Z_{5},\tilde{Z}_{5}]=r^{-3}
, so
\tilde{Z}_{5}(r)\sim r^{-2}
if
r\rightarrow 0
and
\tilde{Z}_{5}(r)\sim 1
if
r\rightarrow\infty
.

By the definition of
\mathcal{M}[\bar{\mu}_{0}]
, it is easy to have

(2.26)

\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}\tilde{H}(z,t)Z_{5}(z)dz=0.

By Corollary
2.3
,
|\tilde{H}|\lesssim t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-4}
.
Due to the special choice of
\bar{\mu}_{0}
, one can get better time decay for
\tilde{H}
. Indeed, we have

\displaystyle w^{2}(\bar{y})\eta^{2}(\frac{\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y}}{\sqrt{t}})\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}](\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y},t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle w^{2}(\bar{y})\eta^{2}(\frac{\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y}}{\sqrt{t}})\bigg[-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\bar{\mu}_{0}t^{-1}+\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\frac{\bar{\mu}_{0t}(s)}{t-s}ds\Big)+O(\bar{\mu}_{0}^{3}t^{-2}|\bar{y}|^{2}+|\bar{\mu}_{0t}||\bar{y}|)+O(t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2})\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big[O(t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}|\bar{y}|)+O(t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}\ln\ln t)\Big]\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-4}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{y}|\leq 2\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}\ln\ln t)\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-3}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{y}|\leq 2\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}},

\bar{\mu}_{0}w(\bar{y})\eta(\frac{\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y}}{\sqrt{t}})\varphi^{2}[\bar{\mu}_{0}](\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y},t)=O(t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-3})\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{y}|\leq 2\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}=O(t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}\ln\ln t)\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-3}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{y}|\leq 2\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}},

which implies
|\tilde{H}|\lesssim t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-3}\ln\ln t\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-3}
. As a result, one has

(2.27)

|\tilde{H}|\lesssim\min\big\{t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-4},t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-3}\ln\ln t\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-3}\big\}.

Claim:

(2.28)

\displaystyle|\Phi_{0}(\bar{y},t)|\lesssim

\displaystyle\min\big\{t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-2}\ln(2+|\bar{y}|),t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-3}\ln\ln t\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-1}\big\},

\displaystyle|\nabla_{\bar{y}}\Phi_{0}(\bar{y},t)|\lesssim

\displaystyle\min\big\{t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-3}\ln(2+|\bar{y}|),t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-3}\ln\ln t\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-2}\big\},

\displaystyle|{\partial}_{t}\Phi_{0}(\bar{y},t)|\lesssim t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-1}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-2}\ln(2+|\bar{y}|).
