Indeed, the estimate about
\Phi_{0}(\bar{y},t)
is derived from (
2.26
) (
2.27
). The upper bound of
\nabla_{\bar{y}}\Phi_{0}(\bar{y},t)
follows by scaling argument. In order to estimate
{\partial}_{t}\Phi_{0}(\bar{y},t)
, we need to take a closer look at
{\partial}_{t}\tilde{H}(z,t)
. By the definition of
\tilde{H}
, it is straightforward to have

\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}{\partial}_{t}\tilde{H}(z,t)Z_{5}(z)dz=0.

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\partial}_{t}\tilde{H}(|\bar{y}|,t)=-3\bar{\mu}_{0t}\bigg(w^{2}(\bar{y})\eta^{2}(\frac{\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y}}{\sqrt{t}})\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}](\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y},t)+\bar{\mu}_{0}w(\bar{y})\eta(\frac{\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y}}{\sqrt{t}})\varphi^{2}[\bar{\mu}_{0}](\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y},t)-\mathcal{M}[\bar{\mu}_{0}]\frac{\eta(\bar{y})Z_{5}(\bar{y})}{\int_{B_{2}}\eta Z_{5}^{2}dz}\bigg)

\displaystyle-3\bar{\mu}_{0}\Bigg[w^{2}(\bar{y})2\eta(\frac{\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y}}{\sqrt{t}})\nabla\eta(\frac{\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y}}{\sqrt{t}})\cdot\frac{\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y}}{\sqrt{t}}(\frac{\bar{\mu}_{0}}{\sqrt{t}})^{-1}{\partial}_{t}(\frac{\bar{\mu}_{0}}{\sqrt{t}})\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}](\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y},t)

\displaystyle+w^{2}(\bar{y})\eta^{2}(\frac{\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y}}{\sqrt{t}})\big(\nabla_{\bar{x}}\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}](\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y},t)\cdot\bar{\mu}_{0t}\bar{y}+{\partial}_{t}\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}](\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y},t)\big)

\displaystyle+\bar{\mu}_{0t}w(\bar{y})\eta(\frac{\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y}}{\sqrt{t}})\varphi^{2}[\bar{\mu}_{0}](\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y},t)+\bar{\mu}_{0}w(\bar{y})\nabla\eta(\frac{\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y}}{\sqrt{t}})\cdot\frac{\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y}}{\sqrt{t}}(\frac{\bar{\mu}_{0}}{\sqrt{t}})^{-1}{\partial}_{t}(\frac{\bar{\mu}_{0}}{\sqrt{t}})\varphi^{2}[\bar{\mu}_{0}](\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y},t)

\displaystyle+\bar{\mu}_{0}w(\bar{y})\eta(\frac{\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y}}{\sqrt{t}})2\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}](\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y},t)\big(\nabla_{\bar{x}}\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}](\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y},t)\cdot\bar{\mu}_{0t}\bar{y}+{\partial}_{t}\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}](\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y},t)\big)-{\partial}_{t}(\mathcal{M}[\bar{\mu}_{0}])\frac{\eta(\bar{y})Z_{5}(\bar{y})}{\int_{B_{2}}\eta Z_{5}^{2}dz}\Bigg].

Using (
C.1
) in Appendix, one has

\displaystyle\Big|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}](\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y},t)\cdot\bar{\mu}_{0t}\bar{y}+{\partial}_{t}\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}](\bar{\mu}_{0}\bar{y},t)\Big|\mathbf{1}_{\{\bar{\mu}_{0}|\bar{y}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big|\Big[(t\ln t)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq\mu_{0}\}}+(t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}|\bar{x}|^{-1}+t^{-\frac{3}{2}}(\ln t)^{-1})\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>\mu_{0}\}}\Big]\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}|\bar{\mu}_{0t}||\bar{x}|+t^{-2}\Big|\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}\lesssim t^{-2}.

Thus

\displaystyle|{\partial}_{t}\tilde{H}(|\bar{y}|,t)|\lesssim t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-3}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-4}+(\ln t)^{-1}\Big[t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-1}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-4}+t^{-2}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-4}+t^{-3}(\ln t)^{-4}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-2}

\displaystyle+t^{-3}(\ln t)^{-3}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-2}+t^{-3}(\ln t)^{-2}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-2}+t^{-3}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{y}|\leq 3\}}\Big]\mathbf{1}_{\{\bar{\mu}_{0}|\bar{y}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-3}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-4}+(\ln t)^{-1}\Big(t^{-2}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-4}+t^{-3}(\ln t)^{-2}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-2}\Big)\mathbf{1}_{\{\bar{\mu}_{0}|\bar{y}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}\lesssim t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-1}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-4}.

Therefore, we have the estimate about
{\partial}_{t}\Phi_{0}(\bar{y},t)
in (
2.28
).

In order to avoid the influence in the remote region
|\bar{x}|\gtrsim t^{\frac{1}{2}}
, we add cut-off function and set
\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4(x-\xi)}{\sqrt{t}})
as the correction term. It is easy to check

{\partial}_{t}\Big(\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\Big)=-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}\bar{\mu}_{0t}\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}\nabla_{\bar{y}}\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\cdot\big(\bar{\mu}_{0t}\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}}+\xi_{t}\big)+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}{\partial}_{t}\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t).

Set
\mu=\bar{\mu}_{0}+\mu_{1}
where
|\mu_{1}|\leq\frac{\bar{\mu}_{0}}{2}
,
|\mu_{1t}|\leq\frac{|\bar{\mu}_{0t}|}{2}
. Let us estimate the new error

(2.29)

\displaystyle S\Big[u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4(x-\xi)}{\sqrt{t}})\Big]

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle-{\partial}_{t}\Big(\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4(x-\xi)}{\sqrt{t}})\Big)+\Delta_{x}\Big(\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4(x-\xi)}{\sqrt{t}})\Big)+S[u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]]

\displaystyle+\Big(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4(x-\xi)}{\sqrt{t}})\Big)^{3}-(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu])^{3}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\nabla\eta(\frac{4(x-\xi)}{\sqrt{t}})\cdot\big(4t^{-\frac{1}{2}}\xi_{t}+2t^{-\frac{3}{2}}(x-\xi)\big)

\displaystyle-\eta(\frac{4(x-\xi)}{\sqrt{t}})\Big(-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}\bar{\mu}_{0t}\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}\nabla_{\bar{y}}\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\cdot(\bar{\mu}_{0t}\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}}+\xi_{t})+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}{\partial}_{t}\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\Big)

\displaystyle+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-3}\Delta_{\bar{y}}\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4(x-\xi)}{\sqrt{t}})+8t^{-\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}\nabla_{\bar{y}}\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\cdot\nabla\eta(\frac{4(x-\xi)}{\sqrt{t}})

\displaystyle+16t^{-1}\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\Delta\eta(\frac{4(x-\xi)}{\sqrt{t}})+S[u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]]+3\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-3}w^{2}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4(x-\xi)}{\sqrt{t}})

\displaystyle+\Big(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4(x-\xi)}{\sqrt{t}})\Big)^{3}-(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu])^{3}-3\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-3}w^{2}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4(x-\xi)}{\sqrt{t}})

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\nabla\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\cdot\big(4t^{-\frac{1}{2}}\xi_{t}+2t^{-\frac{3}{2}}\bar{x}\big)

\displaystyle+8t^{-\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}\nabla_{\bar{y}}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\cdot\nabla\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})+16t^{-1}\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\Delta\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})

\displaystyle-\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\Big[-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}\bar{\mu}_{0t}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}\nabla_{\bar{y}}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\cdot(\bar{\mu}_{0t}\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}}+\xi_{t})+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}{\partial}_{t}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\Big]

\displaystyle+3\Big(\eta^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})-\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\Big)\mu^{-2}w^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu})\varphi[\mu](\bar{x},t)+3\Big(\eta(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})-\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\Big)\mu^{-1}w(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu})\varphi^{2}[\mu](\bar{x},t)

\displaystyle+3\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\Big(\mu^{-2}w^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu})\varphi[\mu](\bar{x},t)-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}w^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}](\bar{x},t)\Big)

\displaystyle+3\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\Big(\mu^{-1}w(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu})\varphi^{2}[\mu](\bar{x},t)-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}w(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\varphi^{2}[\bar{\mu}_{0}](\bar{x},t)\Big)

\displaystyle+\xi_{t}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}\varphi[\mu](\bar{x},t)+\mu^{-2}\xi_{t}\cdot\nabla w(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu})\eta(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})+\mu^{-1}t^{-\frac{1}{2}}w(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu})\xi_{t}\cdot\nabla\eta(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})

\displaystyle+\varphi^{3}[\mu]+3\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}\mathcal{M}[\bar{\mu}_{0}]\big(\int_{B_{2}}\eta(z)Z_{5}^{2}(z)dz\big)^{-1}\eta(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})

\displaystyle+\Big(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\Big)^{3}-(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu])^{3}-3\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-3}w^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\Phi_{0}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}}).

Claim:

(2.30)

\displaystyle\bigg|S\Big[u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4(x-\xi)}{\sqrt{t}})\Big]\bigg|\lesssim t^{-2}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-2}\ln(2+|\bar{y}|)\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|\leq 9t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}

\displaystyle+\bigg[t^{-1}(\ln t)^{2}|\mu_{1}|+(\ln t)^{2}\Big(|\tilde{g}[\bar{\mu}_{0},\mu_{1}]|+|\bar{\mu}_{0t}|\ln t\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|}{\bar{\mu}_{0}(t)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|}{|\bar{\mu}_{0t}(t)|}\Big)\Big)\bigg]\langle y\rangle^{-4}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|\leq 9t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}

\displaystyle+|\xi_{t}|(\ln t)^{2}\langle y\rangle^{-3}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+\Big[|\xi_{t}|t^{\frac{3}{2}}(\ln t)^{-1}|x|^{-6}+(t^{2}(\ln t)^{-1}|x|^{-6})^{3}\Big]\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|>2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+t^{-2}(\ln t)^{2}\eta(\bar{y}).

We need to estimate term by term. Indeed, by (
2.28
), one has

\displaystyle\Big|\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\nabla\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\cdot(4t^{-\frac{1}{2}}\xi_{t}+2t^{-\frac{3}{2}}\bar{x})\Big|\lesssim(t\ln t)^{-1}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-2}\ln(2+|\bar{y}|)(t^{-\frac{1}{2}}|\xi_{t}|+t^{-1})\mathbf{1}_{\{9^{-1}t^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq|x|\leq 9t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle\left[t^{-\frac{5}{2}}(\ln t)^{-2}|\xi_{t}|+t^{-3}(\ln t)^{-2}\right]\mathbf{1}_{\{9^{-1}t^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq|x|\leq 9t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}.

Also, we have

\displaystyle\Big|8t^{-\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}\nabla_{\bar{y}}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\cdot\nabla\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})+16t^{-1}\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\Delta\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big|t^{-\frac{3}{2}}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-3}\ln(2+|\bar{y}|)+t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-1}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-2}\ln(2+|\bar{y}|)\Big|\mathbf{1}_{\{9^{-1}t^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq|x|\leq 9t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}\sim t^{-3}(\ln t)^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{9^{-1}t^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq|x|\leq 9t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}},

\displaystyle\Big|\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}\bar{\mu}_{0t}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}\nabla_{\bar{y}}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\cdot(\bar{\mu}_{0t}\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}}+\xi_{t})-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}{\partial}_{t}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\Big|\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big[(t\ln t)^{-2}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-2}\ln(2+|\bar{y}|)+t^{-1}|\xi_{t}|\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-3}\ln(2+|\bar{y}|)+t^{-2}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-2}\ln(2+|\bar{y}|)\Big]\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle\Big(t^{-1}|\xi_{t}|\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-3}\ln(2+|\bar{y}|)+t^{-2}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-2}\ln(2+|\bar{y}|)\Big)\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}}).

By Corollary
2.3
, we have the following estimates

\displaystyle\bigg|3\big(\eta^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})-\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\big)\mu^{-2}w^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu})\varphi[\mu](\bar{x},t)+3\big(\eta(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})-\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\big)\mu^{-1}w(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu})\varphi^{2}[\mu](\bar{x},t)\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big[(\ln t)^{2}\langle y\rangle^{-4}(t\ln t)^{-1}+\ln t\langle y\rangle^{-2}(t\ln t)^{-2}\Big]\mathbf{1}_{\{9^{-1}t^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq|\bar{x}|\leq 9t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}\sim(t\ln t)^{-3}\mathbf{1}_{\{9^{-1}t^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq|\bar{x}|\leq 9t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}},

\displaystyle\bigg|\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})(\mu^{-2}w^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu})\varphi[\mu](\bar{x},t)-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}w^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}](\bar{x},t))\bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg|\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\Big[\big(\mu^{-2}w^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu})-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}w^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\big)\varphi[\mu]+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}w^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})(\varphi[\mu]-\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}])\Big]\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\bigg[t^{-1}(\ln t)^{2}|\mu_{1}|\langle y\rangle^{-4}+(\ln t)^{2}\langle y\rangle^{-4}\Big(|\tilde{g}[\bar{\mu}_{0},\mu_{1}]|+(t\ln t)^{-1}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|}{\bar{\mu}_{0}(t)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|}{|\bar{\mu}_{0t}(t)|}\Big)\Big)\bigg],

\displaystyle\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\Big|\big(\mu^{-1}w(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu})\varphi^{2}[\mu](\bar{x},t)-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}w(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\varphi^{2}[\bar{\mu}_{0}](\bar{x},t)\big)\Big|

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\Big|\big(\mu^{-1}w(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu})-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}w(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\big)\varphi^{2}[\mu]+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}w(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})(\varphi[\mu]-\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}])(\varphi[\mu]+\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}])\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\bigg[\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}|\mu_{1}|\langle y\rangle^{-2}(t\ln t)^{-2}+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\langle y\rangle^{-2}(t\ln t)^{-1}\Big(|\mu_{1}|t^{-1}+|\tilde{g}[\bar{\mu}_{0},\mu_{1}]|

\displaystyle+|\mu_{t}|\ln t\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|}{\mu(t)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|}{|\mu_{t}(t)|}\Big)\Big)\bigg]

\displaystyle\sim\

\displaystyle\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\bigg[t^{-2}|\mu_{1}|\langle y\rangle^{-2}+t^{-1}\langle y\rangle^{-2}\Big(|\tilde{g}[\bar{\mu}_{0},\mu_{1}]|+(t\ln t)^{-1}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|}{\mu(t)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|}{|\mu_{t}(t)|}\Big)\Big)\bigg]

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\bigg[t^{-1}(\ln t)^{2}|\mu_{1}|\langle y\rangle^{-4}+(\ln t)^{2}\langle y\rangle^{-4}\Big(|\tilde{g}[\bar{\mu}_{0},\mu_{1}]|+(t\ln t)^{-1}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|}{\mu(t)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|}{|\mu_{t}(t)|}\Big)\Big)\bigg].

Using Corollary
2.3
, one has

\displaystyle\Big|\xi_{t}\cdot\nabla_{\bar{x}}\varphi[\mu](\bar{x},t)+\mu^{-2}\xi_{t}\cdot\nabla w(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu})\eta(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})+\mu^{-1}t^{-\frac{1}{2}}w(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu})\xi_{t}\cdot\nabla\eta(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|\xi_{t}|\Big(t^{-1}\langle y\rangle^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+t^{\frac{3}{2}}(\ln t)^{-1}|\bar{x}|^{-6}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+(\ln t)^{2}\langle y\rangle^{-3}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}\Big)

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle|\xi_{t}|\Big((\ln t)^{2}\langle y\rangle^{-3}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+t^{\frac{3}{2}}(\ln t)^{-1}|x|^{-6}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|>2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}\Big).

|\varphi^{3}[\mu]|\lesssim(t\ln t)^{-3}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+(t^{2}(\ln t)^{-1}|\bar{x}|^{-6})^{3}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}},\ \Big|\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}\mathcal{M}[\bar{\mu}_{0}]\frac{\eta(\bar{y})Z_{5}(\bar{y})}{\int_{B_{2}}\eta(z)Z_{5}^{2}(z)dz}\Big|\lesssim t^{-2}(\ln t)^{2}\eta(\bar{y}).

\displaystyle\bigg|\Big(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\Big)^{3}-(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu])^{3}-3\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-3}w^{2}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\Phi_{0}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 3\bigg|\Big(\mu^{-1}w(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu})-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}w(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})+\varphi[\mu]+\theta\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\Big)

\displaystyle\times\Big(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]+\theta\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}w(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\Big)\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(|\mu_{1}|\mu_{0}^{-2}\langle y\rangle^{-2}+(t\ln t)^{-1}+t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-1}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-2}\ln(2+|\bar{y}|)\Big)

\displaystyle\times\big((t\ln t)^{-1}+\ln t\langle y\rangle^{-2}\big)(t\ln t)^{-1}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-2}\ln(2+|\bar{y}|)\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\big(|\mu_{1}|(\ln t)^{2}\langle y\rangle^{-2}+(t\ln t)^{-1}\big)t^{-1}\langle y\rangle^{-4}\ln(2+|y|)\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|\mu_{1}|t^{-1}(\ln t)^{2}\langle y\rangle^{-6}\ln(2+|y|)\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})+t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-1}\langle y\rangle^{-4}\ln(2+|y|)\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}}).

We have completed the proof of claim (
2.30
).

3.
Gluing system and solving the outer problem

In this section, we formulate the inner–outer gluing system such that an infinite time blow-up solution
to (
2.1
) with desired asymptotics can be found. We look for solution of the form

u(x,t)=u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4(x-\xi)}{\sqrt{t}})+\psi(x,t)+\eta_{R}\mu^{-1}\phi(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu},t)

with

\eta_{R}(x,t)=\eta(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu_{0}R(t)}),\quad R(t)=t^{\gamma},\quad 0<\gamma<\frac{1}{2},

where
\psi
,
\phi
are perturbations in the outer region and inner region, respectively. In order for the following to hold

\displaystyle 0=S\Big[u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4(x-\xi)}{\sqrt{t}})+\psi+\eta_{R}\mu^{-1}\phi(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu},t)\Big]

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle-{\partial}_{t}\psi-{\partial}_{t}\eta_{R}\mu^{-1}\phi(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu},t)+\eta_{R}\mu^{-2}\mu_{t}\big(\phi(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu},t)+\frac{x-\xi}{\mu}\cdot\nabla_{y}\phi(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu},t)\big)

\displaystyle+\eta_{R}\mu^{-2}\xi_{t}\cdot\nabla_{y}\phi(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu},t)-\eta_{R}\mu^{-1}{\partial}_{t}\phi(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu},t)

\displaystyle+\Delta_{x}\psi+\Delta_{x}\eta_{R}\mu^{-1}\phi(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu},t)+2\nabla_{x}\eta_{R}\cdot\mu^{-2}\nabla_{y}\phi(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu},t)+\eta_{R}\mu^{-3}\Delta_{y}\phi(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu},t)

\displaystyle+3\big(\mu^{-1}w(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu})\big)^{2}\big(\psi+\eta_{R}\mu^{-1}\phi(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu},t)\big)+S\big[u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4(x-\xi)}{\sqrt{t}})\big]

\displaystyle+\Big(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4(x-\xi)}{\sqrt{t}})+\psi+\eta_{R}\mu^{-1}\phi(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu},t)\Big)^{3}

\displaystyle-\Big(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4(x-\xi)}{\sqrt{t}})\Big)^{3}-3\big(\mu^{-1}w(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu})\big)^{2}\big(\psi+\eta_{R}\mu^{-1}\phi(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu},t)\big),

it suffices to solving the following inner-outer gluing system for
(\psi,\phi)
.

The outer problem for
\psi
:

{\partial}_{t}\psi=\Delta_{x}\psi+\mathcal{G}[\psi,\phi,\mu_{1},\xi]\mbox{ \ \ in \ }{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}\times(t_{0},\infty),

where

(3.1)

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}[\psi,\phi,\mu_{1},\xi]:=3\mu^{-2}w^{2}(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu})\psi(1-\eta_{R})+\eta_{R}\mu^{-2}\xi_{t}\cdot\nabla_{y}\phi(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu},t)

\displaystyle+\Delta_{x}\eta_{R}\mu^{-1}\phi(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu},t)+2\nabla_{x}\eta_{R}\cdot\mu^{-2}\nabla_{y}\phi(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu},t)-{\partial}_{t}\eta_{R}\mu^{-1}\phi(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu},t)

\displaystyle+(1-\eta_{R})S\big[u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4(x-\xi)}{\sqrt{t}})\big]

\displaystyle+\Big(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4(x-\xi)}{\sqrt{t}})+\psi+\eta_{R}\mu^{-1}\phi(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu},t)\Big)^{3}

\displaystyle-\Big(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4(x-\xi)}{\sqrt{t}})\Big)^{3}-3\big(\mu^{-1}w(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu})\big)^{2}\big(\psi+\eta_{R}\mu^{-1}\phi(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu},t)\big)

\displaystyle-\bigg[3\big(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]-\mu^{-1}w(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu})\big)\big(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]+\mu^{-1}w(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu})\big)

\displaystyle+6(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu])\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4(x-\xi)}{\sqrt{t}})\bigg]\eta_{R}\mu^{-1}\phi(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu},t).

The inner problem for
\phi
:

(3.2)

\displaystyle\mu^{2}{\partial}_{t}\phi(y,t)=\Delta_{y}\phi(y,t)+3w^{2}(y)\phi(y,t)+f_{1}(y,t)\phi(y,t)+f_{2}(t)y\cdot\nabla_{y}\phi(y,t)+\mathcal{H}[\psi,\mu_{1},\xi](y,t)\mbox{ \ \ in \ }{\mathcal{D}}_{4R}

where
y=\frac{x-\xi}{\mu}
,
\mathcal{D}_{4R}=\{(y,t)\ :\ y\in B_{4R(t)},\ t\in(t_{0},\infty)\}
, and

\displaystyle f_{1}(y,t)=\mu\mu_{t}+\mu^{2}\Big[3\big(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]-\mu^{-1}w(y)\big)\big(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]+\mu^{-1}w(y)\big)+6(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu])\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\Big],\ f_{2}(t)=\mu\mu_{t},

(3.3)

\mathcal{H}[\psi,\mu_{1},\xi](y,t):=\mu^{3}\Big(3\mu^{-2}w^{2}(y)\psi(\mu y+\xi,t)+S\big[u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4\mu y}{\sqrt{t}})\big]\Big).

By (
2.30
), one has

(3.4)

\displaystyle\big|\mathcal{H}[\psi,\mu_{1},\xi](y,t)\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\ln t)^{-1}\langle y\rangle^{-4}|\psi(\mu y+\xi,t)|+t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-3}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-2}\ln(2+|\bar{y}|)

\displaystyle+(t\ln t)^{-1}|\mu_{1}|\langle y\rangle^{-4}+(\ln t)^{-1}\Big(|\tilde{g}[\bar{\mu}_{0},\mu_{1}]|+|\bar{\mu}_{0t}|\ln t\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|}{\bar{\mu}_{0}(t)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|}{|\bar{\mu}_{0t}(t)|}\Big)\Big)\langle y\rangle^{-4}

\displaystyle+|\xi_{t}|(\ln t)^{-1}\langle y\rangle^{-3}+t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-1}\eta(\bar{y})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\ln t)^{-1}\langle y\rangle^{-4}|\psi(\mu y+\xi,t)|+t^{a_{1}\gamma-2}(\ln t)^{-2}\langle y\rangle^{-2-a_{1}}

\displaystyle+(t\ln t)^{-1}|\mu_{1}|\langle y\rangle^{-4}+(\ln t)^{-1}\Big(|\tilde{g}[\bar{\mu}_{0},\mu_{1}]|+|\bar{\mu}_{0t}|\ln t\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|}{\bar{\mu}_{0}(t)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|}{|\bar{\mu}_{0t}(t)|}\Big)\Big)\langle y\rangle^{-4}

\displaystyle+|\xi_{t}|(\ln t)^{-1}\langle y\rangle^{-3}

where we have used
|y|\leq 4R=4t^{\gamma}
, and for later purpose, we require that

(3.5)

a_{1}\gamma-2<5\delta-\kappa-a\gamma,\ 0<a_{1}\leq 1.

Here above constants are those which measure the weighted topology for the inner problem (see (
3.8
)).
Notice in
{\mathcal{D}}_{4R}
, we have

\displaystyle|f_{1}(y,t)|+|f_{2}(t)|\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-3}+\Big[(t\ln t)^{-1}\ln t\langle y\rangle^{-2}+\ln t\langle y\rangle^{-2}t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-1}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-2}\ln(2+|\bar{y}|)\Big](\ln t)^{-2}

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-3}+t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}\langle y\rangle^{-2}.

Remark 3.0.1
.

Due to the time decay rate of
f_{1}(y,t)
,
f_{2}(t)
, we are forced to put
f_{1}(y,t)\phi(y,t)+f_{2}(t)y\cdot\nabla_{y}\phi(y,t)
in the linear part of the inner problem. We can not put this term in the right hand side of the outer problem since this will influence the Hölder continuity of
\psi
about
t
variable. Besides, we can not use the inner linear theory in
[
5
]
since
f_{1}(y,t)\phi(y,t)+f_{2}(t)y\cdot\nabla_{y}\phi(y,t)
will influence the Hölder about
\mu_{1t}
through the orthogonal equation, which will result in failure to choose suitable topology for solving the inner–outer gluing system. Instead, we rebuild a new inner linear theory in Section
7
to avoid including
f_{1}(y,t)\phi(y,t)+f_{2}(t)y\cdot\nabla_{y}\phi(y,t)
in the orthogonal equation about
\mu_{1}
.

We decompose the inner problem (
3.2
) into two parts. Set
\phi=\phi_{1}+\phi_{2}
, then it suffices to consider

(3.6)

\displaystyle\mu^{2}{\partial}_{t}\phi_{1}(y,t)=\Delta_{y}\phi_{1}(y,t)+3w^{2}(y)\phi_{1}(y,t)+f_{1}(y,t)\phi_{1}(y,t)+f_{2}(t)y\cdot\nabla_{y}\phi_{1}(y,t)+\mathcal{H}[\psi,\mu_{1},\xi](y,t)

\displaystyle+\big(\int_{B_{2}}\eta(z)Z_{5}^{2}(z)dz\big)^{-1}\Big(-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}3\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}w^{2}(z)Z_{5}(z)dz+O((t\ln t)^{-1})\Big)\mu\mathcal{E}_{\nu}[\mu_{1}]\eta(y)Z_{5}(y)\quad\mbox{ \ in \ }{\mathcal{D}}_{4R},

(3.7)

\displaystyle\mu^{2}{\partial}_{t}\phi_{2}(y,t)=\Delta_{y}\phi_{2}(y,t)+3w^{2}(y)\phi_{2}(y,t)+f_{1}(y,t)\phi_{2}(y,t)+f_{2}(t)y\cdot\nabla_{y}\phi_{2}(y,t)

\displaystyle-\big(\int_{B_{2}}\eta(z)Z_{5}^{2}(z)dz\big)^{-1}\Big(-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}3\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}w^{2}(z)Z_{5}(z)dz+O((t\ln t)^{-1})\Big)\mu\mathcal{E}_{\nu}[\mu_{1}]\eta(y)Z_{5}(y)\quad\mbox{ \ in \ }{\mathcal{D}}_{4R},

where
R_{0}(\tau)=\tau^{\delta}
with
\delta>0
very small and

\mathcal{E}_{\nu}[\mu_{1}]=\int_{t-t^{1-\nu}}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}(t)}\frac{\mu_{1t}(s)-\mu_{1t}(t)}{t-s}ds.

Set

\tau(t)=\int_{t_{0}}^{t}\mu^{-2}(s)ds+t_{0}(\ln t_{0})^{2},\quad\tau_{0}=t_{0}(\ln t_{0})^{2}.

Then
\tau(t)\sim t(\ln t)^{2}
for all
t\geq t_{0}
. In
\tau
variable,
\mathcal{D}_{4R}=\{(y,\tau)\ :\ y\in B_{4R(t(\tau))},\ t\in(\tau_{0},\infty)\}
.
It is easy to rewrite (
3.6
) and (
3.7
) in the form as in Proposition
7.1
and Lemma
7.5
, respectively.

The reason for decomposing the inner problem into above two parts is that the orthogonal equation involving
\mu_{1}
is too difficult to solve. More detailed explanations will be given in Section
4.1
.

Before stating the solvability of the outer problem, let us first fix the inner solution
\phi
to the inner problem, the next order of scaling parameter
\mu_{1}
and translating parameter
\xi
in the spaces with the following norms

(3.8)

\|\phi\|_{i,\kappa-5\delta,a}:=\sup\limits_{(y,\tau)\in{\mathcal{D}}_{4R}}\tau^{\kappa-5\delta}\langle y\rangle^{a}\big(\langle y\rangle|\nabla\phi(y,t(\tau))|+|\phi(y,t(\tau))|\big)

where
\kappa
,
a
are some positive constants to be determined later.

For
\mu_{1}(t)\in C^{1}(\frac{t_{0}}{4},\infty)
,
\mu_{1}(t)\rightarrow 0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
, denote

(3.9)

\|\mu_{1}\|_{*1}:=\sup\limits_{t\geq t_{0}/4}\big[\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R(t)^{-a}\big]^{-1}|\mu_{1t}|.

For
\xi(t)=(\xi_{1}(t),\dots,\xi_{4}(t))\in C^{1}(t_{0},\infty)
,
\xi(t)\rightarrow 0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
, denote

(3.10)

\|\xi\|_{*2}:=\max\limits_{1\leq j\leq 4}\sup\limits_{t\geq t_{0}}\big[(\ln t)^{2}(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R(t)^{-a}\big]^{-1}|\xi_{jt}|.

The outer problem is solved in the following Proposition.

Proposition 3.1
.

Consider

(3.11)

{\partial}_{t}\psi(x,t)=\Delta\psi(x,t)+\mathcal{G}[\psi,\phi,\mu_{1},\xi]\mbox{ \ in \ }{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}\times(t_{0},\infty),\quad\psi(x,t_{0})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}

where
\mathcal{G}[\psi,\phi,\mu_{1},\xi]
is given in (
3.1
). Assume
\phi
,
\mu_{1}
,
\xi
satisfy
\|\phi\|_{i,\kappa-5\delta,a},\|\mu_{1}\|_{*1},\|\xi\|_{*2}<\Lambda_{1}
where
\Lambda_{1}>1
is a constant and the parameters satisfy

(3.12)

5\delta-\kappa-a\gamma>-2,\ 5\delta-\kappa<-1,\ 0<a<2,\ 0<\gamma<\frac{1}{2},

then
there exists a solution
\psi=\psi[\phi,\mu_{1},\xi]
with the following estimates:

\displaystyle|\psi(x,t)|\leq

\displaystyle C(\Lambda_{1})\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}\left(\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+t|x|^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}\right),

\displaystyle|\nabla\psi(x,t)|\leq

\displaystyle C(\Lambda_{1})\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a},

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{s_{1},s_{2}\in(t-\frac{\lambda^{2}(t)}{4},t)}\frac{|\psi(x,s_{1})-\psi(x,s_{2})|}{|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{\alpha}}\leq C(\Lambda_{1},\alpha)\bigg\{\lambda^{-2\alpha}(t)\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}

\displaystyle+\lambda^{2-2\alpha}(t)\left[(\mu_{0}R)^{-2}\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}+(\ln t)^{3}(t(\ln t)^{2})^{10\delta-2\kappa}\right]\bigg\}

where
0<\lambda(t)\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}
.

The proof is postponed to Section
B
.

4.
Orthogonal equations for
\mu_{1}
,
\xi

4.1.
Solving
\mu_{1}
and
\xi

In order to utilize Proposition
7.1
with
R_{0}=\tau^{\delta}\sim(t(\ln t)^{2})^{\delta}
where
\delta>0
is small,
one needs to adjust
\mu_{1}
,
\xi
such that
c_{i}[\mathcal{H}]=0
,
i=1,\dots,5
in Proposition
7.1
with
\mathcal{H}
given in (
3.3
).

However, for
i=5
, it is too difficult to solve
c_{5}[\mathcal{H}]=0
thoroughly. We are only able to make

c_{5}[\mathcal{H}]\approx 0
and leave smaller remainder to be solved by the non-orthogonal linear theory of the inner problem.

In this section, we only care about the estimate in
|y|\leq 4R
since this is served for the inner problem. Set

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{i}[\psi,\mu_{1},\xi]=

\displaystyle\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}\mathcal{H}[\psi,\mu_{1},\xi](y,t)Z_{i}(y)dy,\quad i=1,\dots,5,

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{5}(|y|,t)=

\displaystyle\int_{S^{3}}\mathcal{H}[\psi,\mu_{1},\xi](|y|\theta,t)\Upsilon_{0}(\theta)d\theta,\ \mathcal{H}_{i}(|y|,t)=\int_{S^{3}}\mathcal{H}[\psi,\mu_{1},\xi](|y|\theta,t)\Upsilon_{i}(\theta)d\theta,\quad i=1,\dots,4

where
\Upsilon_{i}
are spherical harmonic functions, which are given in Section
7
.

Using (
2.29
), for
i=5
, since
Z_{5}
is radially symmetric, one has

\displaystyle\quad\mathcal{M}_{5}[\psi,\mu_{1},\xi]=\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}3\mu w^{2}(y)\psi(\mu y+\xi,t)Z_{5}(y)dy

\displaystyle+\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}\mu^{3}\Big(\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}\bar{\mu}_{0t}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}\nabla_{\bar{y}}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\cdot\bar{\mu}_{0t}\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}}-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}{\partial}_{t}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\Big)Z_{5}(y)dy

\displaystyle+\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}3\mu^{3}\Big(\mu^{-2}w^{2}(y)\varphi[\mu](\mu y,t)-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}w^{2}(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}](\mu y,t)\Big)Z_{5}(y)dy

\displaystyle+\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}3\mu^{3}\Big(\mu^{-1}w(y)\varphi^{2}[\mu](\mu y,t)-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}w(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\varphi^{2}[\bar{\mu}_{0}](\mu y,t)\Big)Z_{5}(y)dy

\displaystyle+\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}\mu^{3}\varphi^{3}[\mu](\mu y,t)Z_{5}(y)dy+\int_{B_{4R}}3\mu^{3}\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}\mathcal{M}[\bar{\mu}_{0}]\frac{\eta(\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\mu y)Z_{5}(\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\mu y)}{\int_{B_{2}}\eta(z)Z_{5}^{2}(z)dz}Z_{5}(y)dy

\displaystyle+\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}\mu^{3}\bigg[\big(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4\mu y}{\sqrt{t}})\big)^{3}-(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu])^{3}-3\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-3}w^{2}(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\Phi_{0}(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4\mu y}{\sqrt{t}})\bigg]Z_{5}(y)dy,

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{5}(|y|,t)=\int_{S^{3}}3\mu w^{2}(|y|\theta)\psi(\mu|y|\theta+\xi,t)\Upsilon_{0}(\theta)d\theta

\displaystyle+\int_{S^{3}}\mu^{3}\Big(\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}\bar{\mu}_{0t}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\mu|y|\theta}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}\nabla_{\bar{y}}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\mu|y|\theta}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\cdot\bar{\mu}_{0t}\frac{\mu|y|\theta}{\bar{\mu}_{0}}-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}{\partial}_{t}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\mu|y|\theta}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\Big)\Upsilon_{0}(\theta)d\theta

\displaystyle+\int_{S^{3}}3\mu^{3}\Big(\mu^{-2}w^{2}(|y|\theta)\varphi[\mu](\mu|y|\theta,t)-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}w^{2}(\frac{\mu|y|\theta}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}](\mu|y|\theta,t)\Big)\Upsilon_{0}(\theta)d\theta

\displaystyle+\int_{S^{3}}3\mu^{3}\Big(\mu^{-1}w(|y|\theta)\varphi^{2}[\mu](\mu|y|\theta,t)-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}w(\frac{\mu|y|\theta}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\varphi^{2}[\bar{\mu}_{0}](\mu|y|\theta,t)\Big)\Upsilon_{0}(\theta)d\theta

\displaystyle+\int_{S^{3}}\mu^{3}\varphi^{3}[\mu](\mu|y|\theta,t)\Upsilon_{0}(\theta)d\theta+\int_{S^{3}}3\mu^{3}\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}\mathcal{M}[\bar{\mu}_{0}]\frac{\eta(\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\mu|y|\theta)Z_{5}(\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\mu|y|\theta)}{\int_{B_{2}}\eta(z)Z_{5}^{2}(z)dz}\Upsilon_{0}(\theta)d\theta

\displaystyle+\int_{S^{3}}\mu^{3}\bigg[\big(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\mu|y|\theta}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4\mu|y|\theta}{\sqrt{t}})\big)^{3}-(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu])^{3}

\displaystyle-3\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-3}w^{2}(\frac{\mu|y|\theta}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\Phi_{0}(\frac{\mu|y|\theta}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4\mu|y|\theta}{\sqrt{t}})\bigg]\Upsilon_{0}(\theta)d\theta.

For
i=1,\dots,4
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{i}[\psi,\mu_{1},\xi]=\

\displaystyle\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}3\mu w^{2}(y)\psi(\mu y+\xi,t)Z_{i}(y)dy+\xi_{it}\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}\mu^{3}\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}{\partial}_{\bar{y}_{i}}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)Z_{i}(y)dy

\displaystyle+\xi_{it}\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}\Big(\mu^{3}{\partial}_{\bar{x}_{i}}\varphi(\mu y,t)Z_{i}(y)+\mu Z_{i}^{2}(y)\Big)dy

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}3\mu w^{2}(y)\psi(\mu y+\xi,t)Z_{i}(y)dy+\mu\xi_{it}\Big(\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}Z_{i}^{2}(y)dy+O(t^{-\frac{1}{2}})\Big)

by Corollary
2.3
and (
2.28
). Also,

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{i}(|y|,t)=

\displaystyle\int_{S^{3}}3\mu w^{2}(|y|\theta)\psi(\mu|y|\theta+\xi,t)\Upsilon_{i}(\theta)d\theta+\xi_{it}\int_{S^{3}}\mu^{3}\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}{\partial}_{\bar{y}_{i}}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\mu|y|\theta}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\Upsilon_{i}(\theta)d\theta

\displaystyle+\xi_{it}\int_{S^{3}}\mu^{3}\Big({\partial}_{\bar{x}_{i}}\varphi(\mu|y|\theta,t)+\mu^{-2}{\partial}_{z_{i}}w(|y|\theta)\eta(\frac{\mu|y|\theta}{\sqrt{t}})\Big)\Upsilon_{i}(\theta)d\theta.

Using similar calculations as in (
2.30
), one has

\displaystyle|\mathcal{H}_{5}(|y|,t)|\lesssim(\ln t)^{-1}\langle y\rangle^{-3}\sup\limits_{z\in B_{4R(t)}}\langle z\rangle^{-1}|\psi(\mu z+\xi,t)|+(\ln t)^{-3}\bigg\{t^{-2}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-2}\ln(2+|\bar{y}|)

\displaystyle+\Big[t^{-1}(\ln t)^{2}|\mu_{1}|+(\ln t)^{2}\Big(|\tilde{g}[\bar{\mu}_{0},\mu_{1}]|+|\bar{\mu}_{0t}|\ln t\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|}{\bar{\mu}_{0}(t)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|}{|\bar{\mu}_{0t}(t)|}\Big)\Big)\Big]\langle y\rangle^{-4}+t^{-2}(\ln t)^{2}\mathbf{1}_{\{|y|\leq 4\}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\ln t)^{-1}\langle y\rangle^{-3}\sup\limits_{z\in B_{4R(t)}}\langle z\rangle^{-1}|\psi(\mu z+\xi,t)|+t^{a_{1}\gamma-2}(\ln t)^{-2}\langle y\rangle^{-2-a_{1}}

\displaystyle+\Big[(t\ln t)^{-1}|\mu_{1}|+(\ln t)^{-1}\Big(|\tilde{g}[\bar{\mu}_{0},\mu_{1}]|+|\bar{\mu}_{0t}|\ln t\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|}{\bar{\mu}_{0}(t)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|}{|\bar{\mu}_{0t}(t)|}\Big)\Big)\Big]\langle y\rangle^{-4}

where
a_{1}>0
is chosen such that
a_{1}\gamma-2<5\delta-\kappa-a\gamma
. It then follows that

|\mathcal{H}_{i}(|y|,t)|\lesssim(\ln t)^{-1}\langle y\rangle^{-3}\sup\limits_{z\in B_{4R(t)}}\langle z\rangle^{-1}|\psi(\mu z+\xi,t)|+|\xi_{it}|(\ln t)^{2}\langle y\rangle^{-3},\quad i=1,\dots 4.

By Proposition
7.1
, the orthogonal equation
c_{i}[\mathcal{H}][\tau]=0

(i=1,\dots,4)
is equivalent to solving

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{i}[\psi,\mu_{1},\xi]+(t(\ln t)^{2})^{-\delta\epsilon_{0}}O\big(\sup\limits_{y\in B_{4R(t)}}\langle y\rangle^{3}|\mathcal{H}_{i}(y,t)|\big)=\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}3\mu w^{2}(y)\psi(\mu y+\xi,t)Z_{i}(y)dy

\displaystyle+\mu\xi_{it}\big(\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}Z_{i}^{2}(y)dy+O(t^{-\frac{1}{2}})\big)+(t(\ln t)^{2})^{-\delta\epsilon_{0}}O\Big((\ln t)^{-1}\sup\limits_{z\in B_{4R(t)}}\langle z\rangle^{-1}|\psi(\mu z+\xi,t)|+|\xi_{it}|(\ln t)^{2}\Big)=0

where
\epsilon_{0}>0
is given in Proposition
7.1
. One can write above equation as

(4.1)

\xi_{it}=\Pi_{i}[\mu_{1},\xi]

where

\displaystyle\Pi_{i}[\mu_{1},\xi]=

\displaystyle\big(\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}Z_{i}^{2}(y)dy+O(t(\ln t)^{2})^{-\frac{\delta\epsilon_{0}}{2}}\big)^{-1}\bigg[-\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}3w^{2}(y)\psi(\mu y+\xi,t)Z_{i}(y)dy

\displaystyle-(t(\ln t)^{2})^{-\delta\epsilon_{0}}O(\sup\limits_{z\in B_{4R(t)}}\langle z\rangle^{-1}|\psi(\mu z+\xi,t)|)\bigg].

Let us estimate
\mathcal{M}_{5}
term by term. By (
2.28
), one has

\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}\mu^{3}\Big[\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}\bar{\mu}_{0t}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}\nabla_{\bar{y}}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\cdot\bar{\mu}_{0t}\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}}-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}{\partial}_{t}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\Big]Z_{5}(y)dy=O(t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-1}).

By Corollary
2.3
and the special choice of
\bar{\mu}_{0}
, we have for
|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}]=

\displaystyle-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\bar{\mu}_{0}t^{-1}+\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\frac{\bar{\mu}_{0t}(s)}{t-s}ds\Big)+O\Big(\bar{\mu}_{0}t^{-2}|\bar{x}|^{2}+|\bar{\mu}_{0t}|\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\bar{\mu}_{0}}\Big)+O(t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}\ln\ln t)+O\Big(\bar{\mu}_{0}t^{-2}|\bar{x}|^{2}+|\bar{\mu}_{0t}|\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\bar{\mu}_{0}}\Big).

Notice that

\displaystyle\mu^{-2}w^{2}(y)-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}w^{2}(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})=

\displaystyle-2\mu_{1}(\theta\mu+(1-\theta)\bar{\mu}_{0})^{-3}(w^{2}(y_{\theta})+w(y_{\theta})\nabla w(y_{\theta})\cdot y_{\theta})\Big|_{y_{\theta}=\frac{x-\xi}{\theta\mu+(1-\theta)\bar{\mu}_{0}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2\mu_{1}\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-3}(w^{2}(y)+w(y)\nabla w(y)\cdot y)+O(\mu_{1}^{2}\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-4}\langle y\rangle^{-4}).

Then by Corollary
2.3
, it follows that

\displaystyle\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}3\mu^{3}\Big(\mu^{-2}w^{2}(y)\varphi[\mu](\mu y,t)-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}w^{2}(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}](\mu y,t)\Big)Z_{5}(y)dy

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle 3\mu^{3}\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}\Big[\mu^{-2}w^{2}(y)(\varphi[\mu]-\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}])+\big(\mu^{-2}w^{2}(y)-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}w^{2}(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\big)\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}]\Big]Z_{5}(y)dy

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle 3\mu^{3}\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}\Bigg\{\mu^{-2}w^{2}(y)Z_{5}(y)\bigg[-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\mu_{1}t^{-1}+\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{1t}(s)}{t-s}ds\Big)

\displaystyle+O\Big(|\mu_{1}|\bar{\mu}_{0}^{2}t^{-2}\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}^{2}}+|\bar{\mu}_{0t}|\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|}{\bar{\mu}_{0}}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|}{|\bar{\mu}_{0t}|}\Big)\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\bar{\mu}_{0}}\Big)+\tilde{g}[\bar{\mu}_{0},\mu_{1}]\bigg]

\displaystyle+\Big[-2\mu_{1}\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-3}(w^{2}(y)+w(y)\nabla w(y)\cdot y)+O(\mu_{1}^{2}\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-4}\langle y\rangle^{-4})\Big]Z_{5}(y)

\displaystyle\times\Big(O(t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}\ln\ln t)+O(\bar{\mu}_{0}t^{-2}|\bar{x}|^{2}+|\bar{\mu}_{0t}|\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\Big)\Bigg\}dy

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\mu\bigg[-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}3\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}w^{2}(y)Z_{5}(y)dy\Big(\mu_{1}t^{-1}+\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{1t}(s)}{t-s}ds\Big)

\displaystyle+O\Big((t\ln t)^{-1}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|\Big)+\tilde{g}[\bar{\mu}_{0},\mu_{1}]+O(|\mu_{1}|t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-1}\ln\ln t)\bigg].

By Corollary
2.3
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}3\mu^{3}\Big(\mu^{-1}w(y)\varphi^{2}[\mu](\mu y,t)-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}w(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\varphi^{2}[\bar{\mu}_{0}](\mu y,t)\Big)Z_{5}(y)dy

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle 3\mu^{3}\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}\bigg[\mu^{-1}w(y)(\varphi[\mu]-\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}])(\varphi[\mu]+\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}])+\big(\mu^{-1}w(y)-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}w(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\big)\varphi^{2}[\bar{\mu}_{0}]\bigg]Z_{5}(y)dy

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle 3\mu^{3}\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}\Bigg\{\mu^{-1}w(y)Z_{5}(y)\bigg[-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\mu_{1}t^{-1}+\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{1t}(s)}{t-s}ds\Big)

\displaystyle+O\Big(|\mu_{1}|\bar{\mu}_{0}^{2}t^{-2}\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}^{2}}+|\bar{\mu}_{0t}|\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|}{\bar{\mu}_{0}}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|}{|\bar{\mu}_{0t}|}\Big)\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\bar{\mu}_{0}}\Big)+\tilde{g}[\bar{\mu}_{0},\mu_{1}]\bigg](t\ln t)^{-1}

\displaystyle+(-\mu_{1}\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}(w(y)+y\cdot\nabla w(y))+O(\mu_{1}^{2}\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-3})\langle y\rangle^{-2})Z_{5}(y)(t\ln t)^{-2}\Bigg\}dy

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\mu^{3}\Bigg\{\bigg[\Big(\mu_{1}t^{-1}+\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{1t}(s)}{t-s}ds\Big)O(\ln t)+O\Big(|\mu_{1}|\bar{\mu}_{0}^{2}t^{-2}R_{0}^{2}+|\bar{\mu}_{0t}|\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\Big(\frac{|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|}{\bar{\mu}_{0}}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|}{|\bar{\mu}_{0t}|}\Big)R_{0}\Big)

\displaystyle+O(\ln t)\tilde{g}[\bar{\mu}_{0},\mu_{1}]\bigg]O(t^{-1})+\mu_{1}\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}O(t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-1})\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\mu\Bigg[\Big(\mu_{1}t^{-1}+\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{1t}(s)}{t-s}ds\Big)O((t\ln t)^{-1})

\displaystyle+O\Big(t^{-\frac{3}{2}}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|+t^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|\Big)+O((t\ln t)^{-1})\tilde{g}[\bar{\mu}_{0},\mu_{1}]\Bigg]

since
\delta>0
is very small and

\mu^{-1}w(y)-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}w(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})=-\mu_{1}\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}(w(y)+y\cdot\nabla w(y))+O(\mu_{1}^{2}\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-3})\langle y\rangle^{-2}
. Similarly, the following estimates hold

\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}\mu^{3}\varphi^{3}[\mu](\mu y,t)Z_{5}(y)dy=O(\mu^{3}(t\ln t)^{-3}R_{0}^{2})=O(t^{-\frac{5}{2}})

when
\delta
is small enough.

\bigg|\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}\mu^{3}\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}\mathcal{M}[\bar{\mu}_{0}]\frac{\eta(\bar{y})Z_{5}(\bar{y})}{\int_{B_{2}}\eta(z)Z_{5}^{2}(z)dz}Z_{5}(y)dy\bigg|\lesssim O(t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-1}),

\displaystyle\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}\mu^{3}\bigg[\big(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4\mu y}{\sqrt{t}})\big)^{3}-(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu])^{3}-3\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-3}w^{2}(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\Phi_{0}(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4\mu y}{\sqrt{t}})\bigg]Z_{5}(y)dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}\mu^{3}\bigg[\big(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\big)^{3}-(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu])^{3}-3\mu^{-2}w^{2}(y)\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)

\displaystyle+3\big(\mu^{-2}w^{2}(y)-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}w^{2}(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\big)\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\bigg]Z_{5}(y)dy=\mu O(|\mu_{1}|(t\ln t)^{-1}\ln\ln t)+O(t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-4})

since by (
2.28
),

\displaystyle\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}3\mu^{3}\Big[(\mu^{-1}w(y))^{2}-(\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}w(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}}))^{2}\Big]\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)Z_{5}(y)dy

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle 3\mu^{3}\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}\Big[-2\mu_{1}\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-3}(w^{2}(y)+w(y)\nabla w(y)\cdot y)+O(\mu_{1}^{2}\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-4}\langle y\rangle^{-4})\Big]O(t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-3}\ln\ln t\langle y\rangle^{-1})Z_{5}(y)dy

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\mu O(|\mu_{1}|(t\ln t)^{-1}\ln\ln t),

\displaystyle\Bigg|\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}\mu^{3}\bigg[\big(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\big)^{3}-(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu])^{3}-3\mu^{-2}w^{2}(y)\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\bigg]Z_{5}(y)dy\Bigg|

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\Bigg|\mu^{3}\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}\Bigg[3(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu])\big(\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\big)^{2}+\big(\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\big)^{3}

\displaystyle+3(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]-\mu^{-1}w(y))(u_{1}+\varphi[\mu]+\mu^{-1}w(y))\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{\mu y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\Bigg]Z_{5}(y)dy\Bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\mu(\ln t)^{-2}\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}\Bigg[(\ln t\langle y\rangle^{-2}+(t\ln t)^{-1})((t\ln t)^{-1}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-2}\ln(2+|\bar{y}|))^{2}+((t\ln t)^{-1}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-2}\ln(2+|\bar{y}|))^{3}

\displaystyle+\big(\ln t\langle y\rangle^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\geq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+(t\ln t)^{-1}\big)(\ln t\langle y\rangle^{-2}+(t\ln t)^{-1})(t\ln t)^{-1}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-2}\ln(2+|\bar{y}|)\Bigg]\langle y\rangle^{-2}dy

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\mu(\ln t)^{-2}\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}\Bigg[\ln t\langle y\rangle^{-2}((t\ln t)^{-1}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-2}\ln(2+|\bar{y}|))^{2}+((t\ln t)^{-1}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-2}\ln(2+|\bar{y}|))^{3}

\displaystyle+(t\ln t)^{-1}\ln t\langle y\rangle^{-2}(t\ln t)^{-1}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-2}\ln(2+|\bar{y}|)\Bigg]\langle y\rangle^{-2}dy\lesssim t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-4}.

Finally, we get

\displaystyle\quad\mathcal{M}_{5}[\psi,\mu_{1},\xi]=\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}3\mu w^{2}(y)\psi(\mu y+\xi,t)Z_{5}(y)dy+O(t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-1})

\displaystyle+\mu\Bigg[-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}3\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}w^{2}(y)Z_{5}(y)dy\bigg(\mu_{1}t^{-1}+\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{1t}(s)}{t-s}ds\bigg)

\displaystyle+O\big((t\ln t)^{-1}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|\big)+\tilde{g}[\bar{\mu}_{0},\mu_{1}]+O(|\mu_{1}|t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-1}\ln\ln t)\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\mu\Bigg[\bigg(\mu_{1}t^{-1}+\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{1t}(s)}{t-s}ds\bigg)O((t\ln t)^{-1})

\displaystyle+O\big(t^{-\frac{3}{2}}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|+t^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|\big)+O((t\ln t)^{-1})\tilde{g}[\bar{\mu}_{0},\mu_{1}]\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\mu O(|\mu_{1}|(t\ln t)^{-1}\ln\ln t)

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\mu\Bigg\{\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}3w^{2}(y)\psi(\mu y+\xi,t)Z_{5}(y)dy+O(t^{-2})

\displaystyle+O\big((t\ln t)^{-1}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|\big)+O(\tilde{g}[\bar{\mu}_{0},\mu_{1}])+O(|\mu_{1}|(t\ln t)^{-1}\ln\ln t)

\displaystyle+\bigg(-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}3\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}w^{2}(y)Z_{5}(y)dy+O((t\ln t)^{-1})\bigg)\bigg(\mu_{1}t^{-1}+\int_{t/2}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{1t}(s)}{t-s}ds\bigg)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\mu\big(-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}3\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}w^{2}(y)Z_{5}(y)dy+O((t\ln t)^{-1})\big)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{\big(-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}3\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}w^{2}(y)Z_{5}(y)dy+O((t\ln t)^{-1})\big)^{-1}\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}3w^{2}(y)\psi(\mu y+\xi,t)Z_{5}(y)dy

\displaystyle+O(t^{-2})+O\big((t\ln t)^{-1}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|\big)+O(\tilde{g}[\bar{\mu}_{0},\mu_{1}])

\displaystyle+\mu_{1}t^{-1}\big(1+O((\ln t)^{-1}\ln\ln t)\big)+\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu}}\frac{\mu_{1t}(s)}{t-s}ds+\mu_{1t}\big((1-\nu)\ln t+2\ln\ln t\big)+\mathcal{E}_{\nu}[\mu_{1}]\Bigg\}

where

\mathcal{E}_{\nu}[\mu_{1}]=\int_{t-t^{1-\nu}}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}(t)}\frac{\mu_{1t}(s)-\mu_{1t}(t)}{t-s}ds.

By Proposition
7.1
,
c_{5}[\mathcal{H}]=0
is equivalent to

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{5}[\psi,\mu_{1},\xi]+(t(\ln t)^{2})^{-\delta\epsilon_{0}}O\big(\sup\limits_{y\in B_{4R(t)}}\langle y\rangle^{2+a_{1}}|\mathcal{H}_{5}(y,t)|\big)

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\mu\big(-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}3\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}w^{2}(y)Z_{5}(y)dy+O((t\ln t)^{-1})\big)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{\big(-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}3\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}w^{2}(y)Z_{5}(y)dy+O((t\ln t)^{-1})\big)^{-1}\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}3w^{2}(y)\psi(\mu y+\xi,t)Z_{5}(y)dy

\displaystyle+O(t^{-2})+O((t\ln t)^{-1}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|)+O(\tilde{g}[\bar{\mu}_{0},\mu_{1}])

\displaystyle+\mu_{1}t^{-1}(1+O((\ln t)^{-1}\ln\ln t))+\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu}}\frac{\mu_{1t}(s)}{t-s}ds+\mu_{1t}((1-\nu)\ln t+2\ln\ln t)+\mathcal{E}_{\nu}[\mu_{1}]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+(t(\ln t)^{2})^{-\delta\epsilon_{0}}O\Bigg((\ln t)^{-1}\sup\limits_{y\in B_{4R(t)}}\langle y\rangle^{-1}|\psi(\mu y+\xi,t)|+t^{a_{1}\gamma-2}(\ln t)^{-2}

\displaystyle+(t\ln t)^{-1}|\mu_{1}|+(\ln t)^{-1}\left(|\tilde{g}[\bar{\mu}_{0},\mu_{1}]|+|\bar{\mu}_{0t}|\ln t\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\bigg(\frac{|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|}{\bar{\mu}_{0}(t)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|}{|\bar{\mu}_{0t}(t)|}\bigg)\right)\Bigg)

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\mu\big(-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}3\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}w^{2}(y)Z_{5}(y)dy+O((t\ln t)^{-1})\big)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{\big(-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}3\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}w^{2}(y)Z_{5}(y)dy+O((t\ln t)^{-1})\big)^{-1}\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}3w^{2}(y)\psi(\mu y+\xi,t)Z_{5}(y)dy

\displaystyle+O(t^{-2})+O((t\ln t)^{-1}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|)+O(\tilde{g}[\bar{\mu}_{0},\mu_{1}])

\displaystyle+\mu_{1}t^{-1}(1+O((\ln t)^{-1}\ln\ln t))+\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu}}\frac{\mu_{1t}(s)}{t-s}ds+\mu_{1t}((1-\nu)\ln t+2\ln\ln t)+\mathcal{E}_{\nu}[\mu_{1}]

\displaystyle+(t(\ln t)^{2})^{-\delta\epsilon_{0}}O(\sup\limits_{y\in B_{4R(t)}}\langle y\rangle^{-1}|\psi(\mu y+\xi,t)|+t^{a_{1}\gamma-2}(\ln t)^{-1})\Bigg\}=0

where we have used similar calculations as in (
3.4
), and

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{y\in B_{4R(t)}}\langle y\rangle^{2+a_{1}}|\mathcal{H}_{5}(y,t)|\lesssim(\ln t)^{-1}\sup\limits_{y\in B_{4R(t)}}\langle y\rangle^{-1}|\psi(\mu y+\xi,t)|+t^{a_{1}\gamma-2}(\ln t)^{-2}

\displaystyle+(t\ln t)^{-1}|\mu_{1}|+(\ln t)^{-1}\left(|\tilde{g}[\bar{\mu}_{0},\mu_{1}]|+|\bar{\mu}_{0t}|\ln t\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}\bigg(\frac{|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|}{\bar{\mu}_{0}(t)}+\frac{|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|}{|\bar{\mu}_{0t}(t)|}\bigg)\right).

Similar to the methodology in Section
2.3
, we leave
\mathcal{E}_{\nu}[\mu_{1}]
as the remainder term and consider the following equation about
\mu_{1}
.

\displaystyle\big(-2^{-\frac{1}{2}}3\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}w^{2}(y)Z_{5}(y)dy+O((t\ln t)^{-1})\big)^{-1}\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}3w^{2}(y)\psi(\mu y+\xi,t)Z_{5}(y)dy

\displaystyle+O\big((t\ln t)^{-1}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|\big)+O(\tilde{g}[\bar{\mu}_{0},\mu_{1}])

\displaystyle+\mu_{1}t^{-1}(1+O((\ln t)^{-1}\ln\ln t))+\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu}}\frac{\mu_{1t}(s)}{t-s}ds+\mu_{1t}((1-\nu)\ln t+2\ln\ln t)

\displaystyle+(t(\ln t)^{2})^{-\delta\epsilon_{0}}O(\sup\limits_{y\in B_{4R(t)}}\langle y\rangle^{-1}|\psi(\mu y+\xi,t)|)+(t(\ln t)^{2})^{-\delta\epsilon_{0}}O(t^{a_{1}\gamma-2}(\ln t)^{-1})=0

when
a_{1}\gamma>\delta\epsilon_{0}
.
That is,

(4.2)

\mu_{1t}+\beta_{v}(t)\mu_{1}=\Pi_{5}[\mu_{1},\xi]

where

\beta_{\nu}(t)=t^{-1}\left[(1-\nu)\ln t+2\ln\ln t\right]^{-1}(1+O((\ln t)^{-1}\ln\ln t)),

(4.3)

\displaystyle\Pi_{5}[\mu_{1},\xi]=\chi(t)((1-\nu)\ln t+2\ln\ln t)^{-1}\Bigg[-\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu}}\frac{\mu_{1t}(s)}{t-s}ds-O((t\ln t)^{-1}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|)

\displaystyle-O(\tilde{g}[\bar{\mu}_{0},\mu_{1}])+\big(2^{-\frac{1}{2}}3\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}w^{2}(y)Z_{5}(y)dy+O((t\ln t)^{-1})\big)^{-1}\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}3w^{2}(y)\psi(\mu y+\xi,t)Z_{5}(y)dy

\displaystyle-(t(\ln t)^{2})^{-\delta\epsilon_{0}}O(\sup\limits_{y\in B_{4R(t)}}\langle y\rangle^{-1}|\psi(\mu y+\xi,t)|)-(t(\ln t)^{2})^{-\delta\epsilon_{0}}O(t^{a_{1}\gamma-2}(\ln t)^{-1})\Bigg].

Similar to (
2.21
), in order to solve (
4.2
) and (
4.1
), it is sufficient to consider the following fixed point problem:

(4.4)

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{5}[\mu_{1},\xi](t)=

\displaystyle\Pi_{5}[\mu_{1},\xi](t)+\beta_{\nu}(t)e^{-\int^{t}\beta_{\nu}(u)du}\int_{t}^{\infty}e^{\int^{s}\beta_{\nu}(u)du}\Pi_{5}[\mu_{1},\xi](s)ds,

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{i}[\mu_{1},\xi](t)=

\displaystyle\Pi_{i}[\mu_{1},\xi],\quad i=1,\dots 4.

Notice that
|\psi|\lesssim\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}
and
recall the norms (
3.9
), (
3.10
) for
\mu_{1}
,
\xi
. We will solve (
4.4
) in the following spaces

(4.5)

B_{\mu_{1}}=\{\mu_{1}\in C^{1}(t_{0}/4,\infty)\ :\ \|\mu_{1}\|_{*1}\leq 2\},\quad B_{\xi}=\{\xi\in C^{1}(t_{0},\infty)\ :\ \|\xi\|_{*2}\leq 2\}.

For any
\mu_{1a},\mu_{1b}\in B_{\mu_{1}}
and
\xi_{a},\xi_{b}\in B_{\xi}
, similar to (
2.22
), one has

\displaystyle\chi(t)\bigg|\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu}}\frac{\mu_{1at}(s)-\mu_{1bt}(s)}{t-s}ds\bigg|\leq\|\mu_{1a}-\mu_{1b}\|_{*1}\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu}}\frac{\ln s(s(\ln s)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}(s)}{t-s}ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|\mu_{1a}-\mu_{1b}\|_{*1}(1+O((\ln t)^{-1}))\nu(\ln t)^{2}(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}.

By gradient estimate in Proposition
3.1
, we have

\displaystyle\bigg|\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}w^{2}(y)(\psi(\mu_{1a}y+\xi_{a},t)-\psi(\mu_{1b}y+\xi_{b},t))Z_{5}(y)dy\bigg|\leq C\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}(|\mu_{1a}-\mu_{1b}|+|\xi_{a}-\xi_{b}|)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Ct\ln t[\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}]^{2}(\|\mu_{1a}-\mu_{1b}\|_{*1}+\|\xi_{a}-\xi_{b}\|_{*2}).

The estimate for
\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}3w^{2}(y)\psi(\mu y+\xi,t)Z_{i}(y)dy
is the same.

\displaystyle(t(\ln t)^{2})^{-\delta\epsilon_{0}}\bigg|O(\sup\limits_{y\in B_{4R(t)}}\langle y\rangle^{-1}|\psi(\mu_{1a}y+\xi_{a},t)|)-O(\sup\limits_{y\in B_{4R(t)}}\langle y\rangle^{-1}|\psi(\mu_{1b}y+\xi_{b},t)|)\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(t(\ln t)^{2})^{-\delta\epsilon_{0}}\bigg|O(\sup\limits_{y\in B_{4R(t)}}\langle y\rangle^{-1}|\psi(\mu_{1a}y+\xi_{a},t)-\psi(\mu_{1b}y+\xi_{b},t)|)\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(t(\ln t)^{2})^{-\delta\epsilon_{0}}t\ln t[\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}]^{2}(\|\mu_{1a}-\mu_{1b}\|_{*1}+\|\xi_{a}-\xi_{b}\|_{*2})

since
O(\sup\limits_{y\in B_{4R(t)}}\langle y\rangle^{-1}|\psi(\mu y+\xi,t)|)
depends on
\psi
linearly.

From the same calculations as in (
B.1
), one has

\chi(t)|\tilde{g}[\bar{\mu}_{0},\mu_{1a}]-\tilde{g}[\bar{\mu}_{0},\mu_{1b}]|\leq C\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}\|\mu_{1a}-\mu_{1b}\|_{*1}

when
5\delta-\kappa-a\gamma>-2
. Similar to (
2.23
), one has

\displaystyle\chi(t)\Big|O((t\ln t)^{-1}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1a}(t_{1})|+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1at}(t_{1})|)-O((t\ln t)^{-1}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1b}(t_{1})|+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1bt}(t_{1})|)\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\chi(t)O\big((t\ln t)^{-1}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1a}(t_{1})-\mu_{1b}(t_{1})|+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1at}(t_{1})-\mu_{1bt}(t_{1})|\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}\|\mu_{1a}-\mu_{1b}\|_{*1}.

In conclusion, under the following restrictions

(4.6)

a_{1}>0,\ a_{1}\gamma-2<5\delta-\kappa-a\gamma,\ a_{1}\gamma>\delta\epsilon_{0},\ 5\delta-\kappa-a\gamma>-2,\ 0<\nu<\frac{1}{2},

for
t_{0}
is sufficiently large,
(\mathcal{S}_{5},\mathcal{S}_{i})
is a contraction mapping in
B_{\mu_{1}}\times B_{\xi}
.

Similarly, for
(\mu_{1},\xi)\in B_{\mu_{1}}\times B_{\xi}
, we have

\chi(t)\Big|\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu}}\frac{\mu_{1t}(s)}{t-s}ds\Big|\leq\|\mu_{1}\|_{*1}(1+O((\ln t)^{-1}))v(\ln t)^{2}(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a},

|\tilde{g}[\bar{\mu}_{0},\mu_{1}]|\leq C\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}\|\mu_{1}\|_{*1},

\chi(t)\Big|O((t\ln t)^{-1}\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1}(t_{1})|+\sup\limits_{t_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})|)\Big|\lesssim\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}\|\mu_{1}\|_{*1}.

Then

(\mathcal{S}_{5},\mathcal{S}_{i}):B_{\mu_{1}}\times B_{\xi}\rightarrow B_{\mu_{1}}\times B_{\xi}.

Consequently, by the contraction mapping theorem, we find a unique solution
(\mu_{1},\xi)
in
B_{\mu_{1}}\times B_{\xi}
.

4.2.
Hölder continuity of
\mu_{1t}
and estimate for
\mu\mathcal{E}_{\nu}[\mu_{1}]

In order to estimate the left error

\mathcal{E}_{\nu}[\mu_{1}]=\int_{t-t^{1-\nu}}^{t-\mu_{0}^{2}(t)}\frac{\mu_{1t}(s)-\mu_{1t}(t)}{t-s}ds,

we need Hölder estimate of
\mu_{1t}
, which satisfies

\mu_{1t}=\Pi_{5}[\mu_{1},\xi](t)+\beta_{\nu}(t)e^{-\int^{t}\beta_{\nu}(u)du}\int_{t}^{\infty}e^{\int^{s}\beta_{\nu}(u)du}\Pi_{5}[\mu_{1},\xi](s)ds.

Assume
\frac{3t}{4}\leq t_{2}<t_{1}\leq t
,
\frac{8}{9}<A<1
.
A
will be chosen to be close to
1
later depending on
\nu
and independent of
t_{0}
. We revisit (
4.3
) term by term.

Notice that
\psi
only has Hölder continuity in
t
variable, which restricts the regularity for
\mu_{1t}
. Using Proposition
3.1
with
\lambda^{2}(t)=t^{\frac{1}{2}}
, one has

\displaystyle|\psi(\mu(t_{1})y+\xi(t_{1}),t_{1})-\psi(\mu(t_{2})y+\xi(t_{2}),t_{2})|

\displaystyle\leq\

\displaystyle|\psi(\mu(t_{1})y+\xi(t_{1}),t_{1})-\psi(\mu(t_{2})y+\xi(t_{2}),t_{1})|+|\psi(\mu(t_{2})y+\xi(t_{2}),t_{1})-\psi(\mu(t_{2})y+\xi(t_{2}),t_{2})|

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle\big[\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}(t)|y|+(\ln t)^{2}(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}(t)\big]\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}(t)|t_{1}-t_{2}|

\displaystyle+C(\alpha)\Bigg\{\lambda^{-2\alpha}(t)\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}(t)

\displaystyle+\lambda^{2-2\alpha}(t)[(\mu_{0}R)^{-2}(t)\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}(t)+(\ln t)^{3}(t(\ln t)^{2})^{10\delta-2\kappa}]\Bigg\}|t_{1}-t_{2}|^{\alpha}

which implies

\displaystyle(\ln t_{1})^{-1}\Big|\int_{B_{2R_{0}}}w^{2}(y)Z_{5}(y)(\psi(\mu(t_{1})y+\xi(t_{1}),t_{1})-\psi(\mu(t_{2})y+\xi(t_{2}),t_{2}))dy\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle[\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}(t)]^{2}|t_{1}-t_{2}|+C(\alpha)\Bigg\{\lambda^{-2\alpha}(t)(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}(t)

\displaystyle+\lambda^{2-2\alpha}(t)[(\mu_{0}R)^{-2}(t)(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}(t)+(\ln t)^{2}(t(\ln t)^{2})^{10\delta-2\kappa}]\Bigg\}|t_{1}-t_{2}|^{\alpha}.

Similarly,
(t(\ln t)^{2})^{-\delta\epsilon_{0}}O(\sup\limits_{y\in B_{4R(t)}}\langle y\rangle^{-1}|\psi(\mu y+\xi,t)|)
provides the same Hölder estimate as above.

Reviewing the analysis details in solving (
4.4
), one has

|\Pi_{5}[\mu_{1},\xi]|\lesssim\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}\chi(t).

Then

\displaystyle\Big|(\beta_{\nu}(t)e^{-\int^{t}\beta_{\nu}(u)du}\int_{t}^{\infty}e^{\int^{s}\beta_{\nu}(u)du}\Pi_{5}[\mu_{1},\xi](s)ds)^{\prime}\Big|=\Bigg|\beta_{\nu}^{\prime}(t)e^{-\int^{t}\beta_{\nu}(u)du}\int_{t}^{\infty}e^{\int^{s}\beta_{\nu}(u)du}\Pi_{5}[\mu_{1},\xi](s)ds

\displaystyle-\beta_{\nu}^{2}(t)e^{-\int^{t}\beta_{\nu}(u)du}\int_{t}^{\infty}e^{\int^{s}\beta_{\nu}(u)du}\Pi_{5}[\mu_{1},\xi](s)ds-\beta_{\nu}(t)\Pi_{5}[\mu_{1},\xi]\Bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-1}e^{-\int^{t}\beta_{\nu}(u)du}\int_{t}^{\infty}e^{\int^{s}\beta_{\nu}(u)du}\ln s(s(\ln s)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}(s)ds

\displaystyle+(t\ln t)^{-2}e^{-\int^{t}\beta_{\nu}(u)du}\int_{t}^{\infty}e^{\int^{s}\beta_{\nu}(u)du}\ln s(s(\ln s)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}(s)ds+(t\ln t)^{-1}\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(t\ln t)^{-1}\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}

where in the last inequality, we have used
e^{-\int^{t}\beta_{\nu}(u)du}\int_{t}^{\infty}e^{\int^{s}\beta_{\nu}(u)du}\ln s(s(\ln s)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}(s)ds\lesssim t\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}
when
t_{0}
is sufficiently large. Also

\left|[((1-\nu)\ln t+2\ln\ln t)^{-1}\chi(t)]^{\prime}\big[((1-\nu)\ln t+2\ln\ln t)\Pi_{5}[\mu_{1},\xi]\big]\right|\lesssim t^{-1}\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}1_{\{t\geq\frac{3t_{0}}{4}\}},

\displaystyle\Big|((1-\nu)\ln t+2\ln\ln t)^{-1}[-O((t\ln t)^{-1}\sup\limits_{\tau_{1}\in[t/2,t]}|\mu_{1}(\tau_{1})|)-O(\tilde{g}[\bar{\mu}_{0},\mu_{1}])-(t(\ln t)^{2})^{-\delta\epsilon_{0}}O(t^{a_{1}\gamma-2}(\ln t)^{-1})]^{\prime}\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\ln t)^{-1}(t^{-1}\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}+t^{-1}(t(\ln t)^{2})^{-\delta\epsilon_{0}}O(t^{a_{1}\gamma-2}(\ln t)^{-1}))\lesssim(t\ln t)^{-1}\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}.

In order to get the estimate

\displaystyle((1-\nu)\ln t+2\ln\ln t)^{-1}\Big|O(\sup\limits_{\tau_{1}\in[t_{1}/2,t_{1}]}|\mu_{1t}(\tau_{1})|)-O(\sup\limits_{\tau_{1}\in[t_{2}/2,t_{2}]}|\mu_{1t}(\tau_{1})|)\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\ln t)^{-1}(C(A)t^{-1}\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}|t_{1}-t_{2}|+[\mu_{1t}]_{C^{\alpha}(\frac{3At}{4},t)}|t_{1}-t_{2}|^{\alpha}),

rigorously speaking, we need to estimate all the terms that appeared in the proof of Lemma
2.1
and Lemma
2.2
except the leading term. For simplicity, we take
\tilde{\varphi}_{1b}[\mu+\mu_{1}]-\tilde{\varphi}_{1b}[\mu]
as an example to illustrate the key idea. We decompose
\tilde{\varphi}_{1b}[\mu+\mu_{1}]-\tilde{\varphi}_{1b}[\mu]
into two parts to estimate.

\displaystyle(\tilde{\varphi}_{1b}[\mu+\mu_{1}]-\tilde{\varphi}_{1b}[\mu])(\bar{x},t)={\mathcal{T}}_{4}^{out}[-\mu_{1t}\hat{\varphi}_{1}+(E-\tilde{E})[\mu+\mu_{1}]-(E-\tilde{E})[\mu]](\bar{x},t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\int_{t_{0}}^{At}+\int_{At}^{t}\right)\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}(4\pi(t-s))^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}-z|^{2}}{4(t-s)}}(-\mu_{1t}\hat{\varphi}_{1}+(E-\tilde{E})[\mu+\mu_{1}]-(E-\tilde{E})[\mu])(z,s)dzds.

Here
\bar{x}
is regarded to be independent of
t
. Then

\displaystyle\left|{\partial}_{t}(\int_{t_{0}}^{At}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}(4\pi(t-s))^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}-z|^{2}}{4(t-s)}}(-\mu_{1t}\hat{\varphi}_{1}+(E-\tilde{E})[\mu+\mu_{1}]-(E-\tilde{E})[\mu])(z,s)dzds)\right|

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\Bigg|A\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}(4\pi(t-At))^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}-z|^{2}}{4(t-At)}}(-\mu_{1t}\hat{\varphi}_{1}+(E-\tilde{E})[\mu+\mu_{1}]-(E-\tilde{E})[\mu])(z,At)dz

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{0}}^{At}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}{\partial}_{t}((4\pi(t-s))^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}-z|^{2}}{4(t-s)}})(-\mu_{1t}\hat{\varphi}_{1}+(E-\tilde{E})[\mu+\mu_{1}]-(E-\tilde{E})[\mu])(z,s)dzds\Bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle C(A)t^{-2}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}-z|^{2}}{4(t-At)}}\Bigg(|\mu_{1t}(At)|t^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{|z|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+|\mu_{1t}(At)||z|^{-2}e^{-\frac{|z|^{2}}{4t}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|z|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}

\displaystyle+|\mu_{1}(At)|\mu^{2}(At)t^{-3}\mathbf{1}_{\{2^{-1}\sqrt{t}\leq|z|\leq 4\sqrt{t}\}}\Bigg)dz

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{0}}^{At}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}(t-s)^{-3}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}-z|^{2}}{8(t-s)}}\left|-\mu_{1t}\hat{\varphi}_{1}+(E-\tilde{E})[\mu+\mu_{1}]-(E-\tilde{E})[\mu])(z,s)\right|dzds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle C(A)t^{-2}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}e^{-\frac{|z|^{2}}{4(t-At)}}\Bigg(|\mu_{1t}(At)|t^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{|z|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+|\mu_{1t}(At)||z|^{-2}e^{-\frac{|z|^{2}}{4t}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|z|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}

\displaystyle+|\mu_{1}(At)|\mu^{2}(At)t^{-3}\mathbf{1}_{\{|z|\leq 4\sqrt{t}\}}\Bigg)dz

\displaystyle+C(A)t^{-1}\int_{t_{0}}^{At}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}(t-s)^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}-z|^{2}}{8(t-s)}}\bigg|(-\mu_{1t}\hat{\varphi}_{1}+(E-\tilde{E})[\mu+\mu_{1}]-(E-\tilde{E})[\mu])(z,s)\bigg|dzds

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle C(A)\Bigg[t^{-1}|\mu_{1t}(At)|+t^{-3}|\mu_{1}(At)|\mu^{2}(At)

\displaystyle+t^{-1}\int_{t_{0}}^{At}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}(t-s)^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}-z|^{2}}{8(t-s)}}\bigg|(-\mu_{1t}\hat{\varphi}_{1}+(E-\tilde{E})[\mu+\mu_{1}]-(E-\tilde{E})[\mu])(z,s)\bigg|dzds\Bigg]

\displaystyle\lesssim\

\displaystyle C(A)t^{-1}\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}

where the last inequality follows from the same calculations as in (
2.11
).

For the other part, we have

\displaystyle\int_{At}^{t}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}(4\pi(t-s))^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}-z|^{2}}{4(t-s)}}\bigg(-\mu_{1t}\hat{\varphi}_{1}+(E-\tilde{E})[\mu+\mu_{1}]-(E-\tilde{E})[\mu]\bigg)(z,s)dzds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{A}^{1}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}t(t-ta)^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}-z|^{2}}{4(t-ta)}}\bigg(-\mu_{1t}\hat{\varphi}_{1}+(E-\tilde{E})[\mu+\mu_{1}]-(E-\tilde{E})[\mu]\bigg)(z,ta)dzda.

The terms independent of
\mu_{1t}
are
C^{1}
in time variable
t
.
We only need to focus on the terms including
\mu_{1t}
. By similar calculations in (
2.11
), we have

\displaystyle\left|\int_{A}^{1}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}{\partial}_{t}(t(t-ta)^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}-z|^{2}}{4(t-ta)}})(\mu_{1t}\hat{\varphi}_{1})(z,ta)dzda\right|\lesssim\int_{A}^{1}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}(t-ta)^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}-z|^{2}}{8(t-ta)}})|\mu_{1t}\hat{\varphi}_{1}(z,ta)|dzda

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-1}\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a},

\displaystyle\left|\int_{A}^{1}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}t(t-ta)^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}-z|^{2}}{4(t-ta)}}(\mu_{1t}(t_{1}a)-\mu_{1t}(t_{2}a))\hat{\varphi}_{1}(z,ta)dzda\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{A}^{1}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}t(t-ta)^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}-z|^{2}}{4(t-ta)}}[\mu_{1t}]_{C^{\alpha}(\frac{3At}{4},t)}|t_{1}-t_{2}|^{\alpha}a^{\alpha}|\hat{\varphi}_{1}(z,ta)|dzda\lesssim[\mu_{1t}]_{C^{\alpha}(\frac{3At}{4},t)}|t_{1}-t_{2}|^{\alpha}.

Next, for
\int_{t/2}^{t-t^{1-\nu}}\frac{\mu_{1t}(s)}{t-s}ds=(\int_{t/2}^{At}+\int_{At}^{t-t^{1-\nu}})\frac{\mu_{1t}(s)}{t-s}ds
, we have

\left|(\int_{t/2}^{At}\frac{\mu_{1t}(s)}{t-s}ds)^{\prime}\right|=\left|\frac{A\mu_{1t}(At)}{t-At}-\frac{\mu_{1t}(\frac{t}{2})}{t}-\int_{t/2}^{At}\frac{\mu_{1t}(s)}{(t-s)^{2}}ds\right|\lesssim C(A)t^{-1}\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a},

and

\displaystyle\left|\int_{At_{1}}^{t_{1}-t_{1}^{1-\nu}}\frac{\mu_{1t}(s)}{t_{1}-s}ds-\int_{At_{2}}^{t_{2}-t_{2}^{1-\nu}}\frac{\mu_{1t}(s)}{t_{2}-s}ds\right|=\left|\int_{A}^{1-t_{1}^{-\nu}}\frac{\mu_{1t}(t_{1}z)}{1-z}dz-\int_{A}^{1-t_{2}^{-\nu}}\frac{\mu_{1t}(t_{2}z)}{1-z}dz\right|

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\left|\int_{A}^{1-t_{1}^{-\nu}}\frac{\mu_{1t}(t_{1}z)-\mu_{1t}(t_{2}z)}{1-z}dz+\int_{1-t_{2}^{-\nu}}^{1-t_{1}^{-\nu}}\frac{\mu_{1t}(t_{2}z)}{1-z}dz\right|

\displaystyle\leq\

\displaystyle|t_{1}-t_{2}|^{\alpha}[\mu_{1t}]_{C^{\alpha}(\frac{3At}{4},t)}\int_{A}^{1-t_{1}^{-\nu}}\frac{z^{\alpha}}{1-z}dz+C\int_{1-t_{2}^{-\nu}}^{1-t_{1}^{-\nu}}\frac{(t_{2}z)^{5\delta-\kappa-a\gamma}(\ln(t_{2}z))^{1+2(5\delta-\kappa)}}{1-z}dz

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle|t_{1}-t_{2}|^{\alpha}[\mu_{1t}]_{C^{\alpha}(\frac{3At}{4},t)}\nu\ln t_{1}(1+O(|\ln(1-A)|(\ln t_{1})^{-1}))

\displaystyle+C(1+O((\ln t_{2})^{-1}))t_{2}^{5\delta-\kappa-a\gamma}(\ln t_{2})^{1+2(5\delta-\kappa)}\int_{1-t_{2}^{-\nu}}^{1-t_{1}^{-\nu}}\frac{z^{5\delta-\kappa-a\gamma}}{1-z}dz

\displaystyle\leq\

\displaystyle|t_{1}-t_{2}|^{\alpha}[\mu_{1t}]_{C^{\alpha}(\frac{3At}{4},t)}\nu\ln t_{1}(1+O(|\ln(1-A)|(\ln t_{1})^{-1}))

\displaystyle+C(1+O((\ln t_{2})^{-1}))t_{2}^{5\delta-\kappa-a\gamma}(\ln t_{2})^{1+2(5\delta-\kappa)}(1+O(t^{-1}))\nu|\ln t_{1}-\ln t_{2}|

\displaystyle\leq\

\displaystyle|t_{1}-t_{2}|^{\alpha}[\mu_{1t}]_{C^{\alpha}(\frac{3At}{4},t)}\nu\ln t_{1}(1+O(|\ln(1-A)|(\ln t_{1})^{-1}))

\displaystyle+C(1+O((\ln t_{2})^{-1}))t_{2}^{5\delta-\kappa-a\gamma}(\ln t_{2})^{1+2(5\delta-\kappa)}(1+O(t^{-1}))\nu t_{2}^{-1}|t_{1}-t_{2}|.

Combining the estimates above, one gets that

\displaystyle|\mu_{1t}(t_{1})-\mu_{1t}(t_{2})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(A)[(t\ln t)^{-1}\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}(t)+t^{-1}\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}(t)1_{\{t\geq\frac{3t_{0}}{4}\}}]|t_{1}-t_{2}|

\displaystyle+C(\alpha)\Big\{\lambda^{-2\alpha}(t)(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}(t)

\displaystyle+\lambda^{2-2\alpha}(t)[(\mu_{0}R)^{-2}(t)(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}(t)+(\ln t)^{2}(t(\ln t)^{2})^{10\delta-2\kappa}]\Big\}|t_{1}-t_{2}|^{\alpha}

\displaystyle+|t_{1}-t_{2}|^{\alpha}[\mu_{1t}]_{C^{\alpha}(\frac{3At}{4},t)}[\nu(1-\nu)^{-1}(1+O(|\ln(1-A)|(\ln t)^{-1})+O((\ln t)^{-1}))]

where we have used
\frac{3t}{4}\leq t_{2}<t_{1}\leq t
. Thus one has

[\mu_{1t}]_{C^{\alpha}(\frac{3t}{4},t)}\leq C(A,\alpha)\rho(t)+[\mu_{1t}]_{C^{\alpha}(\frac{3At}{4},t)}[\nu(1-\nu)^{-1}(1+O(|\ln(1-A)|(\ln t_{0})^{-1})+O((\ln t)^{-1})])\mathbf{1}_{\{t\geq\frac{3t_{0}}{4}\}}

where

\rho(t)=t^{-\alpha}\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}+t^{1-\alpha}[(\mu_{0}R)^{-2}(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}+(\ln t)^{2}(t(\ln t)^{2})^{10\delta-2\kappa}].

Thus

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{\frac{t_{0}}{2}\leq t\leq T}\rho^{-1}(t)[\mu_{1t}]_{C^{\alpha}(\frac{3t}{4},t)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(A,\alpha)+[\nu(1-\nu)^{-1}(1+O(|\ln(1-A)|(\ln t_{0})^{-1}))+O((\ln t_{0})^{-1})]\sup\limits_{\frac{t_{0}}{2}\leq t\leq T}\rho^{-1}(t)[\mu_{1t}]_{C^{\alpha}(\frac{3At}{4},t)}\mathbf{1}_{\{t\geq\frac{3t_{0}}{4}\}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle C(A,\alpha)+[\nu(1-\nu)^{-1}(1+O(|\ln(1-A)|(\ln t_{0})^{-1}))+O((\ln t_{0})^{-1})]\sup\limits_{\frac{3t_{0}}{4}\leq t\leq T}\rho^{-1}(t)[\mu_{1t}]_{C^{\alpha}(\frac{3At}{4},t)}.

Notice

\displaystyle\rho^{-1}(t)[\mu_{1t}]_{C^{\alpha}(\frac{3At}{4},t)}=\rho^{-1}(t)\sup\limits_{s_{1},s_{2}\in(\frac{3At}{4},t)}\frac{|\mu_{1t}(s_{1})-\mu_{1t}(s_{2})|}{|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\max\Bigg\{\rho^{-1}(t)\sup\limits_{s_{1},s_{2}\in(\frac{3t}{4},t)}\frac{|\mu_{1t}(s_{1})-\mu_{1t}(s_{2})|}{|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{\alpha}},\rho^{-1}(t)\sup\limits_{s_{1},s_{2}\in(\frac{3At}{4},At)}\frac{|\mu_{1t}(s_{1})-\mu_{1t}(s_{2})|}{|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{\alpha}},

\displaystyle\rho^{-1}(t)\sup\limits_{s_{2}\in(\frac{3At}{4},\frac{3t}{4}),s_{1}\in(At,t)}\frac{|\mu_{1t}(s_{1})-\mu_{1t}(s_{2})|}{|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{\alpha}}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\

\displaystyle\max\Bigg\{\rho^{-1}(t)\sup\limits_{s_{1},s_{2}\in(\frac{3t}{4},t)}\frac{|\mu_{1t}(s_{1})-\mu_{1t}(s_{2})|}{|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{\alpha}},\rho^{-1}(t)\rho(At)\rho^{-1}(At)\sup\limits_{s_{1},s_{2}\in(\frac{3At}{4},At)}\frac{|\mu_{1t}(s_{1})-\mu_{1t}(s_{2})|}{|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{\alpha}}\Bigg\}+C,

then one has

\sup\limits_{\frac{3t_{0}}{4}\leq t\leq T}\rho^{-1}(t)[\mu_{1t}]_{C^{\alpha}(\frac{3At}{4},t)}\leq A^{C(\alpha,\delta,\kappa,a)}(1+O((\ln t_{0})^{-1}))\sup\limits_{\frac{t_{0}}{2}\leq t\leq T}\rho^{-1}(t)[\mu_{1t}]_{C^{\alpha}(\frac{3t}{4},t)}+C(A,\alpha).

Thus, when
\nu<\frac{1}{2}
, taking
A
close to
1
sufficiently, which depends on
\nu
, and then making
t_{0}
large enough, one has
\sup\limits_{\frac{t_{0}}{2}\leq t\leq T}\rho^{-1}(t)[\mu_{1t}]_{C^{\alpha}(\frac{3t}{4},t)}\leq C(\nu,\alpha)
. Making
T\rightarrow\infty
, one finally gets

(4.7)

\sup\limits_{t\geq\frac{t_{0}}{2}}\rho^{-1}(t)[\mu_{1t}]_{C^{\alpha}(\frac{3t}{4},t)}\leq C(\nu,\alpha).

Finally, we estimate
\mu\mathcal{E}_{\nu}[\mu_{1}]
as follows

(4.8)

\displaystyle|\mu\mathcal{E}_{\nu}[\mu_{1}]|\lesssim C(\nu,\alpha)\{(\ln t)^{-1}t^{-\nu\alpha}\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}

\displaystyle+(\ln t)^{-1}t^{1-\nu\alpha}(\mu_{0}R)^{-2}(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}+(\ln t)^{-1}t^{1-\nu\alpha}(\ln t)^{2}(t(\ln t)^{2})^{10\delta-2\kappa}\}.

Although
C_{\nu,\alpha}
goes to
\infty
as
\nu\rightarrow\frac{1}{2}
and
\alpha\rightarrow 1
, the smallness is given by
t_{0}^{-\epsilon}
where
\epsilon>0
when solving (
3.7
). Once
\nu
and
\alpha
are fixed, we take
t_{0}
large enough.

5.
Solving the inner problem

Recalling (
3.8
), for any fixed
\phi\in B_{i}
with

(5.1)

B_{i}=\{\phi\ :\ \|\phi\|_{i,\kappa-5\delta,a}\leq 2C_{i}\}

where
C_{i}>1
is a constant,
we have found
\psi[\phi]\in B_{o}
,
\mu_{1}[\phi]\in B_{\mu_{1}}
and
\xi[\phi]\in B_{\xi}
. We abbreviate
\mathcal{H}[\phi]=\mathcal{H}[\psi[\phi],\mu_{1}[\phi],\xi[\phi]]
.
By (
3.4
), (
3.5
) and (
4.5
), we obtain
|\mathcal{H}[\phi](y,t(\tau))|\lesssim\tau^{5\delta-\kappa}(\ln\tau)^{4}R^{-a}(t(\tau))\langle y\rangle^{-2-a_{1}}
.
The orthogonal equations of
\mu_{1}
and
\xi
have been solved in Section
4.1
, then by Proposition
7.1
, one finds a solution for (
3.6
) satisfying

\langle y\rangle|\nabla\phi_{1}(y,\tau)|+|\phi_{1}(y,\tau)|\lesssim\tau^{5\delta-\kappa}(\ln\tau)^{4}R^{-a}(t(\tau))R_{0}^{5}\langle y\rangle^{-a_{1}}\lesssim\tau_{0}^{-\epsilon}\tau^{5\delta-\kappa}\langle y\rangle^{-a}

with
\epsilon>0
sufficiently small provided

(5.2)

\gamma\min\{a,a_{1}\}>5\delta.

Combining (
4.8
) and Lemma
7.5
, one can find a solution for (
3.7
) with the estimate

\langle y\rangle|\nabla\phi_{2}(y,\tau)|+|\phi_{2}(y,\tau)|\lesssim\tau_{0}^{-\epsilon}\tau^{5\delta-\kappa}\langle y\rangle^{-a}

if

(5.3)

-\nu\alpha+(2-a)\gamma<0,\ 1-\nu\alpha-a\gamma<0,\ 1-\nu\alpha+5\delta-\kappa+2\gamma<0,\ 0<a<2.

Combining (
3.5
), (
3.12
), (
4.6
), (
5.2
), (
5.3
) and the assumption about parameters in Proposition
7.1
and Lemma
7.5
, one needs to choose parameters such that all the inequalities below hold

(5.4)

\displaystyle 5\delta-\kappa-a\gamma>-2,\ 5\delta-\kappa<-1,\ 0<a<2,\ 0<\gamma<\frac{1}{2},\ 0<\alpha<1,\ 0<\nu<\frac{1}{2},

\displaystyle a_{1}\gamma-2<5\delta-\kappa-a\gamma,\ 0<a_{1}\leq 1,\ \gamma\min\{a,a_{1}\}>5\delta,\ 6\delta<1,

\displaystyle-\nu\alpha+(2-a)\gamma<0,\ 1-\nu\alpha-a\gamma<0,\ 1-\nu\alpha+5\delta-\kappa+2\gamma<0.

There exists solution given by

(5.5)

\displaystyle 1<\kappa\leq\frac{5}{4},\ \frac{2-2\alpha\nu}{-1+\kappa+\nu}<a<2,\ \frac{1-\alpha\nu}{a}<\gamma<\frac{-1+\kappa+\nu}{2},\

\displaystyle\frac{2-\kappa}{2}<\alpha\nu<\frac{1}{2},\ 0<\alpha<1,\ 0<\nu<\frac{1}{2},\ 6\delta<1,

\displaystyle 0<5\delta<\kappa-1,\ a_{1}\gamma<5\delta+2-\kappa-a\gamma,\ 5\delta<\gamma\min\{a,a_{1}\},\ 0<a_{1}\leq 1.

Indeed, one may take for example

\kappa=\frac{9}{8},\nu=\frac{49}{100},\alpha=\frac{375}{392},a=\frac{36}{19},\gamma=\frac{9}{32},5\delta=\frac{1}{64},a_{1}=\frac{1}{9}
.

Thanks to (
5.4
), the desired
\phi_{1}
,
\phi_{2}
can really be found and then
\phi_{1}+\phi_{2}\in B_{i}
when
\tau_{0}
is large enough. The compactness is a consequence of parabolic estimates, so we can find a solution for the inner problem (
3.2
). Making more efforts to calculate the Lipschitz continuity of
\mathcal{H}[\phi]
about
\phi
, one can prove the existence for the inner problem (
3.2
) by the contraction mapping theorem.

Collecting the estimates in Proposition
3.1
, Corollary
2.3
, (
2.28
) and (
5.1
), one gets

\displaystyle\left|\varphi[\mu]+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}(\frac{x-\xi}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t)\eta(\frac{4(x-\xi)}{\sqrt{t}})+\psi+\eta_{R}\mu^{-1}\phi(\frac{x-\xi}{\mu},t)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(t\ln t)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+O(t^{2}(\ln t)^{-1}|\bar{x}|^{-6})\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+(\ln t)t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}\langle\bar{y}\rangle^{-2}\ln(2+|\bar{y}|)\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}

\displaystyle+\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}\left(\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+t|x|^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}\right)+(t(\ln t)^{2})^{-\kappa+5\delta}\langle y\rangle^{-a}\mathbf{1}_{\{|y|\leq 4R\}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(t\ln t)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+O((\ln t)^{-1}|\bar{x}|^{-2})\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}\lesssim(\ln t)^{-1}\min\{t^{-1},|x|^{-2}\}.

Positivity of the solution
u
.

We will demonstrate that the initial value
u(x,t_{0})
that we take in the construction is positive. For simplicity, we abuse the symbols
\mu=\mu(t_{0}),\bar{\mu}_{0}=\bar{\mu}_{0}(t_{0})
in the remainder of this section. Indeed, recalling (
2.6
), (
2.28
) and (
5.1
), we have

\displaystyle u(x,t_{0})=\mu^{-1}w\left(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu}\right)\eta\left(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t_{0}}}\right)+\tilde{\varphi}_{1}(\bar{x},t_{0})+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}\left(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t_{0}\right)\eta\left(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t_{0}}}\right)+\eta_{R}\mu^{-1}\phi\left(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu},t_{0}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2^{\frac{3}{2}}\mu|\bar{x}|^{-2}\Bigg\{\left[e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{4t_{0}}}-\left(1+\left(\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\mu}\right)^{2}\right)^{-1}+2^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mu^{-1}|\bar{x}|^{2}\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}\left(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t_{0}\right)\eta\left(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t_{0}}}\right)\right]\eta\left(\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\sqrt{t_{0}}}\right)

\displaystyle+e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{4t_{0}}}\left(1-\eta\left(\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\sqrt{t_{0}}}\right)\right)\Bigg\}+\eta_{R}\mu^{-1}\phi\left(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu},t_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 2^{\frac{3}{2}}\mu|\bar{x}|^{-2}\left[1-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{4t_{0}}-\left(1+\left(\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\mu}\right)^{2}\right)^{-1}-C|\bar{x}|^{2}(t_{0}\ln t_{0})^{-1}\ln\ln t_{0}\eta\left(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t_{0}}}\right)\right]\eta\left(\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\sqrt{t_{0}}}\right)+\eta_{R}\mu^{-1}\phi\left(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu},t_{0}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2^{\frac{3}{2}}\mu|\bar{x}|^{-2}|\bar{x}|^{2}\left[(\mu^{2}+|\bar{x}|^{2})^{-1}-(4t_{0})^{-1}-C(t_{0}\ln t_{0})^{-1}\ln\ln t_{0}\eta\left(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t_{0}}}\right)\right]\eta\left(\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\sqrt{t_{0}}}\right)+\eta_{R}\mu^{-1}\phi\left(\frac{\bar{x}}{\mu},t_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 2^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mu^{-1}(1+|y|^{2})^{-1}\eta\left(\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\sqrt{t_{0}}}\right)-C_{1}\mu^{-1}(t_{0}(\ln t_{0})^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}\langle y\rangle^{-a}\eta_{R}>0

where we have used
\eta(s)=0
for
s\geq\frac{3}{2}
to make
[\frac{5}{8}(\mu^{2}+|\bar{x}|^{2})^{-1}-(4t_{0})^{-1}-C(t_{0}\ln t_{0})^{-1}\ln\ln t_{0}\eta(\frac{4\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t_{0}}})]\eta(\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\sqrt{t_{0}}})\geq 0
when
t_{0}
is large, and
5\delta-\kappa+\gamma(2-a)<0
is used in the last inequality. Therefore, the solution
u(x,t)
is positive by maximum principle.

6.
Stability of blow-up: proof of Theorem
1.2

In this section, we will analyze the stability of the blow-up solution constructed in Theorem
1.1
.

Proof of Theorem
1.2
.

Consider any perturbation
g_{0}(x)
satisfying
|g_{0}(x)|\lesssim t_{0}^{-\frac{\min{\{\ell,4\}}}{2}}\langle x\rangle^{-\ell}
,
\ell>2
. Set

\psi_{0}(x,t)=(4\pi(t-t_{0}))^{-2}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}e^{-\frac{|x-z|^{2}}{4(t-t_{0})}}g_{0}(z)dz

which satisfies

{\partial}_{t}\psi_{0}=\Delta\psi_{0}
in
{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}\times(t_{0},\infty)
,
\psi(x,t_{0})=g_{0}(x)
in
{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}
.
Without loss of generality, we only consider the case
2<\ell<4
. By Lemma
A.3
, one has

|\psi_{0}(x,t)|\lesssim t_{0}^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}\left(\langle t-t_{0}\rangle^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|\leq\langle t-t_{0}\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+|x|^{-\ell}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|>\langle t-t_{0}\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}\right)\lesssim t^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+|x|^{-\ell}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}.

We modify the proof of Proposition
3.1
slightly in order to match the perturbation
g_{0}
. Indeed, we split
\psi=\bar{\psi}+\psi_{0}
and consider

{\partial}_{t}\bar{\psi}(x,t)=\Delta\bar{\psi}(x,t)+\mathcal{G}[\bar{\psi}+\psi_{0},\phi,\mu_{1},\xi]\mbox{ \ in \ }{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}\times(t_{0},\infty),\quad\bar{\psi}(x,t_{0})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}.

When
\ell>2(\kappa+a\gamma)
, by (
5.5
), one has
|\psi_{0}|\lesssim t_{0}^{-\epsilon}w_{o}
, and thus
\bar{\psi}
can be solved in
B_{o}
by the same method in Proposition
3.1
. Repeating the rest procedures in the construction of Theorem
1.1
,
(\mu_{1},\xi,\phi,e_{0})=(\mu_{1}[g_{0}],\xi[g_{0}],\phi[g_{0}],e[g_{0}])
can be solved in the same topology that we have used before, and the leading order of blowup rate
\bar{\mu}_{0}\sim(\ln t)^{-1}
remains the same. The perturbed initial value is then given by

\displaystyle\Bigg[(\bar{\mu}_{0}+\mu_{1}[g_{0}])^{-1}w\bigg(\frac{x-\xi[g_{0}]}{\bar{\mu}_{0}+\mu_{1}[g_{0}]}\bigg)\eta\bigg(\frac{x-\xi[g_{0}]}{\sqrt{t}}\bigg)+2^{\frac{3}{2}}(\bar{\mu}_{0}+\mu_{1}[g_{0}])|x-\xi[g_{0}]|^{-2}\bigg(e^{-\frac{|x-\xi[g_{0}]|^{2}}{4t}}-\eta(\frac{x-\xi[g_{0}]}{\sqrt{t}})\bigg)

\displaystyle+\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\Phi_{0}\bigg(\frac{x-\xi[g_{0}]}{\bar{\mu}_{0}},t\bigg)\eta\bigg(\frac{4(x-\xi[g_{0}])}{\sqrt{t}}\bigg)+\eta\bigg(\frac{x-\xi[g_{0}]}{\mu_{0}R}\bigg)e_{0}[g_{0}](\bar{\mu}_{0}+\mu_{1}[g_{0}])^{-1}Z_{0}\bigg(\frac{x-\xi[g_{0}]}{\bar{\mu}_{0}+\mu_{1}[g_{0}]}\bigg)\Bigg]\Bigg|_{t=t_{0}}+g_{0}.

From (
5.5
),
\kappa>1
and
a\gamma>1-\alpha\nu
. So all
\ell>3
is permitted for
\kappa
and
\alpha\nu
close to
1
and
\frac{1}{2}
, respectively.

In the radial setting, the translation parameter
\xi\equiv 0
automatically in (
1.2
). Then for
2<\ell\leq 3
, we put
3u_{1}^{2}\psi_{0}
into the right hand side in the equation (
2.19
). Since
|\psi_{0}(x,t)|\lesssim t^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}
,
\ell>2
, the extra term involving
3u_{1}^{2}\psi_{0}
will not influence the leading order
\mu_{0}
and will be absorbed into
\Phi_{0}
. But recalling the construction of
\bar{\mu}_{0}
in Section
2.3
,
\bar{\mu}_{0}
depends on
g_{0}
, namely,
\bar{\mu}_{0}=\bar{\mu}_{0}[g_{0}]
.

We omit the tedious calculations about the Lipschitz continuity with respect to
g_{0}
for
\psi,\phi,\mu_{1},\xi
here.
∎

Remark 6.0.1
.

•

In general nonradial case and
\ell>2
, since
\psi_{0}
is not radial about
\bar{x}=x-\xi
, the previous ODE solution about (
2.19
) is not allowed. Instead, we can expand (
2.19
) by modes similar to the manipulation in section
7
and solve the leading order of
\mu
and
\xi
. Since this involves more technicalities, given the length of this paper, we refrain from considering
such a generality here.

•

The borderline
\ell>2
is also provided in
[
15
]
.

•

The stability result can be expected for
|g_{0}(x)|\lesssim t_{0}^{-1}(\ln t_{0})^{-b_{1}}\langle x\rangle^{-2}(\ln(|x|+2))^{-b_{2}}
