for (
3.11
) by the contraction mapping theorem.

From now on, we also regard
D_{o}(\Lambda_{1})
as a constant depending on
\Lambda_{1}
. Reviewing the estimates above and utilizing
\mathbf{1}_{\{|x|\geq\frac{\mu_{0}R}{2}\}}
to transform the spatial decay to time decay, one has

|\mathcal{G}[\psi,\phi,\mu_{1},\xi]|\lesssim C(\Lambda_{1})[(\mu_{0}R)^{-2}\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}+(\ln t)^{3}(t(\ln t)^{2})^{10\delta-2\kappa}]

where
C(\Lambda_{1})
is a constant depending on
\Lambda_{1}
which changes from line to line.

By gradient estimate, we have

|\nabla\psi|\lesssim C(\Lambda_{1})\ln t(t(\ln t)^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}.

Next, we will use scaling argument to deduce the Hölder estimate of
\psi(x,t)
in time variable
t
. For
x_{1}\in{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}
,
t_{1}>4t_{0}
, set

\tilde{\psi}(z,s)=\psi(x_{1}+\lambda(t_{1})z,t_{1}+\lambda^{2}(t_{1})s)

where
0<\lambda(t_{1})\leq t_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}
.
Then

{\partial}_{s}\tilde{\psi}=\Delta_{z}\tilde{\psi}+\tilde{\mathcal{G}}(z,s)

where
\tilde{\mathcal{G}}(z,s)=\lambda^{2}(t_{1})\mathcal{G}[\psi,\phi,\mu_{1},\xi](x_{1}+\lambda(t_{1})z,t_{1}+\lambda^{2}(t_{1})s)
, and standard parabolic regularity theory implies

\|\tilde{\psi}\|_{C^{2\alpha,\alpha}(B(0,\frac{1}{16})\times(-\frac{1}{4},0))}\leq C(\alpha)\left(\|\tilde{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}(B(0,\frac{1}{4})\times(-\frac{1}{2},0))}+\|\tilde{\mathcal{G}}\|_{L^{\infty}(B(0,\frac{1}{4})\times(-\frac{1}{2},0))}\right)

where
\alpha
can be chosen as any constant in
(0,1)
and
C(\alpha)
is a constant depending on
\alpha
.
Moreover, one has

\displaystyle\|\tilde{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}(B(0,\frac{1}{4})\times(-\frac{1}{2},0))}\lesssim

\displaystyle C(\Lambda_{1})\ln t_{1}(t_{1}(\ln t_{1})^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}(t_{1}),

\displaystyle\|\tilde{\mathcal{G}}\|_{L^{\infty}(B(0,\frac{1}{4})\times(-\frac{1}{2},0))}\lesssim

\displaystyle C(\Lambda_{1})\lambda^{2}(t_{1})\left[(\mu_{0}R)^{-2}(t_{1})\ln t_{1}(t_{1}(\ln t_{1})^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}(t_{1})+(\ln t_{1})^{3}(t_{1}(\ln t_{1})^{2})^{10\delta-2\kappa}\right],

and

\displaystyle\|\tilde{\psi}\|_{C^{2\alpha,\alpha}(B(0,\frac{1}{16})\times(-\frac{1}{4},0))}\geq\sup\limits_{s_{1},s_{2}\in(-1/4,0)}\frac{|\psi(x_{1},t_{1}+\lambda(t_{1})^{2}s_{1})-\psi(x_{1},t_{1}+\lambda(t_{1})^{2}s_{2})|}{|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda^{2\alpha}(t_{1})\sup\limits_{s_{1},s_{2}\in(-1/4,0)}\frac{|\psi(x_{1},t_{1}+\lambda(t_{1})^{2}s_{1})-\psi(x_{1},t_{1}+\lambda(t_{1})^{2}s_{2})|}{|(t_{1}+\lambda(t_{1})^{2}s_{1})-(t_{1}+\lambda(t_{1})^{2}s_{2})|^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda^{2\alpha}(t_{1})\sup\limits_{s_{1},s_{2}\in(t_{1}-\frac{\lambda(t_{1})^{2}}{4},t_{1})}\frac{|\psi(x_{1},s_{1})-\psi(x_{1},s_{2})|}{|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{\alpha}}.

Thus

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{s_{1},s_{2}\in(t_{1}-\frac{\lambda(t_{1})^{2}}{4},t_{1})}\frac{|\psi(x_{1},s_{1})-\psi(x_{1},s_{2})|}{|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{\alpha}}\lesssim C(\Lambda_{1},\alpha)\{\lambda^{-2\alpha}(t_{1})\ln t_{1}(t_{1}(\ln t_{1})^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}(t_{1})

\displaystyle+\lambda^{2-2\alpha}(t_{1})[(\mu_{0}R)^{-2}(t_{1})\ln t_{1}(t_{1}(\ln t_{1})^{2})^{5\delta-\kappa}R^{-a}(t_{1})+(\ln t_{1})^{3}(t_{1}(\ln t_{1})^{2})^{10\delta-2\kappa}]\}.

∎

Appendix C
Estimates for
\nabla_{\bar{x}}\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}]
and
{\partial}_{t}\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}]

In this section, we will revisit the calculations in Section
2.2
and derive the following estimates

(C.1)

|{\partial}_{t}\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}]|\lesssim t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-1},\quad|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}]|\lesssim\begin{cases}(t\ln t)^{-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|\leq\mu_{0}\\
t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}|\bar{x}|^{-1}+t^{-\frac{3}{2}}(\ln t)^{-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\mu_{0}<|\bar{x}|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
t^{-\frac{3}{2}}(\ln t)^{-1}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}+t(\ln t)^{-2}|\bar{x}|^{-5}&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\end{cases}.

Proof.

Notice
\bar{\mu}_{0}\sim(\ln t)^{-1}
and
|\bar{\mu}_{0t}|\sim t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}
.
By (
2.7
), we have

|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\tilde{\varphi}_{1}[\bar{\mu}_{0}]|\lesssim|\bar{x}|t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+|\bar{x}|^{-1}(t\ln t)^{-1}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{4t}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}.

For
\nabla_{\bar{x}}\tilde{\varphi}_{1b}[\bar{\mu}_{0}]
, we abbreviate
\nabla_{\bar{x}}\tilde{\varphi}_{1b}[\bar{\mu}_{0}]
as
\nabla_{\bar{x}}\tilde{\varphi}_{1b}
. By (
2.8
), then

\nabla_{\bar{x}}\tilde{\varphi}_{1b}(\bar{x},t)={\mathcal{T}}_{4}^{out}[\nabla_{\bar{x}}(-\bar{\mu}_{0t}\hat{\varphi}_{1}+(E-\tilde{E})[\bar{\mu}_{0}])](\bar{x},t).

Notice by (
2.7
), we have

|\bar{\mu}_{0t}\nabla_{\bar{x}}\hat{\varphi}_{1}(\bar{x},t)|\lesssim|\bar{x}|t^{-3}(\ln t)^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+|\bar{x}|^{-1}t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{4t}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}},

|\nabla_{\bar{x}}(E-\tilde{E})[\bar{\mu}_{0}]|\lesssim t^{-\frac{7}{2}}(\ln t)^{-3}\mathbf{1}_{\{t^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}},

and

{\mathcal{T}}_{4}^{out}\left[t^{-\frac{7}{2}}(\ln t)^{-3}\mathbf{1}_{\{t^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+|\bar{x}|t^{-3}(\ln t)^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}\right]\lesssim{\mathcal{T}}_{4}^{out}\left[t^{-\frac{5}{2}}(\ln t)^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}\right]\lesssim t^{-\frac{3}{2}}(\ln t)^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}},

{\mathcal{T}}_{4}^{out}\left[|\bar{x}|^{-1}t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{4t}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}\right]\lesssim{\mathcal{T}}_{4}^{out}\left[(\ln t)^{-2}|\bar{x}|^{-5}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}\right]\lesssim\begin{cases}t^{-\frac{3}{2}}(\ln t)^{-2}&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
t(\ln t)^{-2}|\bar{x}|^{-5}&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\end{cases}.

Thus

|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\tilde{\varphi}_{1b}|\lesssim t^{-\frac{3}{2}}(\ln t)^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+t(\ln t)^{-2}|\bar{x}|^{-5}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}.

Next, let us consider
\nabla_{\bar{x}}\varphi_{2}[\bar{\mu}_{0}]
. Recall the definition of
\varphi_{2}
in Lemma
2.2
, then

\nabla_{\bar{x}}\varphi_{2}={\mathcal{T}}^{out}_{4}\left[\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}\bar{\mu}_{0t}\nabla_{\bar{x}}\left(Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\eta(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\right)\right].

Notice

\displaystyle\left|\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}\bar{\mu}_{0t}\nabla_{\bar{x}}(Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\eta(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}}))\right|=\left|\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}\bar{\mu}_{0t}(\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-1}\nabla Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\eta(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})+t^{-\frac{1}{2}}Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\nabla\eta(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}}))\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-1}\left[\ln t(1+|\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}}|)^{-3}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+t^{-\frac{1}{2}}(1+|\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}}|)^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{t^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}\right]\sim t^{-1}\ln t(1+|\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}}|)^{-3}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle t^{-1}\ln t\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq\mu_{0}\}}+t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}|\bar{x}|^{-3}\mathbf{1}_{\{\mu_{0}<|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}.

Therefore, we obtain

|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\varphi_{2}|\lesssim{\mathcal{T}}_{4}^{out}\left[t^{-1}\ln t\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq\mu_{0}\}}+t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}|\bar{x}|^{-3}\mathbf{1}_{\{\mu_{0}<|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}\right]\lesssim\begin{cases}(t\ln t)^{-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|\leq\mu_{0}\\
t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}|\bar{x}|^{-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\mu_{0}<|\bar{x}|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
t^{-\frac{3}{2}}(\ln t)^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{16t}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }|\bar{x}|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\end{cases}.

Since
\varphi=\tilde{\varphi}_{1}+\tilde{\varphi}_{1b}+\varphi_{2}
, one concludes the upper bound of
|\nabla_{\bar{x}}\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}]|
in (
C.1
).

The left part is devoted to estimating
{\partial}_{t}\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}]
. For
{\partial}_{t}\tilde{\varphi}_{1}[\bar{\mu}_{0}]
, by (
2.6
),

|{\partial}_{t}\tilde{\varphi}_{1}[\bar{\mu}_{0}]|=\left|2^{\frac{3}{2}}\bar{\mu}_{0t}|\bar{x}|^{-2}\left(e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{4t}}-\eta(\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\sqrt{t}})\right)+2^{\frac{3}{2}}\bar{\mu}_{0}|\bar{x}|^{-2}\left(\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{4t^{2}}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{4t}}+\frac{|\bar{x}|}{2t^{\frac{3}{2}}}\eta^{\prime}(\frac{|\bar{x}|}{\sqrt{t}})\right)\right|\lesssim t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-1}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{4t}}.

Next, we estimate
{\partial}_{t}\tilde{\varphi}_{1b}[\bar{\mu}_{0}]
.
For any integer
n\geq 1
and
f(x,t)\in C^{1}({{\mathbb{R}}}^{n}\times(t_{0},\infty))
,

(C.2)

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\partial}_{t}\left(\int_{t_{0}}^{t}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{n}}\left[4\pi(t-s)\right]^{-\frac{n}{2}}e^{-\frac{|x-y|^{2}}{4(t-s)}}f(y,s)dyds\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\partial}_{t}\left(\int_{t_{0}}^{\frac{t}{2}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{n}}\left[4\pi(t-s)\right]^{-\frac{n}{2}}e^{-\frac{|x-y|^{2}}{4(t-s)}}f(y,s)dyds+\int_{0}^{\frac{t}{2}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{n}}\left(4\pi a\right)^{-\frac{n}{2}}e^{-\frac{|x-y|^{2}}{4a}}f(y,t-a)dyda\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{n}}(2\pi t)^{-\frac{n}{2}}e^{-\frac{|x-y|^{2}}{2t}}f(y,\frac{t}{2})dy+\int_{t_{0}}^{\frac{t}{2}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{n}}{\partial}_{t}\left\{\left[4\pi(t-s)\right]^{-\frac{n}{2}}e^{-\frac{|x-y|^{2}}{4(t-s)}}\right\}f(y,s)dyds

\displaystyle+\int_{\frac{t}{2}}^{t}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{n}}\left[4\pi(t-s)\right]^{-\frac{n}{2}}e^{-\frac{|x-y|^{2}}{4(t-s)}}({\partial}_{t}f)(y,s)dyds.

As a consequence of (
2.8
) and (
C.2
), we have

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\partial}_{t}\tilde{\varphi}_{1b}=\frac{1}{2}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}(2\pi t)^{-2}e^{-\frac{|x-y|^{2}}{2t}}\left(-\bar{\mu}_{0t}\hat{\varphi}_{1}+(E-\tilde{E})[\bar{\mu}_{0}]\right)(y,\frac{t}{2})dy

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{0}}^{\frac{t}{2}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}{\partial}_{t}\left\{\left[4\pi(t-s)\right]^{-2}e^{-\frac{|x-y|^{2}}{4(t-s)}}\right\}\left(-\bar{\mu}_{0t}\hat{\varphi}_{1}+(E-\tilde{E})[\bar{\mu}_{0}]\right)(y,s)dyds

\displaystyle+\int_{\frac{t}{2}}^{t}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}\left[4\pi(t-s)\right]^{-2}e^{-\frac{|x-y|^{2}}{4(t-s)}}\left[{\partial}_{t}\left(-\bar{\mu}_{0t}\hat{\varphi}_{1}+(E-\tilde{E})[\bar{\mu}_{0}]\right)\right](y,s)dyds.

where by (
2.9
) and (
2.10
), it follows that

\displaystyle\left|\left(-\bar{\mu}_{0t}\hat{\varphi}_{1}+(E-\tilde{E})[\bar{\mu}_{0}]\right)(\bar{x},\frac{t}{2})\right|+\left|\left(-\bar{\mu}_{0t}\hat{\varphi}_{1}+(E-\tilde{E})[\bar{\mu}_{0}]\right)(\bar{x},t)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(t\ln t)^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}|\bar{x}|^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{4t}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+(t\ln t)^{-3}\mathbf{1}_{\{t^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}\lesssim(t\ln t)^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{4t}},

and

\displaystyle|{\partial}_{t}(\bar{\mu}_{0t}\hat{\varphi}_{1})|=|\bar{\mu}_{0tt}\hat{\varphi}_{1}+\bar{\mu}_{0t}{\partial}_{t}\hat{\varphi}_{1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|(t\ln t)^{-2}\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}+|\bar{x}|^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{4t}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|>2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}\right)+t^{-3}(\ln t)^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{4t}}\right|\sim t^{-3}(\ln t)^{-2}e^{-\frac{|\bar{x}|^{2}}{4t}},

and

|{\partial}_{t}(E-\tilde{E})[\bar{\mu}_{0}]|\lesssim t^{-4}(\ln t)^{-3}\mathbf{1}_{\{\sqrt{t}\leq|\bar{x}|\leq 2\sqrt{t}\}}.

Thus by Lemma
A.3
and same calculation for deducing (
2.12
), we have

|{\partial}_{t}\tilde{\varphi}_{1b}|\lesssim(t\ln t)^{-2}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}t^{-2}e^{-\frac{|x-y|^{2}}{2t}}dy+t^{-1}\int_{t_{0}}^{t}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}(t-s)^{-2}e^{-\frac{|x-y|^{2}}{8(t-s)}}(s\ln s)^{-2}e^{-\frac{|y|^{2}}{4s}}dyds\lesssim(t\ln t)^{-2}.

Finally, we consider
{\partial}_{t}\varphi_{2}[\bar{\mu}_{0}]
.
By (
C.2
) and the definition of
\varphi_{2}
in Lemma
2.2
, we have

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\partial}_{t}\varphi_{2}=\frac{1}{2}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}(2\pi t)^{-2}e^{-\frac{|x-y|^{2}}{2t}}\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}(\frac{t}{2})\bar{\mu}_{0t}(\frac{t}{2})Z_{5}(\frac{y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}(\frac{t}{2})})\eta(\frac{\sqrt{2}y}{\sqrt{t}})dy

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{0}}^{\frac{t}{2}}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}{\partial}_{t}\left\{\left[4\pi(t-s)\right]^{-2}e^{-\frac{|x-y|^{2}}{4(t-s)}}\right\}\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}(s)\bar{\mu}_{0t}(s)Z_{5}(\frac{y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}(s)})\eta(\frac{y}{\sqrt{s}})dyds

\displaystyle+\int_{\frac{t}{2}}^{t}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}\left[4\pi(t-s)\right]^{-2}e^{-\frac{|x-y|^{2}}{4(t-s)}}\left[{\partial}_{s}\left(\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}(s)\bar{\mu}_{0t}(s)Z_{5}(\frac{y}{\bar{\mu}_{0}(s)})\eta(\frac{y}{\sqrt{s}})\right)\right]dyds,

and by (
2.16
),

\displaystyle\left|\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}(\frac{t}{2})\bar{\mu}_{0t}(\frac{t}{2})Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}(\frac{t}{2})})\eta(\frac{\sqrt{2}\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\right|+\left|\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}(t)\bar{\mu}_{0t}(t)Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}(t)})\eta(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq(\ln t)^{-1}\}}+t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}|\bar{x}|^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{(\ln t)^{-1}<|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}},

and

\displaystyle\left|{\partial}_{t}(\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}\bar{\mu}_{0t}Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\eta(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}}))\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|{\partial}_{t}(\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}\bar{\mu}_{0t})Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\eta(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}\bar{\mu}_{0t}\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}}\cdot\nabla Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\frac{\bar{\mu}_{0t}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}}\eta(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})-\bar{\mu}_{0}^{-2}\bar{\mu}_{0t}Z_{5}(\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}})\frac{\bar{x}}{2t^{\frac{3}{2}}}\cdot\nabla\eta(\frac{\bar{x}}{\sqrt{t}})\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-2}\langle\frac{\bar{x}}{\bar{\mu}_{0}}\rangle^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{x}|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}.

Thus, by similar calculation for Lemma
A.3
and the upper bound of
\varphi_{2}
in Lemma
2.2
, we have

\displaystyle|{\partial}_{t}\varphi_{2}|\lesssim\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}t^{-2}e^{-\frac{|x-y|^{2}}{2t}}\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{|y|\leq(\ln t)^{-1}\}}+t^{-1}(\ln t)^{-2}|y|^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{(\ln t)^{-1}<|y|\leq 2t^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}\right)dy

\displaystyle+t^{-1}\int_{t_{0}}^{t}\int_{{{\mathbb{R}}}^{4}}(t-s)^{-2}e^{-\frac{|x-y|^{2}}{8(t-s)}}\left(s^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{|y|\leq(\ln s)^{-1}\}}+s^{-1}(\ln s)^{-2}|y|^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{(\ln s)^{-1}<|y|\leq 2s^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}\right)dyds\lesssim t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-1}.

Collecting above estimates, we obtain
|{\partial}_{t}\varphi[\bar{\mu}_{0}]|\lesssim t^{-2}(\ln t)^{-1}
.

∎
